### [1] [What Stops Recursive Self-Improvement in Robotics? Lessons from 123 Rounds of Agentic Skill Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31760)
*Jiaming Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究构建了一个机器人自我改进智能体系统，在123轮家居操作任务迭代中检验其能否像编码智能体改进软件那样改进机器人，结果发现智能体能自主发现并部署新能力，但改进无法累积——目标任务始终失败，瓶颈不在智能体本身，而在感知模块、技能链结构和评估框架三个方面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索机器人能否像当前编码智能体改进软件那样实现自我改进。作者希望检验：在没有人类编写机器人代码的情况下，智能体能否自主发现缺失能力、编写新技能或引入外部模型，并持续迭代提升机器人性能。

Method: 构建了一个智能体系统，工作流程为：观察机器人失败→判断缺失能力→编写新技能或寻找并安装外部模型→在仿真中测试每项改动→循环迭代。在无人编写机器人代码的条件下，于家居操作任务上运行了123轮改进迭代，并以"把调料放到冰箱顶层货架"为目标任务。

Result: 智能体确实能自主发现能力缺口：它察觉到目标不在视野内，主动请求主动视觉模型、调试并部署了可用的搜索技能。但改进无法累积：改动持续通过测试，目标任务却从未成功。作者发现智能体很少是瓶颈，真正的瓶颈有三：（1）链式感知模块不理解关系，如SAM 3等分割器能定位货架却无法识别"顶层货架"，智能体以不断增多的几何规则弥补却无法收敛；（2）技能链将学习锁定在第一步，长任务多在前段失败，证据与修复堆积于此，后续技能很少被触及、测试和改进；（3）智能体所学内容由框架决定，智能体精确优化了评估器所测量的内容（包括测量错误之处），弱测试与误导性记忆使活动沦为停滞。

Conclusion: 作者将上述教训提炼为构建可自我改进机器人系统的具体建议，并为每条建议配对一个可证伪的实验，以便后续研究验证或推翻。

Abstract: Can a robot improve itself the way coding agents now improve software? We built an agentic system to find out. It watches a robot fail, works out which capability is missing, writes new skills or finds and installs external models, tests every change in simulation, and repeats, with no human writing robot code. We ran it for 123 improvement rounds on household manipulation tasks. This report describes what we learned. The good news is that the agent can discover capabilities on its own: noticing that its targets were out of view, it asked for an active-viewing model, debugged it, and deployed a working search skill. The bad news is that its improvements did not add up. Changes kept passing their tests, yet the target task, putting condiments on the top shelf of a fridge, never succeeded. We found that the agent was rarely the bottleneck. Three things around it were. First, chained perception modules do not understand relations. Segmenters such as SAM 3 find shelves but not "the top shelf", so the agent filled the gap with ever more geometric rules that never converged, when what it needed was a different kind of model. Second, skill chains lock learning onto the first step. Long tasks mostly fail early, so evidence and fixes pile up there, and later skills are rarely reached, tested, or improved. Third, what the agent learns is decided by the harness. The agent optimized exactly what the evaluator measured, including where it was wrong, and weak tests and misleading memory turned activity into a standstill. We distill these lessons into concrete recommendations for building robot systems that improve themselves, each paired with an experiment that could prove it wrong.

</details>


### [2] [Robot Manipulation with GPT-6-Astra: Body Knowledge, Experience Reuse, Emergent Skills, and Sim2Real Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31770)
*Sida He,Lingxi Xie,Yunning Cao,Pengfei Chen,Kaiwen Duan,Jiannan Ge,Xinyue Huo,Jiacheng Shao,Qi Tian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究以GPT-6-Astra驱动的XLeRobot为对象，在仿真与真实电梯按钮任务中验证：提供机器可读的机体描述（几何/相机信息、仿真XML资产）与同步演示经验，可将任务完成时间降低约50%–70%，并将智能体自发产生的视觉反馈程序重构为可复用技能以进一步提速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用多模态智能体虽能编写机器人控制程序，但反复探索与模型介导的动作选择导致执行缓慢。作者希望探究外部机体知识、成功经验与可执行技能能否提升执行效率，并给出一种围绕GPT-6-Astra构建通用操作实验的实用方法。

Method: 在仿真与真实物理平台上开展电梯按钮任务实验。设置基线（仅通用控制接口、无先验经验），分别加入：完整机器人几何与相机信息；带同步动作-状态记录的图像；跨起始位置（偏移10–100 cm）的经验迁移；将智能体自发生成的短视觉反馈程序由研究者重构为可复用技能；以及操作员确认按钮接触条件下的12次真机sim2real试验。

Result: 30次固定起点仿真试验中，几何+相机信息使平均完成时间较基线降低57.4%，同步动作-状态图像降低68.6%（无需额外机体资产）。9组配对比较（18次试验，起点偏移10–100 cm）中，原始起点经验使平均时间降低58–63%，表明对测试新起点具有泛化性。重构技能使27次仿真试验的平均局部任务时间降低29–31%。12次真机试验中，仿真XML资产与仿真经验分别使共享标称起点平均时间降低53.0%和49.9%，真实经验亦可迁移至两个新起点。

Conclusion: 围绕GPT-6-Astra构建通用操作实验的实用路径是：提供机器可读的机体描述与同步演示，将智能体生成的有用反馈例程转化为可复用技能，同时由智能体基于当前图像自适应调整动作。任务提示、试验级实验数据与已获技能实现已开源。

Abstract: General-purpose multimodal agents can write robot-control programs, but repeated exploration and model-mediated action selection can make execution slow. We study how external body knowledge, successful experience, and executable skills improve an XLeRobot controlled by GPT-6-Astra in a simulated and a physical elevator-button task. In 30 fixed-start simulation trials, complete robot geometry and camera information reduce mean completion time by 57.4% relative to a baseline with only the common control interface and no prior experience; images with synchronized action and state records reduce it by 68.6% without additional body assets. In nine paired comparisons (18 trials) at starts displaced by 10-100 cm, experience recorded at the original start reduces mean time by 58-63% relative to no experience, demonstrating generalization to the tested new starting positions. During experience experiments, GPT-6-Astra spontaneously generates a short visual-feedback program. Researcher-refactored versions reduce mean local-task time by 29-31% in 27 simulation trials. Finally, 12 real-robot trials using operator-confirmed button contact demonstrate sim2real reuse: at a shared nominal start, simulation XML assets and simulation experience reduce mean time by 53.0% and 49.9%, respectively; real experience also transfers to two new starts. These results suggest a practical way to build general-purpose manipulation experiments around GPT-6-Astra: supply machine-readable body descriptions and synchronized demonstrations, and turn useful agent-generated feedback routines into reusable skills, while the agent adapts actions from current images. We release all task prompts, trial-level experimental data, and acquired skill implementations at https://github.com/hesd10/astra-robot-sim2real.

</details>


### [3] [Reduced Cartesian Kinetostatics for Tendon-Driven Continuum Robots: Residual-Stabilized Full-Shape Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31771)
*Ke Wu,Fangju Yang,Xiaohui Zhang,Zhengqiang Zhang,Jingang Yi,Jian S. Dai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向平面轴向可压缩腱驱动连续体机器人（TDCR）的降阶笛卡尔框架，用于沿给定腱力/腱位移轨迹高效、准确地预测完整骨干几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TDCR 的规划与控制任务需要完整笛卡尔骨干几何，而逐点几何变应变（GVS）平衡求解计算代价高，难以满足在线/轨迹级预测的效率需求。

Method: 以两个全局位置场表示骨干，通过精确变分与 Taylor-Galerkin 降阶，将给定空间属性与分布载荷离线凝聚为矩向量，得到解析降阶残差与雅可比，无需在线空间求积与数值微分；结合解析微分与残差修正，构造一阶速率系统，每次速率评估仅需一次固定维线性求解（在规则分支初始平衡对齐后）。

Result: 四个仿真案例（变腱路由、非均匀几何、轴向压缩及其组合）中，传播的笛卡尔形状与分布应变与逐点 GVS 平衡解逐点吻合；残差修正抑制步长相关的传播漂移，仅增加约 0.98% 的平均更新时间；平均每次更新 0.508 ms，约为逐点 GVS 求解的 11 倍加速；位移驱动实验最大归一化平均骨干位置误差 1.02%，最大末端位置误差 0.28%。

Conclusion: 该降阶笛卡尔框架能够在给定驱动路径上实现高效且准确的完整形状预测，兼顾精度与实时性，适用于 TDCR 的规划与控制。

Abstract: Many planning and control tasks for tendon-driven continuum robots (TDCRs) require the complete Cartesian backbone geometry. We present a reduced Cartesian framework for planar, axially compressible TDCRs that propagates equilibrium configurations along prescribed tendon-force and tendon-displacement trajectories. The backbone is represented by two global position fields. Following exact variation, a Taylor-Galerkin reduction condenses prescribed spatial properties and distributed loads into offline moment vectors, yielding analytic reduced residuals and Jacobians without online spatial quadrature or numerical differentiation. Analytical differentiation and residual correction yield first-order rate systems requiring one fixed-dimensional linear solve per rate evaluation after initial equilibrium alignment on a regular branch. Across four simulated cases covering variable tendon routing, nonuniform geometry, axial compression, and their combined effects, the propagated Cartesian shapes and distributed strains closely match pointwise geometrically variable-strain (GVS) equilibrium solutions. Residual correction suppresses propagation drift across the tested step sizes while adding only about 0.98% to the mean update time of uncorrected Euler. The proposed method requires 0.508 ms per update on average, approximately 11 times faster than pointwise GVS solves. Displacement-driven experiments yield a maximum normalized mean backbone position error of 1.02% and a maximum end-effector position error of 0.28%. These results support efficient and accurate Cartesian full-shape prediction along prescribed actuation paths.

</details>


### [4] [Timed Rule-Based Supervision of an End-to-End Autonomous Parking Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31773)
*Kejia Gao,Liguo Zhou,Lei Yu,Alois Knoll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在固定CARLA停车场景下，为已有端到端停车策略加装人工标定的时序规则安全盾（PSS），可将目标成功率由85.16%提升至97.66%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端停车策略在闭环运行中会出现重复性失效（越界、制动延迟、停滞或振荡控制），作者希望验证一个手工设定的运行时监督器能否在固定仿真停车场景中纠正这些失败。

Method: 视觉Transformer架构沿用Yang等的工作；本文贡献是加装在控制输出端的时间化、基于规则的安全盾PSS，使用人工标定的速度、位置、时长阈值，对越界、制动延迟、停滞/振荡等失效模式进行干预；闭环评估使用16个留出目标车位与6个初始位姿，每种配置4轮，共384次尝试。

Result: 重训练策略目标成功327/384（85.16%），加入PSS后为375/384（97.66%）；成功尝试的平均位置误差0.21 m、朝向误差0.33°。

Conclusion: 在该仿真器设定内PSS带来提升，但所有尝试共享同一地图、车辆与传感器配置，且PSS使用仿真世界坐标，因此结果不能证明对其他停车场或真实车辆的泛化能力，也不构成形式化安全保证。

Abstract: We study whether a manually specified runtime supervisor can correct recurring failures of an existing end-to-end parking policy in a fixed CARLA parking lot. The vision-based Transformer architecture is inherited from Yang et al.; our contribution is a timed, rule-based Parametric Safety Shield (PSS) applied to its control outputs. The PSS uses hand-calibrated speed, position, and duration thresholds to intervene in observed failure modes, including boundary exits, delayed braking, and stalled or oscillatory control. In the reported closed-loop evaluation, 16 held-out target slots and six initial poses are each evaluated in four rounds (384 attempts per configuration). Target success increases from 327/384 (85.16%) for the retrained policy to 375/384 (97.66%) with the PSS; mean position and orientation errors among successful attempts are 0.21m and 0.33 degrees. These results show an improvement within this simulator setup. The repeated attempts share one map, vehicle, and sensor configuration, and the PSS uses simulator world coordinates; thus the results do not establish generalization to other lots or real vehicles, or a formal safety guarantee.

</details>


### [5] [Path Planning with Motion Primitives in Dynamic Environments: SIPP on Lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31803)
*Marat Agranovskiy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在状态格上运行的SIPP（安全区间路径规划）改编算法，使用预计算的运动基元，并将动态障碍物的时空扫掠体素化到栅格上，从而在动态环境中生成光滑、可执行的轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态环境中与其他移动智能体共享空间时导航困难；传统基于栅格的2^k连通规划器依赖停转机制，产生分段线性轨迹，对差速约束机器人而言运动动力学上高度次优。

Method: 将SIPP扩展到状态格（state lattice）上，采用预计算的、运动动力学平滑的运动基元；将移动障碍物的时空扫掠体（swept volumes）直接体素化到栅格上，以栅格单元为原子空间单位，其分辨率由定位噪声决定；在多种拓扑环境中，将格基方法与2^k连通栅格规划器进行对比评估。

Result: 评估指标包括规划时间、路径角性、累积航向变化（角度/长度）和弯曲能量。结果表明，虽然格基搜索扩展的状态空间增加了计算开销，但生成轨迹的运动动力学特性显著更优；该方法在可达性上可与高连通栅格相当，同时确保路径平滑、连续且物理可执行。

Conclusion: 格基SIPP虽然计算开销更高，但能生成运动动力学更优、可直接部署的平滑轨迹，在可达性上不逊于高连通栅格规划器。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation in dynamic environments is a critical challenge, particularly when spaces are shared with other mobile agents whose future trajectories are known. While traditional grid-based planners efficiently find collision-free paths, their reliance on stop-and-turn mechanics over $2^k$-connected grids produces piecewise-linear trajectories that are kinodynamically highly sub-optimal for differentially constrained robots. In this paper, we present an adaptation of Safe Interval Path Planning (SIPP) that operates on state lattices, utilizing precomputed, kinodynamically smooth motion primitives. To efficiently handle dynamic environments, we rasterize the spatiotemporal swept volumes of moving obstacles directly onto the grid, treating grid cells as atomic units of space, whose resolution is typically dictated by inherent localization noise. We perform a comprehensive comparative analysis between our lattice-based approach and $2^k$-connected grid planners across diverse topological environments. Our evaluation considers a broad spectrum of performance metrics, including planning time, path angularity, cumulative heading change (angle-over-length), and bending energy. The results demonstrate that while the expanded state space of lattice-based search increases computational overhead, it yields trajectories with significantly superior kinodynamic properties. Specifically, our method achieves a reachability comparable to highly connected grids while ensuring smooth, continuous, and physically executable paths ready for real-world deployment.

</details>


### [6] [Humanoid Badminton: Learning Dynamic Racket Skills from Limited Human Motion Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31840)
*Jingzhi Cui,Zhexiong Wang,Bangjie Xu,Pengyu Zhao,Youyuan Li,Zhi Su,Peng Ren,Mengdi Xu,Chao Yu,Yi Wu,Luyang Wang,Zhongyu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出三阶段分层强化学习框架，实现真实人形机器人在羽毛球中的多技能连续对打，包括跳跃回球。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高速球拍类运动对人形机器人提出时间敏感决策、精确击球和动态全身协调的极高要求。羽毛球中球路变化快，需要在极短接触窗口内确定拍面姿态与速度，并覆盖三维击球空间。人类动捕数据可提供先验，但可用的羽毛球参考数据稀少且不完善：直接跟踪难以生成适应多样球况的可执行变化，而纯任务驱动优化易产生不自然动作。

Method: 三阶段分层强化学习框架：（1）任务随机化动作增强，将稀疏标注的击球事件扩展为目标条件化的可执行击球变体，形成连续隐技能空间；（2）高层规划器依据观测到的球状态输出连续隐技能编码，在线组合技能；（3）上下文条件对抗正则器，在保持回球性能的同时使规划器技能使用更自然。

Result: 在真实人形机器人上部署后，系统可与人类选手进行持续的多技能对打，涵盖正手、反手以及高度动态的跳跃回球。这是首个在真实世界中展示多技能人机对打（含高动态跳跃回球）的人形球拍运动系统。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了分层强化学习结合动作增强与对抗正则在人形机器人高速球拍运动中的有效性，为真实场景下的人形机器人敏捷运动技能学习提供了可行路径。

Abstract: High-speed racket sports provide a demanding testbed for humanoid robots, requiring time-critical decisions, precise striking, and dynamic whole-body coordination. In badminton, fast-changing shuttle trajectories require timely contact decisions, while successful returns demand precise racket pose and velocity within a brief contact window and across a broad three-dimensional striking workspace. Human motion data provide valuable priors for such athletic skills, but usable badminton references are limited and imperfect. Direct tracking provides insufficient executable variation for diverse shuttle conditions, while purely task-driven optimization may produce unnatural motion. To address these challenges, we present a three-stage hierarchical reinforcement learning framework for dynamic humanoid badminton. First, task-randomized motion augmentation expands sparse annotated hitting events into executable target-conditioned stroke variations, forming a continuous latent skill space. Second, a high-level planner outputs continuous latent skill codes to compose these skills online according to the observed shuttle state. Third, a context-conditioned adversarial regularizer encourages more natural planner-level skill usage while preserving return performance. When deployed on a real humanoid robot, our system achieves sustained multi-skill rallies with human players, including forehand, backhand, and highly dynamic jump returns. This is the first real-world humanoid racket-sport system to demonstrate multi-skill human--robot rallies including highly dynamic jump returns.

</details>


### [7] [PHIRL: Aligning Learned Rewards with Task Progress for Inverse Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31855)
*Hang Yu,James Staley,Cheng Xi Tsou,Xiujin Liu,Wenchang Gao,Jindan Huang,Shijie Fang,Zhegong Shangguan,Angelo Cangelosi,Reuben Aronson,Elaine Short*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PHIRL框架，联合利用示范的稠密策略信息与progress反馈的精确局部评价，通过逆强化学习迭代学习鲁棒奖励函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类示范提供稠密的策略级信息但缺乏局部精确性；人类反馈提供准确的局部评价但仅为稀疏评估，而非直接策略指导。二者单独使用均存在局限，需要一种能同时利用二者优势的数据高效方法。

Method: PHIRL（Progress-Heuristicized Inverse Reinforcement Learning）：使用progress（描述累积任务完成度的反馈模态），迭代地通过逆强化学习从示范中推断奖励函数，计算学习到的奖励在progress标注示范上的取值，并在四个维度上将奖励与progress标注对齐。

Result: 在真实与仿真机器人任务上评估，并额外使用微调的视觉语言模型提供progress反馈。PHIRL显著优于基线，在仅标注20%示范的情况下取得显著更高的环境回报奖励与任务成功率。奖励黑客场景分析表明所学奖励函数对利用行为具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 联合示范与progress反馈可学习出数据高效、鲁棒且可靠的奖励函数，在有限标注条件下提升策略性能并抵抗奖励黑客。

Abstract: Human demonstrations provide dense policy-level information but sometimes lack local precision. Human feedback presents accurate local critiques, but offers sparse evaluations rather than direct policy guidance. We propose Progress-Heuristicized Inverse Reinforcement Learning (PHIRL), a data-efficient framework that learns robust reward functions by jointly leveraging demonstrations and feedback. Specifically, we use progress, a feedback modality that describes cumulative task completion. PHIRL iteratively infers a reward function from demonstrations via inverse reinforcement learning, calculates the learned rewards over the progress-annotated demonstrations, and aligns the rewards with progress annotations over four dimensions. We evaluate PHIRL on real and simulated robot tasks, with additional exploration using a fine-tuned vision-language model to provide progress feedback. Results demonstrate that PHIRL significantly outperforms the baselines, achieving substantially higher environmental return rewards and task success with only twenty percent of demonstrations annotated. Analysis of reward-hacking scenarios demonstrates that PHIRL learned reward functions are reliable against exploitation.

</details>


### [8] [Efficient Bezier Velocity Optimization for Free-Floating Space Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31880)
*Duo Zhang,Zhizhuo Zhang,Xiaoli Bai,Jingjin Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FAVOR：面向无驱动航天器上7自由度机械臂的无碰撞规划器，基于Bezier关节速度曲线与递归灵敏度，在仿真中显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决无驱动航天器上机械臂在基座-臂耦合运动下进行无碰撞到达、跟踪与规定时间预抓取拦截的规划问题，克服传统方法计算量大、成功率低、易超时的不足。

Method: 优化Bezier关节速度曲线，施加线速度、加速度与连续性约束，决策维度与规划分辨率解耦；通过解析递归灵敏度获得任务与间隙梯度；采用并行评估、缓存与增量式碰撞发现降低计算量。

Result: 7自由度机械臂与5个仿真航天器模型下，点到点成功率达99.8%，平均计算1.779 s；IK初始化位置样条基线为62.9%与50.270 s。跟踪任务完成30/36（单步QP为15），拦截全部36例（样条基线为22）。消融显示有限差分使平均规划时间增加4.7倍。

Conclusion: FAVOR在成功率与计算效率上均显著优于基线，递归灵敏度与增量碰撞发现等设计有效，适用于无驱动航天器机械臂的到达、跟踪与拦截规划。

Abstract: We present FAVOR (Free-floating Arm Velocity Optimization with Recursive Sensitivities), a planner for collision-free reaching, tracking, and prescribed-time pre-grasp interception on an unactuated spacecraft. It optimizes Bezier joint-velocity curves with linear velocity, acceleration, and continuity constraints. Decision dimension is independent of rollout resolution. Analytical recursive sensitivities provide task and clearance gradients through the coupled base-arm motion. Parallel evaluation, caching, and incremental collision discovery reduce computation. With a seven-DoF arm and five simulated spacecraft models, FAVOR achieves 99.8% point-to-point success with 1.779 s mean computation, versus 62.9% and 50.270 s for an IK-initialized position-spline baseline. Means include failures and timeouts. FAVOR completes 30 of 36 tracking cases, versus 15 for single-step QP, and all 36 interception instances, versus 22 for the spline baseline. A controlled ablation shows that finite differences increase mean planning time 4.7-fold.

</details>


### [9] [Differentiable Dynamics for Autonomous Micro-Mobility Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31885)
*Grace Cai,Joey Lee,Nithin Parepally,Laura Zheng,Ming C. Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出可微分动力学模型 DiffKBM 与 DiffGM3，支持微出行车辆端到端可微优化，在开环轨迹拟合与闭环导航中均优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微出行车辆（轮椅、滑板车、自行车）实现自主化需要真实、可预测的运动模型，但现有自动驾驶栈多依赖简化运动学模型，无法刻画轮胎滑移、摩擦与轮系布局等关键特性，限制了真实性与基于梯度的优化。

Method: 构建 DiffKBM（运动学自行车模型的可微分版本）；进一步提出 DiffGM3，即通用微出行模型 GM3 的可微分形式，该模型为基于轮胎力学的统一动力学建模，支持多种微出行构型。两者使微出行动力学可端到端求导，便于嵌入可微分自动驾驶栈。评估分两类：(1) 开环轨迹匹配，将两模型作为 DiffStack 中的动力学层，优化以复现真实微出行轨迹；(2) 闭环自主导航，将两模型与可微分 MPC 控制器结合，在 CrowdNav 行人场景中测试。

Result: 开环设置下，DiffGM3 在自行车、滑板车、摩托车三种模式下复现轨迹的 ADE 与 NLL 均优于 DiffKBM，并降低自行车与摩托车轨迹的规划损失；闭环设置下，DiffGM3 在自行车模式上相较 DiffKBM 使碰撞减少 55%、不适频次降低 75%。

Conclusion: 可微分的轮胎基统一动力学（DiffGM3）比可微分运动学模型（DiffKBM）更真实、更适用于微出行自主系统的开环学习与闭环控制，是构建微出行可微分自动驾驶栈的有效方案。

Abstract: Autonomous micro-mobility vehicles (MMVs) such as wheelchairs, scooters, and bicycles have the potential to improve mobility access and support safe low-speed transportation in pedestrian-shared spaces. Achieving MMV autonomy will require realistic, predictable MMV motion. However, many existing autonomous vehicle stacks rely on simplified kinematic models that fail to capture key MMV characteristics such as tire slip, friction, and wheel layouts, limiting realism and gradient-based optimization. In this paper, we explore differentiable formulations of dynamics models for autonomous micro-mobility systems. We first construct DiffKBM, a differentiable version of the kinematic bicycle model (KBM). Then, we introduce DiffGM3, a differentiable formulation of the General Micro-Mobility Model (GM3), a unified tire-based dynamics formulation for micro-mobility vehicles that supports a wide range of MMV configurations. DiffKBM and DiffGM3 enable end-to-end differentiable optimization through MMV dynamics, making them suitable for integration into differentiable autonomy stacks. We evaluate these dynamics models in both open-loop and closed-loop settings: (1) open-loop trajectory matching, where DiffKBM and DiffGM3 are integrated as a dynamics layer within DiffStack and optimized to reproduce real-world MMV trajectories, and (2) closed-loop autonomous navigation, where DiffKBM and DiffGM3 are paired with a differentiable MPC controller in CrowdNav pedestrian scenarios. In the open-loop setting, DiffGM3 outperforms DiffKBM in reproducing trajectories with improvements in ADE and NLL across bicycle, scooter, and motorcycle modes, and reductions in planning loss for bicycle and motorcycle trajectories. We also find that, in closed-loop settings, DiffGM3 improves on DiffKBM's CrowdNav performance by producing 55\% fewer collisions and a 75\% lower discomfort frequency for the bicycle mode.

</details>


### [10] [Game-Theoretic Control with Constrained Potential Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31901)
*Zhiyuan Zhang,Panagiotis Tsiotras*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种用于约束一般和动态博弈的快速通用内点求解器，配合高效二阶校正，提升局部广义纳什均衡收敛概率，并在数值基准与缩比赛车实验中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约束一般和动态博弈广泛用于高交互多智能体规划。现有广义纳什均衡（GNE）求解器虽在小规模动态博弈中达到实时性能，但求解速度仍是瓶颈，难以控制四个以上智能体；且基于一阶条件的牛顿求解器易陷入非纳什鞍点，解的有效性受限。

Method: 提出面向约束动态博弈的快速通用内点求解器，并设计计算高效的二阶校正方法，以提高在约束动态博弈中收敛到局部GNE解的概率。

Result: 在数值基准测试以及缩比赛车物理实验上对所提方法进行了性能评估。

Conclusion: 该方法在求解速度与解的有效性方面具有优势，扩展了约束动态博弈在多智能体实时规划中的适用范围。

Abstract: Constrained general-sum dynamic games are a popular formulation for highly interactive multi-agent planning problems. In recent years, Generalized Nash Equilibrium (GNE) solvers have achieved real-time performance for small dynamic games. However, solution speed still remains a bottleneck, and controlling even a small number of agents (e.g., more than four) in a dynamic task remains elusive. In addition, Newton solvers that focus on the first-order conditions are vulnerable to non-Nash saddle points, limiting the usefulness of the provided solution. In this work, we propose a fast and versatile interior point solver for constrained dynamic games, along with a computationally efficient second-order correction that increases the probability of converging to a local GNE solution in constrained dynamic games. The performance of the proposed method is evaluated on numerical benchmarks and a physical experiment involving scaled race cars.

</details>


### [11] [GT-VLA: Target-Conditioned Trace Guidance for Generalizable Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31904)
*Ninghan Zhong,Jing-Chen Peng,Sriram Vishwanath*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GT-VLA 通过外部通用 VLM 生成 2D 视觉轨迹作为条件，结合 Mixture-of-Experts 技能专用模块，提升 VLA 模型在新任务、新配置和长时序场景下的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型在机器人操作上表现良好，但易过拟合训练场景，难以泛化到未见任务、配置和长时序设定，且难以遵循新语言指令；而通用 VLM 泛化更强却无法直接控制动作。

Method: 提出 GT-VLA 框架：由通用 VLM 识别当前技能的语义引导，将其转化为 2D 视觉轨迹，并以轨迹渲染观测作为动作策略的条件输入，实现语义目标获取、轨迹生成与底层动作执行的解耦；采用 Mixture-of-Experts 架构，配备技能专用的轨迹与动作模块。

Result: 在 LIBERO 基准和真实机器人平台上评估，GT-VLA 在两种设定下的泛化性能均优于近期 VLA 基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过轨迹条件化动作生成将通用 VLM 的常识与 VLA 的控制能力结合，可有效提升机器人操作的泛化性，代码与补充材料已公开。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown strong performance on robotic manipulation, but they often struggle to generalize to unseen tasks, configurations, and long-horizon settings. A key challenge is that VLAs overfit to training scenes and fail to follow novel language instructions. Off-the-shelf vision-language models (VLMs) often provide stronger generalization, but cannot directly control robot actions. To combine the common sense of VLMs with VLA control, we propose Guided Trace VLA (GT-VLA), a steerable framework that accepts guidance from an external generalist VLM through trace-conditioned action generation. GT-VLA uses a generalist model to identify semantic guidance for the current skill, converts this guidance into a 2D visual trace, and conditions its action policy on the resulting trace-rendered observation. This design separates semantic target acquisition, trace generation, and low-level action execution, allowing high-level guidance to propagate to robot actions. GT-VLA uses a Mixture-of-Experts architecture with skill-specific trace and action modules for robust execution. We evaluate GT-VLA on LIBERO and a physical robot platform, showing improved generalization over recent VLA baselines in both settings. The code and additional supplemental materials are available on our project website at https://ivaniz.github.io/gt-vla/.

</details>


### [12] [End-to-end QP-based policies: A unified perspective on robust control and robot learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31905)
*Fausto Vega,Priyanka Supraja Balaji,Chase Dunaway,Joe Koszut,Jon Arrizabalaga,Zachary Manchester*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种端到端、基于二次规划（QP）的策略框架，用最少领域特定设计实现系统化策略构建，同时保持模型控制的透明性与可解释性，并在仿真与硬件上验证了鲁棒性、自动调参及未知动力学控制等应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于模型的策略表示通常依赖较多领域特定设计；而端到端学习策略虽灵活却缺乏透明性与可解释性。论文旨在兼顾系统化、低设计成本的策略构建与模型控制的可解释性。

Method: 提出统一的QP策略表示：支持域随机化下的基于模型自动调参（在干扰与模型变化分布上优化策略参数），以及黑盒策略构建（在模型未知、无需显式状态、输入或动力学概念的情况下构建问题）；并将该表示与鲁棒控制、多层感知机及机器人学习等范式建立联系，视其为这些方法的共同抽象。

Result: 在仿真与硬件实验中验证了框架有效性，覆盖鲁棒性、自动策略调参以及未知系统动力学下的控制等广泛应用。

Conclusion: 端到端QP策略可作为多种控制与学习范式的统一抽象，在保持可解释性的同时实现系统化策略构建，并适用于鲁棒性、自动调参和未知动力学控制等任务。

Abstract: We present an end-to-end QP-based policy framework that enables systematic policy construction with minimal domain-specific design, while preserving the transparency and interpretability of model-based control. The proposed policy representation supports both domain-randomized model-based auto-tuning, where policy parameters are optimized over distributions of disturbances and model variations, and black-box policy construction, where the problem is formulated in terms of a (possibly) unknown model without requiring explicit notions of states, inputs, or the underlying system dynamics. We establish connections to existing policy representations and control paradigms, including robust control, multilayer perceptrons, and robot learning, and interpret our end-to-end QP policies as a common abstraction of these approaches. We validate the resulting framework in both simulation and hardware, demonstrating a broad range of applications spanning robustness, automatic policy tuning, and control under unknown system dynamics.

</details>


### [13] [HapticWorld: an Interactive World Simulator with Real-time Torque Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31924)
*Shaoting Peng,Litian Liang,Yixuan Wang,Ming Yang,Katherine Driggs-Campbell,Mark Cutkosky,James Jingxi Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HapticWorld 是一个可交互的世界模拟器，能在预测观测的同时预测关节力矩，并实时将力反馈渲染给操作者，从而在人机之间闭合触觉回路，实现高吞吐的力/力矩标注数据采集与策略评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触密集型操作依赖力感知，而力信息难以从视觉信号中推断，这同时影响示教采集与策略训练。力标注数据难以规模化获取：真机采集受硬件限制；物理仿真器的接触力与真实测量存在系统性偏差；学习型世界模拟器虽可扩展且逼真，但仅支持视觉，导致操作者在采集时无任何触觉感受、数据也不带力/力矩标签。

Method: 提出 HapticWorld：一个可交互的世界模拟器，在预测观测的同时预测关节力矩，并将力矩实时渲染回传给操作者，从而在人类与学习型世界模型之间闭合触觉回路。

Result: 在三个接触密集型任务上，力矩反馈使数据采集吞吐量平均提升 1.6 倍。基于 HapticWorld 生成示教训练的策略在真实世界试验中成功 54/60，接近真实数据 56/60 的上界，远高于仅视觉基线的 19/60。此外，HapticWorld 内部测得成功率与真实世界评估高度吻合。

Conclusion: HapticWorld 可作为一个独立的、以力/力矩为条件的策略评估平台，同时显著提升力标注示教数据的采集效率与下游策略的真实世界表现。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation depends on force sensing that is hard to infer from visual signals alone, both for collecting demonstrations and for training policies. Force-annotated data, however, remains hard to obtain at scale: real-robot collection ties every demonstration to physical hardware, physics simulators report contact forces that deviate systematically from real measurements, and learned world simulators, though scalable and realistic, are vision-only, so operators feel nothing during data collection and the data carries no force/torque (F/T) labels. We present HapticWorld, an interactive world simulator that predicts joint torque together with observations and renders it back to the operator in real time, closing the haptic loop between a human and a learned world model. Across three contact-rich tasks, torque feedback raises data collection throughput by 1.6 times on average. Policies trained on HapticWorld-generated demonstrations succeed in 54/60 real-world trials, approaching the 56/60 upper bound of real-world data, and far exceeding the 19/60 success rate of the vision-only baseline. Moreover, the success rates measured inside HapticWorld closely match real-world evaluation, demonstrating that HapticWorld can serve as a stand-alone F/T-conditioned policy evaluation platform.

</details>


### [14] [Inertia-Corrected Newton Method For Generalized Nash Equilibria in Dynamic Games with Optimality Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31929)
*Zhiyuan Zhang,Panagiotis Tsiotras*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种高效数值方法，用于验证动态博弈中局部广义纳什均衡（GNE）的二阶充分条件（SOSC），并给出一个带惯性校正更新的快速牛顿求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 牛顿法通过求解KKT必要条件可高效求取动态博弈的GNE，且速度快、可支持高动态多智能体MPC。然而，仅凭较小的KKT残差无法证明所得解满足局部GNE的二阶充分条件，因此缺乏可靠的最优性认证手段。

Method: 作者将智能体KKT矩阵的惯性（inertia）与代价函数约化Hessian在约束零空间投影上的正定性建立联系，据此验证局部GNE的SOSC；并引入惯性校正的更新步，通过使弱跨智能体耦合的严格鞍点失稳来改善向局部GNE的收敛。

Result: 数值基准实验表明，所提求解器在实际多智能体规划问题中具有良好的运行时间与收敛性能；并在微型自主赛车平台上验证了其实时能力。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献是一个面向约束动态博弈的快速牛顿求解器，能够高效地进行最优性检验，并在仿真与物理实验中验证了其实用性与实时性。

Abstract: Newton methods efficiently find Generalized Nash Equilibria (GNE) in dynamic games by solving for the KKT necessary conditions. These methods are fast and can support multi-agent Model Predictive Control (MPC) for highly dynamic robots. However, a small KKT residual alone does not certify that the returned solution satisfies the second-order sufficient conditions for a local GNE.
  In this paper, we propose an efficient numerical method to verify the second-order sufficient conditions (SOSC) for a local GNE. We connect the inertia of the agent KKT matrix with the positive definiteness of the reduced Hessian of the cost function, projected onto the null space of the constraints. Furthermore, we introduce an inertia-corrected update step that improves convergence to local GNEs by destabilizing strict saddle points with weak cross-agent coupling.
  Our main contribution is a fast Newton solver for Constrained Dynamic Games that provides efficient optimality checking. Through numerical benchmarks, we demonstrate the proposed solver's runtime and convergence performance in practical multi-agent planning problems. We also validate the solver's real-time capabilities in physical experiments using a platform of miniature autonomous race cars.

</details>


### [15] [Low Cost Eye Tracking for Vision Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31941)
*Maissa Abir Smaili,Yaman Ismaiel,Zafer Iscan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工程研究评估了在视觉筛查中采用注视辅助数据采集的效果，比较了基于网络摄像头与GC308近红外摄像机的Orlosky眼动追踪流程，但所报告的结果为行为输出与采集稳定性，而非视觉 acuity 或注视精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估在视觉筛查任务中引入注视辅助数据采集的可行性与稳定性，并比较两种采集条件（网络摄像头流程与GC308近红外眼动仪流程）的表现，以探索眼动追踪用于视觉筛查数据采集的工程实现。

Method: 10名被试在两种采集条件下完成Gaze Quest视觉筛查任务。行为输出包括Tumbling E方向判断准确率、应用程序logMAR值和按键反应潜伏期。GC308眼动仪衍生诊断指标为相邻归一化注视向量间夹角（28.2度）的逐帧变化，有效采样率约8 FPS。GC308输出未做屏幕目标校准或注视到屏幕的映射，因此仅反映采集稳定性而非目标注视精度；网络摄像头流程采用25点屏幕校准。

Result: GC308的有效采样率约为8 FPS，其逐帧归一化注视向量夹角变化为28.2度。由于未进行屏幕目标校准与注视-屏幕映射，GC308数据仅代表采集稳定性。由于研究记录未保留显示分辨率和像素间距，所报告的应用程序logMAR值不能被视为视觉 acuity 的度量。

Conclusion: 该研究的眼动相关数据反映的是采集稳定性而非注视精度，logMAR值也因缺少显示参数记录而不能作为视觉 acuity 指标；结果应被理解为工程层面的行为输出与数据采集稳定性评估，而非临床视觉筛查结论。

Abstract: This engineering study assesses gaze-assisted data acquisition during visual screening with a Gaze Quest implementation via webcam and GC308 near infrared camera with an Orlosky eye tracking pipeline. Ten subjects performed under both acquisition conditions. Accuracy of Tumbling E orientation, reported application logMAR value,and response latency were determined from keyboard responses and hence are behavioral outputs as opposed toeye tracker screening results. Frame-to-frame change in theangle between successive normalized gaze vectors (28.2degrees) served as the GC308 eye tracker derived diagnostic with an effective sampling rate of roughly 8 FPS. No screen target calibration or gaze to screen translation was done on the GC308 eye tracker output data for the current analysis and hence, these data represent acquisition stability and not gaze accuracy in relation to the target. The webcam pipeline relied on 25-point screen calibration. Because the display resolution and pixel pitch could not be retained in the records of the study, the reported application logMAR values cannot be viewed as measures of visual acuity in any way.

</details>


### [16] [Grasp2Twist: Learning Bimanual Dexterous Jar Opening by Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32064)
*Mo Xu,Yunfu Deng,Jianuo Wang,Josiah Hanna,Bilge Mutlu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Grasp2Twist 双臂灵巧操作系统，用强化学习实现抓取并拧开瓶盖，零样本迁移到真实系统，六种家用容器上成功率达 88%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习"抓取并拧开瓶盖"任务存在三大挑战：多阶段任务的统一策略学习、持续拧动瓶盖、以及仿真到现实的迁移。

Method: 1) 引入连续包络度量引导抓取形成、二元包络指示器引导抓取到拧动的过渡，二者均由物体中心与手掌指尖构成凸包的几何关系推导而来，用于统一策略学习；2) 采用三阶段课程学习，促进手指接触重构的探索并提升迁移鲁棒性，以应对固定接触下运动学约束限制旋转幅度的问题。

Result: 学习到的策略展现出手指换位（finger gaiting），通过重构手-物接触维持瓶盖旋转；零样本迁移到物理系统，在包括花生酱、维生素和速溶咖啡罐在内的六种家用容器上达到 88% 的任务成功率。消融实验验证了几何包络在抓取形成中的作用以及课程学习在接触重构探索中的作用。

Conclusion: 几何包络度量与分阶段课程学习相结合，可有效支撑多阶段双臂灵巧操作策略的统一学习与仿真到现实迁移。

Abstract: This paper presents Grasp2Twist, a bimanual dexterous manipulation system that learns to grasp and twist open jar lids using reinforcement learning. Learning this task raises three challenges: learning a unified policy for a multi-stage task, sustaining lid twisting, and sim-to-real transfer. To address the first challenge, we introduce a continuous enclosure measure to guide grasp formation and a binary enclosure indicator to guide the grasp-to-twist transition for unified policy learning. We derive both from the geometric relationship between the object center and the convex hull formed by the hand's palm and fingertips. Kinematic constraints limit how far the hand can rotate the lid with fixed contacts, so sustained twisting requires finger contact reconfiguration. We use a three-stage curriculum to facilitate exploration of these contact changes and also improve robustness for sim-to-real transfer. With our approach, the learned policy demonstrates finger gaiting, reconfiguring hand-object contacts to sustain lid rotation. It transfers zero-shot to the physical system and achieves an 88% task success rate across six household containers, including peanut-butter, vitamin, and instant-coffee jars. Ablations further validate the roles of the geometric enclosure in grasp formation and the curriculum in contact-reconfiguration exploration.

</details>


### [17] [Find Something You Can't Do: Agentic Real-World Reinforcement Learning for Self-Improving VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32069)
*Yuan Fang,Zechu Li,Haolei Tong,Puze Liu,Georgia Chalvatzaki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FIND 是一个面向真实世界的自主强化学习框架，将机器人自主练习建模为"场景条件化、性能感知的任务选择"问题，通过视觉-语言智能体识别可行任务、优先练习近期成功率低的任务，并用执行前后观测对自评估结果，从而在冻结 VLA 策略之上实现持续改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型为机器人操作提供了强先验，但通常以冻结策略形式部署，无法从自身失败中学习。真实世界强化学习虽可持续提升，却受制于人工环境重置与任务成功监督，难以实现自主学习。

Method: FIND 在持久化工作空间中将场景理解、弱点感知练习、自评估与策略改进闭环。它不预设场景，而是利用每次 rollout 后产生的场景决定下一步练习内容；由视觉-语言智能体从预定义任务库中筛选可行任务，优先选择近期成功率较低者，并通过执行前-执行后配对观测评估结果。实例化采用冻结的 π₀.₅ VLA 与残差 off-policy RL。

Result: 在八项真实世界操作任务上，独立人工评估的成功率从 55% 提升至 71.9%。一次代表性运行在 6 小时交互内完成 456 个自主 episode，仅需 30 次场景恢复干预，在线学习过程中无人工提供奖励标签。消融实验与系统性评估进一步考察了关键设计选择、智能体评估准确率及人工干预需求。

Conclusion: FIND 证明了在真实世界中无需人工奖励标签与频繁环境重置即可实现自主持续学习，为在冻结 VLA 策略上进行自我进化的机器人操作提供了可行路径，并公开了项目网站。

Abstract: Vision--language--action (VLA) models provide strong priors for robotic manipulation but are typically deployed as frozen policies, unable to improve from their own failures. Real-world reinforcement learning (RL) offers a path to continued improvement, yet manual environment resets and task-success supervision hinder autonomous learning. We introduce \textbf{FIND}, an agentic real-world RL framework that closes the loop between scene understanding, weakness-aware practice, self-evaluation, and policy improvement in a persistent workspace. FIND reframes autonomous practice as a scene-conditioned, performance-aware task-selection problem: instead of restoring a predefined scene after each rollout, it uses the resulting scene to determine what to practice next. A vision--language agent identifies feasible tasks from a predefined library, prioritizes those with lower recent success rates, and evaluates outcomes using paired pre- and post-execution observations. We instantiate FIND with a frozen $π_{0.5}$ VLA and residual off-policy RL. Across eight real-world manipulation tasks, the independent human-assessed success rate improves from $55\%$ to $71.9\%$. A representative run completes 456 autonomous episodes within 6 hours of interaction, requiring 30 scene-recovery interventions and no human-provided reward labels during online learning. Ablations and systematic evaluations further examine key design choices, agent evaluation accuracy, and human intervention requirements. Our website is made publicly available at: FIND.github.io.

</details>


### [18] [Fiber-Normalized Manipulability and Determinant Proxies: Intrinsic Redundancy Optimization Across and Within Task Fibers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32079)
*Antonio Franchi,Mirko Mizzoni*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作证明行列式型可操作度是固定任务冗余优化的精确目标，并提出纤维归一化可操作度以实现跨任务纤维的比较与轨迹优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行列式型可操作度依赖任务坐标与任务空间度量，其作为优化目标的合理性以及跨任务纤维比较的可行性受到质疑，因此需要澄清其在固定纤维上的等价性并构造可跨纤维比较的内禀指标。

Method: 在每条正则任务纤维上，证明行列式代理、其在任意任务图卡中的表示以及任何度量补全后的可操作度仅相差正常数；据此等价性分析排序、约束极值、梯度方向、临界点与局部最优分类。进而定义纤维归一化可操作度：内部状态处的能力除以同一纤维上可达到的最佳能力，得到无量纲标量，并构造相应损失用于跨纤维轨迹优化。

Result: 证明了固定纤维上多种可操作度表示仅差正标量，因而诱导相同的完全序、约束极值、梯度方向、临界点及局部最优分类；纤维归一化可操作度对内部状态流形与任务流形的坐标变换不变，且独立于任务空间度量，其损失可作为物理可行跨纤维轨迹的内禀目标，涵盖任务演化给定或自由以及时间耦合的情形。

Conclusion: 行列式型可操作度因其纤维内等价性而成为固定任务冗余优化的精确目标；纤维归一化可操作度则为跨纤维比较与轨迹优化提供了不依赖坐标与度量的内禀度量。平面机械臂与冗余气动分配示例验证了固定纤维等价性、任务相关的跨纤维差异以及纤维归一化轨迹优化的不变性。

Abstract: This work establishes that determinant-based manipulability is an exact objective for fixed-task redundancy optimization, despite its dependence on task coordinates and the choice of task-space metric used for volume measurement. On every regular task fiber, the determinant proxy, its representation in any task chart, and every metric-completed manipulability differ only by positive constants. They consequently induce the same complete ordering, constrained extrema, gradient directions, critical points, and local optimality classifications.
  For comparisons and trajectory optimization across task fibers, this work introduces fiber-normalized manipulability: the capability attained at an internal state divided by the best capability available on the same fiber. The resulting dimensionless scalar is invariant under coordinate changes on the internal-state and task manifolds and independent of the task-space metric. Its associated loss provides an intrinsic objective for physically admissible cross-fiber trajectories, including problems with prescribed or free task evolution and temporal coupling. Planar-manipulator and redundant aerodynamic-allocation examples demonstrate fixed-fiber equivalence, task-dependent cross-fiber differences, and invariant fiber-normalized trajectory optimization.

</details>


### [19] [Active 3D weaves for load-bearing and damage-resilient locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32099)
*Guowei Wayne Tu,Evgueni T. Filipov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出将活性材料集成到三维编织壳体的通用框架，用于构建兼具高轴向刚度承载、低弯曲刚度驱动及系统级抗损伤能力的编织机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编织工艺通过材料交织，在软体机器人、假肢、可穿戴设备和外骨骼等领域具有广阔应用潜力，但这类织物系统固有柔性限制了承载能力，而承载能力对许多机器人功能至关重要。

Method: 将活性材料集成到三维编织壳体中，以"编织角"（3D编织结构基本单元）进行模块化装配；利用特征值计算识别三维编织结构的承载与驱动机理，并据此设计五种具备运动能力的机器人。

Result: 所制备的三维编织机器人能够负重达自重的70倍进行运动，且在承受极端压缩后仍能保持可重复的性能。

Conclusion: 该工作为未来三维编织机器人系统的设计、制造和仿真提供了路径，此类系统可同时满足承载、高刚度、功能性主动变形、运动及系统级韧性等需求。

Abstract: The craft of weaving, where different materials are interlaced, has tremendous potential for creating active and functional systems for use in soft robots, prosthetics, wearables, exoskeletons, and more. These textile-like systems are flexible and safe for human-machine interaction; however, this inherent flexibility limits their ability to carry loads, which is essential for many robotic functions. In this work, we introduce a general framework for integrating active materials into three-dimensional (3D) woven shells to create robotic structures that combine high axial stiffness for load bearing, low bending stiffness for efficient actuation, and system-level resilience for damage tolerance. These woven robots can be modularly assembled from 'woven corners', a fundamental unit of 3D woven structures. We use eigenvalue calculations to identify load bearing and actuation mechanisms of the 3D woven structures, and use that information to make five different robots capable of locomotion. We demonstrate that these 3D woven robots can locomote carrying loads 70 times their self-weight, and can maintain repeatable performance even after being subjected to extreme compression. This work is a pathway toward the design, manufacturing, and simulation of future 3D woven robotic systems where load bearing, high stiffness, active functional deformation, locomotion, and system-level resilience are all needed.

</details>


### [20] [Residual Denoising Enables Sample-Efficient Multi-Agent Coordination on Demand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32129)
*Dayi Dong,Maulik Bhatt,Aayushi Shrivastava,Lasse Peters,Negar Mehr*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 ALTER，一种将预训练单智能体扩散策略适配到多智能体协作场景的方法，通过少量协作数据与残差去噪头的协同优化，在仅用自身视觉观测、无通信的去中心化执行下，同时实现高协作性能与单智能体技能保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练机器人策略具备较强的操作技能，但通常局限于单智能体设定，无法在团队中与其他机器人协作；而直接用少量协作数据微调又会导致原有单智能体技能退化。因此需要一种能同时兼顾"按需协作"与"技能保留"的适配方法。

Method: ALTER 将单智能体扩散策略适配为多智能体策略：训练一个协调头（coordination head）预测残差去噪器，在需要时将单智能体行为转换为协调的多智能体行为，无需时则保留原行为。为保持单智能体能力，在训练残差去噪器时，用基础策略自蒸馏生成的样本对小规模协作示范数据进行增强。执行阶段完全去中心化，每个机器人仅依赖自身视觉观测、无智能体间显式通信。

Result: 在仿真中，ALTER 相比基线取得更高的协作成功率，同时源技能保留率显著更高；在真实硬件实验中呈现类似趋势，ALTER 在协作成功率与单智能体技能保留两方面均优于基线。

Conclusion: 结果表明，通过残差式协调头与自蒸馏数据增强，可在少量协作数据下有效实现预训练单智能体扩散策略的按需协作适配，兼顾团队协作与独立作业能力，并具备迁移到真实硬件的可行性。

Abstract: Pretrained robot policies offer strong manipulation skills but are typically limited to single-agent settings, where a robot acts in isolation. In this work, we study how to adapt pretrained single-agent diffusion policies to multi-agent settings using minimal collaborative data, co-optimizing for two key objectives: high coordination performance and single-agent skill retention. To this end, we introduce ALTER, an adaptation method for coordination on demand: the adapted policy coordinates with other robots when deployed in a team while remaining capable of acting independently when operating alone. Execution is decentralized: each robot acts only on its own visual observations, without explicit inter-agent communication. Our method trains a coordination head that predicts a residual denoiser to transform single-agent behavior into coordinated multi-agent behavior when necessary while also preserving single-agent capabilities. To preserve single-agent capabilities, we augment a small number of collaborative demonstrations with self-distilled data generated by the base policy during training of the residual denoiser. In simulation,ALTER achieves higher coordination success over our baselines while retaining much higher source-skill retention. In our hardware experiments, we find similar trends where ALTER better co-optimizes for coordination success and single-agent skill retention than the baselines

</details>


### [21] [GAUGE: Planner-Conditioned Active Calibration of Opaque Quadruped Velocity Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32154)
*Tianhao Zang,Zihan Liu,Shanze Wang,Liyou Luo,Xingjian Xie,Chengtai Li,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GAUGE框架，用目标感知与不确定性引导的主动校准方法，校准不透明的四足机器人速度接口，在更少试验次数下达到精度与不确定性双指标，并在仿真导航中不劣于密集校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商用四足机器人通常只开放平面速度指令接口，底层运动控制器不可访问，导致指令速度与实际运动之间存在系统性偏差；而导航规划器通常只使用指令包络中的一个结构化子集，因此需要针对该任务相关分布进行高效、可靠的接口校准。

Method: GAUGE维护一个贝叶斯“指令—运动”模型，基于规划器诱发（planner-induced）的指令分布，按后验认知不确定性（epistemic uncertainty）的期望缩减量选择被授权的试验；其后验用于基于验证的停止准则、有界逆补偿，以及检测到接口漂移后的任务相关重校准。

Result: 在三个受控响应族中，GAUGE以比被动、D-最优（D-optimal）和任务无关方法更少的试验次数，达到面向任务的精度与不确定性的联合判据；在六张留出的Isaac Sim导航地图上，相对密集校准满足预设的非劣性裕度。

Conclusion: 目标感知、不确定性引导的主动校准能够在规划器相关指令分布下高效校准不透明四足速度接口，兼顾精度与不确定性，并在导航任务中达到与密集校准相当的性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a Goal-Aware Uncertainty-Guided Exploration (GAUGE) framework for planner-conditioned active calibration of opaque quadruped velocity interfaces. Commercial quadrupeds commonly expose planar-velocity commands, but the underlying locomotion controller remains inaccessible and can produce systematic discrepancies between commanded and realized motion. A navigation planner typically uses a structured subset of the command envelope. GAUGE maintains a Bayesian command-to-motion model and selects authorized trials according to their expected reduction of posterior epistemic uncertainty under the planner-induced command distribution. The resulting posterior supports validation-based stopping, bounded inverse compensation, and task-relevant recalibration after detected interface shifts. In three controlled response families, GAUGE reaches the joint criterion for task-facing accuracy and uncertainty with fewer trials than passive, D-optimal, and task-agnostic alternatives. Across six held-out Isaac Sim navigation maps, it meets the declared noninferiority margins against dense calibration. Code is available at https://github.com/EurekaZang/CalibAgent.

</details>


### [22] [RecastVLA: From Past Interaction to Future Control with Adaptive Policy States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32155)
*Wenbo Li,Jun Yang,Yiteng Chen,Wei Zhang,Qingyao Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RecastVLA 将"历史表示"从观测侧转移到动作生成侧，用共享快速权重在流匹配 VLA 策略内部维护随动作生成而演化的自适应策略状态，并通过后续动作损失端到端训练状态的初始化、接口与更新规则，在仿真与真机任务上稳定超越无测试时训练的匹配基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 顺序操作任务要求机器人记住已发生的交互，而当场景本身不再暴露这些信息时，仅靠当前观测无法恢复历史。已有做法多用显式的历史表示（如观测/隐状态记忆）把过去作为上下文喂给当前决策，但并未回答"动作生成过程本身能否构成一个持续状态"。本文正是要探索：能否让动作生成自身形成用于后续控制的持久状态。

Method: 基于动作侧测试时训练（action-side test-time training），在流匹配（flow-matching）VLA 策略内部维持一个自适应策略状态。该状态由共享快速权重（shared fast weights）表示，并在整个动作生成过程中保持固定；不同深度的专用接口读取同一状态，而跨深度与跨流评估步的特征共同定义一个更新量，用于下一次策略调用。训练阶段通过反向传播穿过更早的状态转移，用后续动作损失来学习状态的初始化、接口和更新规则；部署阶段只用策略自身的动作生成特征进行更新，不需要专家动作标签。

Result: 在 LIBERO、RoboTwin、RoboDojo 与 12 项真机任务上，RecastVLA 相比禁用测试时训练的匹配策略提升了平均成功率，其中 RoboTwin Clean-to-Clean 提升 10.68 个百分点。在受控的 RoboTwin 对比中，保留状态可提升成功率；共享状态设计比各层独立训练的 layer-local TTT 高出 2.58 个百分点。

Conclusion: 动作生成过程本身可以充当持久策略状态的载体：以共享快速权重表达、跨深度跨流步联合更新的 RecastVLA 证明，动作侧的测试时训练能在无需专家动作标签的情况下持续改进后续控制，且共享状态优于逐层独立的自适应，为顺序操作中的历史建模提供了观测侧记忆之外的一条有效路径。

Abstract: Sequential manipulation requires a robot to track what has already happened, even when the current scene no longer reveals it. Policies with explicit history representations make past interactions available as context for current decisions. We ask how action generation itself can form a persistent state for subsequent control. Building on action-side test-time training, RecastVLA maintains an adaptive policy state within a flow-matching vision-language-action policy. The state is represented by shared fast weights and remains fixed throughout action generation. Depth-specific interfaces read the same state, while features across depths and flow evaluations jointly define one update for the next policy call. Subsequent action losses train the initialization, interfaces, and update rule by differentiating through earlier state transitions. At deployment, updates use the policy's own action-generation features without expert action labels. Across LIBERO, RoboTwin, RoboDojo, and twelve real-robot tasks, RecastVLA improves mean success over a matched policy trained without test-time training, including 10.68 percentage points on RoboTwin Clean-to-Clean. In controlled RoboTwin comparisons, retaining state improves success, and the shared design exceeds independently trained layer-local TTT by 2.58 points.

</details>


### [23] [AquaBEV-Nav: Learned BEV Occupancy for Underwater Navigation and Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32156)
*Trung Tien Dong,Zhenqi Wu,Sahasra Kondapalli,Jiayi Wu,Yi Sheng,Xiaomin Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AquaBEV-Nav是一种水下探索框架，通过直接预测鸟瞰图占据栅格来绕过显式单目深度估计，在水下珊瑚礁环境中实现了高占据预测精度与零碰撞的全覆盖探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下安全探索需要机器人理解周围结构位置及可通行区域。现有基于视觉的水下系统通常依赖单目深度估计，再将几何反投影到3D空间并累积为2D鸟瞰占据图。然而，水下散射和波长相关衰减会削弱视觉线索，导致单目深度估计可靠性下降，这种对中间深度估计的依赖在水下尤为严重。

Method: 提出AquaBEV-Nav框架，基于CORAL分层探索框架，将其基于深度的感知前端替换为AquaBEV。给定单张RGB帧，AquaBEV将视觉特征映射到学习到的极坐标表示，沿距离维度进行因果推理，并重建局部笛卡尔占据栅格，无需中间深度预测。所得占据图累积到CORAL的持久空间记忆中，为基于VLM的高层规划提供空间上下文，并为动力学感知的局部轨迹生成提供碰撞约束。

Result: 在十种模拟珊瑚礁环境和六种占据骨干网络、单一协议下评估，AquaBEV-Nav达到37.48的结构IoU、53.2的目标IoU、88.95%的闭环覆盖率，且零碰撞。

Conclusion: AquaBEV-Nav通过直接鸟瞰占据预测避免了对水下不可靠单目深度估计的依赖，验证了该范式在水下探索中兼顾感知精度与安全性的有效性。

Abstract: Safe underwater exploration requires a robot to understand where surrounding structures are located and which regions are available for motion. Existing vision-based underwater exploration systems commonly obtain this information indirectly by estimating monocular depth, unprojecting the geometry into 3D space, and accumulating it into a 2D bird's-eye-view occupancy map. This reliance on intermediate depth estimation is particularly problematic underwater, where scattering and wavelength-dependent attenuation degrade visual cues and limit the reliability of monocular depth estimates. We introduce AquaBEV-Nav, an underwater exploration framework that bypasses explicit monocular depth estimation through direct bird's-eye-view occupancy prediction. Built upon the CORAL hierarchical exploration framework, AquaBEV-Nav replaces its depth-based perception front end with AquaBEV. Given a single RGB frame, AquaBEV maps visual features into a learned polar representation, performs causal reasoning along the range dimension, and reconstructs local Cartesian occupancy without relying on intermediate depth prediction. The resulting occupancy map is accumulated into CORAL's persistent spatial memory, providing spatial context for VLM-based high-level planning and collision constraints for dynamics-aware local trajectory generation. Across ten simulated reef environments and six occupancy backbones evaluated under a single protocol, AquaBEV-Nav reaches 37.48 structure IoU and 53.2 target IoU, 88.95% closed-loop coverage with zero collisions.

</details>


### [24] [FutureRay: Control-Aligned Future Range for Agile Quadruped Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32158)
*Tianhao Zang,Shanze Wang,Ziqian Wang,Liyou Luo,Zihan Liu,Xingjian Xie,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 FutureRay，联合预测未来距离范围与遭遇风险，结合足迹与时延-制动约束进行局部规划，在仿真中把动态避障完成率从 75%/80% 提升到 93.3%，且无需重训运动策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人执行运动指令时，移动障碍物可能堵塞原本畅通的路径。若运动选择不考虑机器人足迹、反应与制动所需时间，仅依赖当前净空信息难以安全避障，因此需要预测净空的变化。

Method: 提出 FutureRay：基于深度导出的距离历史与可观测的机器人运动，预测各观察方向在未来若干时刻的距离范围，并同时输出遭遇风险。训练强调近端净空，并对高估可用空间的误差施加惩罚。局部规划器对候选航向查询同一预测结果，与当前观测结合，围绕机器人足迹检查净空；以基于模型的反应-制动极限指导速度选择，生成的速度指令交给固定的运动控制策略执行。

Result: 在 60 个静态与动态仿真场景的配对评估中，感知、规划与运动控制均保持不变，FutureRay 完成率为 93.3%，高于当前距离持续性方法的 75.0% 和笛卡尔卡尔曼推演方法的 80.0%，且碰撞次数少于两个基线。在实体四足机器人上的定性试验显示，机器人能在障碍物逼近路线时提前避让，随后恢复朝目标前进。

Conclusion: 联合预测距离范围与遭遇风险可在不重新训练运动策略的前提下提升四足机器人的动态障碍物避让性能。

Abstract: Moving obstacles can block a previously clear route while a quadruped robot executes a motion command. We investigate whether predicting changing clearance improves navigation when motion selection accounts for the robot footprint and the time needed to react and brake. We present FutureRay, which predicts ranges across viewing directions and future times, together with encounter risk, from depth-derived range history and observable robot motion. Training emphasizes near-term clearance and penalizes errors that overstate available space. A local planner queries the same forecast for candidate headings and combines it with current observations to check clearance around the robot footprint. Model-based reaction--braking limits guide speed selection, and the resulting velocity commands are passed to a fixed locomotion policy. In paired evaluations on 60 static and dynamic simulation scenes, FutureRay achieves 93.3% completion, compared with 75.0% for current-range persistence and 80.0% for Cartesian Kalman rollout, with perception, planning, and locomotion held fixed. FutureRay also records fewer collisions than both baselines. Qualitative trials on a physical quadruped show avoidance initiated while an obstacle is approaching the route, followed by renewed goal progress. These results show that joint range and encounter-risk prediction can improve obstacle avoidance without retraining the locomotion policy.

</details>


### [25] [RoboFFT: Finetuning generative robot policy via online reinforcement learning with forward process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32236)
*Yu Li,Shenghe Hu,Yuhan Wang,Yaoxiang Pu,Haotong Zhang,Yuanpei Chen,Yaodong Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RoboFFT，一种前向过程强化学习框架，用于微调生成式机器人策略，通过前向加噪与加权score/flow matching损失构造代理策略比率以进行PPO更新，在仿真与真实任务中提升性能、稳定性与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式模型（扩散、流模型）能捕捉复杂多模态动作分布，但仅靠模仿学习的策略受限于演示不完美与分布偏移，进一步提升通常需要额外专家数据。强化学习可通过环境交互改进策略，但生成式策略的似然估计不可解析，导致微调困难。

Method: 提出RoboFFT前向过程强化学习框架。对采样动作施加前向加噪，利用加权score / flow matching损失构造代理策略比率（surrogate policy ratio），据此执行PPO风格更新，从而实现对生成式机器人策略的强化学习微调。

Result: 在主流生成式机器人策略与代表性仿真基准（长时程规划、稀疏奖励场景）上评估，RoboFFT持续提升性能，并具有更好的稳定性与训练效率；进一步集成到真实世界RL框架中，验证了实际任务的有效性。

Conclusion: RoboFFT通过前向过程RL绕过生成式策略似然不可解析的问题，为生成式机器人策略提供了有效、稳定且高效的强化学习微调方案，并具备真实场景可行性。

Abstract: Generative models, such as diffusion and flow-based models, have shown strong promise for robot policy learning by capturing complex and multimodal action distributions from demonstrations. However, policies trained solely with imitation learning often suffer from imperfect demonstrations and distributional shifts, while further improvement typically requires additional expert data. Reinforcement learning offers a natural solution through environment interaction, but effectively finetuning generative robot policies remains challenging due to the intractability of likelihood estimation. In this work, we propose RoboFFT, a forward-process reinforcement learning framework for finetuning generative robot policies, which applies forward noising to sampled actions and uses the weighted score / flow matching loss to construct a surrogate policy ratio for PPO-style updates. We evaluate RoboFFT with popular generative robot policies on representative simulation benchmarks, including long-horizon planning and sparse reward settings. Extensive experiments and analysis demonstrate that RoboFFT consistently improves performance while achieving better stability and training efficiency. We further integrate RoboFFT into a real world RL framework and demonstrate its effectiveness in real world tasks. Project website: https://student-of-holmes.github.io/RoboFFT/.

</details>


### [26] [Federated Subspace Guided Vision-Language-Action Policy Distillation for Non-IID Multi-Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32239)
*Biprodip Pal,Kaushik Roy,Yanming Zhu,Brendan Tidd,Alan Wee-Chung Liew,Peyman Moghadam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FedDRMan联邦子空间引导蒸馏框架，解决异构机器人操作中非IID分布导致的表示漂移与更新不兼容问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习可让多机器人协同改进操作策略而无需集中训练示范，但任务与环境的非IID分布会引发表示漂移和机器人策略更新互不兼容，导致简单参数聚合具有破坏性。

Method: 服务器模型作为冻结教师指导本地行为克隆；通过低秩多模态子空间蒸馏与动作分布蒸馏保留全局有用的表示几何与策略行为；按更新兼容性对客户端聚类并为每簇维护持久模型；服务器对每个兼容聚合结果进行谱重平衡，以缓解较弱任务相关更新方向的衰减。

Result: 在LIBERO上跨多种非IID设置、异构程度、客户端参与度变化进行实验，并结合消融与聚合分析，FedDRMan显著提升知识迁移，持续优于强联邦基线，峰值平均成功率达80.7%，较最强评估联邦基线高11.6个百分点。

Conclusion: FedDRMan通过子空间引导蒸馏、兼容性聚类聚合与谱重平衡有效应对异构机器人操作中的非IID问题，实现更优知识迁移与性能。

Abstract: Federated learning offers a natural way for multiple robots to jointly improve manipulation policies without requiring centralized access to training demonstrations. However, non-IID task and environment distributions can induce representation drift and mutually incompatible robot-policy updates, making naive parameter aggregation destructive. We present FedDRMan, a federated subspace-guided distillation framework for heterogeneous robot manipulation. At each communication round, the server model provides a frozen teacher for local behavior cloning, while low-rank multimodal subspace and action-distribution distillation preserve globally useful representation geometry and policy behavior. To address heterogeneous aggregation, FedDRMan groups clients by update compatibility and maintains a persistent model for each cluster. The server then spectrally rebalances each compatible aggregate to mitigate attenuation of weaker task-relevant robot-policy update directions. Extensive experiments on LIBERO across diverse non-IID settings, heterogeneity levels, client participation variation, together with ablations and aggregation analyses, show that FedDRMan substantially improves knowledge transfer and consistently outperforms strong federated baselines achieving a peak mean success rate of 80.7%, 11.6 percentage points above the strongest evaluated federated baseline.

</details>


### [27] [DS-VLA: A Dendritic-inspired Vision-Language-Action Model for Robust Action Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32253)
*Yaxing Lyu,Jingyi Li,Mingkun Xu,Yujie Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DS-VLA 将树突脉冲动力学引入 VLA 控制，通过多分支树突与神经元级抑制门提升闭环扰动下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型在标称评测下表现良好，但当执行动作出现瞬时扰动时，闭环鲁棒性不足，说明标称成功率无法代表真实闭环鲁棒行为。

Method: 提出树突启发的动作架构 DS-VLA：为动作神经元配置多个稀疏连接的树突分支，每个分支具有异质、可学习的衰减因子，以实现模块化特征处理与时间信息整合；并引入神经元级抑制门，在胞体动力学之前自适应调控新多模态证据进入树突状态，抑制不可靠状态更新并保留任务相关历史信息。

Result: 在 LIBERO 四个套件上，标称平均成功率 91.6%，扰动后平均成功率 87.35%，保留标称性能的 95.4%；相同扰动设置下 OpenVLA-OFT、FAST、π0、GR00T 分别仅为 39.45%、23.90%、28.55%、30.75%。消融实验验证了神经元级共享抑制的贡献，神经动力学与扰动后轨迹分析表明鲁棒性来自选择性证据抑制与有效行为恢复。

Conclusion: 将脑启发计算机制作为架构先验，是超越单纯扩大视觉语言骨干或生成式动作解码器、实现鲁棒具身智能的有效途径。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have achieved strong performance in language-conditioned manipulation, yet success under nominal evaluation does not necessarily translate into robust closed-loop behavior when executed actions are transiently corrupted. We introduce DS-VLA, a dendritic-inspired action architecture that incorporates dendritic spiking dynamics into VLA control to address this limitation. Specifically, to enable modularized feature processing and temporal information integration, DS-VLA equips action neurons with multiple sparsely connected dendritic branches, each featuring heterogeneous, learned decay factors. Furthermore, to suppress unreliable state updates while preserving task-relevant historical information, we introduce a neuron-wise inhibitory gate that adaptively regulates the admission of new multimodal evidence into dendritic states prior to somatic dynamics. We evaluate DS-VLA on all four LIBERO suites under both nominal rollouts and a unified closed-loop action-perturbation protocol. DS-VLA achieves a 91.6\% average nominal success rate and an 87.35\% average perturbed success rate, retaining 95.4\% of its nominal performance. Under the same reported perturbation setting, OpenVLA-OFT, FAST, $π_0$, and GR00T achieve 39.45\%, 23.90\%, 28.55\%, and 30.75\%, respectively. A controlled ablation isolates the contribution of neuron-wise shared inhibition, while analyses of neural dynamics and post-perturbation trajectories associate robust performance with selective evidence suppression and effective behavioral recovery. Together, these results demonstrate that integrating brain-inspired computational mechanisms offers a promising architectural prior for robust embodied intelligence beyond merely scaling vision-language backbones or generative action decoders.

</details>


### [28] [Affordance-Conditioned Decision Making: Bridging the Semantic-Spatial Gap in Zero-Shot Cross-Floor Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32292)
*Xuekang Yang,Lu Chen,Shuang Luo,Jialing Zhu,Qi Zhang,Yue Gao,Xiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PACE 是一种监督式局部执行模块，通过将过渡语义落地为可通行可供性位姿，并条件化短程动作生成，叠加失败感知偏好精修，提升冻结零样本语义规划器的跨楼层导航可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言导航虽依赖通用语义规划器，但在空间受限过渡场景（楼梯、门框、窄通道）中，正确的高层意图难以转化为可靠物理执行：到达过渡点不等于能通过，智能体需识别可执行的可供性位姿并从累积动作误差中恢复。现有零样本语义规划器缺乏这种执行层的对齐与闭环纠错能力。

Method: 提出 PACE（Preference-refined Affordance-Conditioned Execution）：1）将过渡相关语义落地为长时程、以智能体为中心的可通行可供性位姿；2）以该空间目标为条件进行短时程动作生成，对齐语义目标与物理执行；3）通过失败感知偏好精修进行后训练，利用 rollout 派生对（正常/恢复行为 vs. 放大偏差行为）改进闭环纠正。PACE 作为监督式局部执行模块，增强冻结的零样本语义规划器。

Result: 将 PACE 集成到六个开源零样本 VLN 导航器中，在 R2R-CE 与 RxR-CE 的跨楼层子集上均取得一致提升：平均成功率分别由 16.35% 提升至 27.65%、由 4.76% 提升至 12.06%。真实世界实验进一步验证其在未见环境中的适用性。

Conclusion: 可通行可供性提供了桥接语义意图与可靠具身行为的有效途径；PACE 证明执行层对齐与偏好精修能显著提升跨楼层导航的闭环可靠性，并具备真实环境泛化潜力。

Abstract: Vision-and-language navigation increasingly relies on general-purpose semantic planners, yet translating correct high-level intent into reliable physical execution remains difficult in spatially constrained transitions. Reaching a staircase, doorway, or narrow passage does not ensure traversal; the agent must identify an executable affordance pose and recover from accumulated action errors. We propose PACE (Preference-refined Affordance-Conditioned Execution), a supervised local execution module that augments frozen zero-shot semantic planners for reliable cross-floor navigation. PACE grounds transition-related semantics into a long-horizon, agent-centric traversable affordance pose and conditions short-horizon action generation on this spatial target, thereby aligning semantic goals with physical execution. We further post-train PACE through failure-aware preference refinement using rollout-derived pairs that contrast normal or recovery behaviors with deviation-amplifying behaviors, thereby improving closed-loop correction. We integrate PACE into six open-source zero-shot VLN navigators and demonstrate consistent improvements on the cross-floor subsets of R2R-CE and RxR-CE, increasing the average success rate from 16.35% to 27.65% and from 4.76% to 12.06%, respectively. Real-world experiments further demonstrate PACE's applicability in unseen environments, highlighting the potential of traversable affordances to bridge semantic intent and reliable embodied behavior.

</details>


### [29] [MemTransfer: Benchmarking Memory Beyond Matched Experience in Embodied Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32313)
*Haiming Tang,Xianjie Dai,Gujie Shao,Zuyi Guo,Jingguang Li,Kailang Ma,Yihong Tang,Heye Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MemTransfer 基准在冻结 VLM 策略下系统比较六种记忆表示，发现对某一类条件错配的鲁棒性并不蕴含对另一类错配的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记忆使具身智能体能够复用过去经验，但"保留有用信息"不等于"条件变化时能正确调用"。现有研究缺乏在统一策略下对多种记忆表示进行受控、多维错配评估的手段，因此需要一个新的基准来区分记忆的存储能力与决策时使用能力。

Method: 提出 MemTransfer 基准：在模拟仓库中构建 100 个导航案例、覆盖十类任务，由专家示范提供历史。在共享的冻结视觉-语言模型策略下比较六种记忆表示——一个工作记忆基线加五种过去经验表示（Full-context、Episodic、Summary 及两种轨迹记忆）。设置三类比较，分别变化起始位姿、路径可用性，以及历史的数量与任务相关性。

Result: 每任务一个示范时，Full-context 与 Episodic 在原始示范起点成功率分别为 95.3% 和 100.0%，但换到新测试起点后下降 48–49 个百分点。Summary 在两个测试起点间变化不大，但每任务四个示范时，其在阻塞路径后保留原路径成功率的比例（39.3%）低于 Working memory（44.8%）与两种轨迹记忆（56–58%）。在新测试起点，把相关示范从一增至四个，Episodic 成功率提升 14.3 个百分点，其他表示提升不超过 1.3 个百分点。将一半相关历史替换为其他任务经验会降低两种轨迹记忆的成功率。

Conclusion: 结果表明确保存什么与确保如何使用是两类独立问题：对某一类错配的鲁棒性不能外推到另一类。评估具身智能体记忆时，必须同时考察所存信息本身及其在决策时刻的调用方式。

Abstract: Memory lets an embodied agent reuse past experience, yet retaining useful information does not ensure that the agent can apply it when conditions change. We present MemTransfer, a benchmark comparing six memory representations, a working-memory baseline and five representations of past experience, under a shared frozen vision-language-model policy. It comprises 100 navigation cases across ten task types in a simulated warehouse, with expert demonstrations supplying the history. Three comparisons vary the starting pose, route availability, and amount and task relevance of history. With one demonstration per task, Full-context and Episodic memory reach 95.3% and 100.0% success at the original demonstration start, but lose 48-49 percentage points at a new test start. Summary changes little between these two test starts, yet with four demonstrations per task it retains a smaller fraction of its unchanged-route success after blocking (39.3%) than Working memory (44.8%) or the two trajectory memories (56-58%). At the new test start, increasing from one to four relevant demonstrations raises Episodic success by 14.3 percentage points, while the other evaluated representations gain no more than 1.3 percentage points. Replacing half of the relevant histories with other-task experience lowers success for both trajectory memories. These results show that robustness to one kind of mismatch does not imply robustness to another, motivating evaluation of both stored information and its use at decision time.

</details>


### [30] [WSM-Aware HRI: An IoT-Enhanced Framework for Early Detection and Norm-Guided Repair of Failures with LLM Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32336)
*Hanlin Zhang,Yuquan Wang,Tianwei Zhang,Zhenglong Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 WSM-Aware HRI 框架，将人机交互失败统一建模为人类指令隐含假设与机器人世界模型之间的“世界状态不匹配（WSM）”，并借助 LLM 在意图形成阶段主动检测不匹配，而非事后恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机交互失败是机器人落地的主要障碍。以往研究多将失败视为孤立的技术故障，或聚焦于事后恢复行为，忽视了实践中许多故障源于人机对当前世界状态假设不一致这一根本原因。

Method: 提出 IoT 增强的模块化框架 WSM-Aware HRI：将各类交互故障统一归因为世界状态不匹配。利用多模态感知与数字增强构建机器人接地世界模型；使用 LLM 将人类指令中的隐含假设显式化，映射到少量不匹配类型，并指定验证所需证据；把失败处理从执行时恢复前移到意图形成阶段的主动检测，并按安全、规范合规与多用户协调进行干预，提供可解释性。评估涵盖视觉与潜在状态不匹配的十个日常案例。

Result: 系统能准确产生预期输出结果。消融实验表明，可靠的不匹配识别依赖于恰当的接地表示与面向验证的精炼机制。

Conclusion: 将交互故障视为显式的世界状态不匹配，能够更早检测即将发生的失败，并为在 human-robot interaction 中整合外部证据与社会约束提供有原则的机制。

Abstract: Human-robot interaction (HRI) failures remain a major barrier to deploying robots in real-world environments. Prior work often treats failures as isolated technical faults or focuses on post-hoc recovery behaviors. In practice, many breakdowns arise because humans and robots operate under inconsistent assumptions about the current world state. We propose WSM-Aware HRI, an IoT-enhanced modular framework that unifies diverse HRI breakdowns as World-State Mismatches (WSMs) between a human's instruction-implied assumptions and a robot's grounded world model built from multimodal perception and digital augmentation. A Large Language Model (LLM) is used to make implicit assumptions explicit, map them to a small set of mismatch types, and specify the evidence needed for verification against the robot's world state. WSM-Aware HRI shifts failure handling from execution-time recovery to proactive mismatch detection during intention formation, enabling interventions guided by safety, norm compliance, and multi-user coordination with transparent explanations. We evaluate mismatch identification in ten everyday cases spanning both visual and latent-state mismatches. The system can accurately produce the expected output results, and ablations show that reliable identification depends on appropriate grounding representations and verification-oriented refinement. These results indicate that treating interaction breakdowns as explicit world-state mismatches enables earlier detection of impending failures and offers a principled mechanism for integrating external evidence and social constraints into human-robot interaction.

</details>


### [31] [Human Motion Prediction for Human-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32346)
*Placido Falqueto,Elena Basei,Edoardo Lamon,Giovanni Perantoni,Matteo Saveriano,Daniele Fontanelli,Luigi Palopoli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对比了基于GMM聚类、TransFusion与Graph-Mixer三种人体运动预测方法，评估其在人机协作中的有效性，以服务于机器人运动规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人机协作的动态环境中，机器人需提前预判人的运动以提升安全性与效率，因此有必要系统比较现有人体运动预测方法的适用性。

Method: 选取基于高斯混合模型（GMM）的聚类方法、TransFusion（基于Transformer的融合预测）以及Graph-Mixer（基于图结构的混合网络）三类人体运动预测方法，进行对比评估。

Result: 摘要未给出具体的量化指标或对比结论，仅说明对三种方法在人机协作场景下的效能进行了评估。

Conclusion: 人体运动预测有助于机器人提前感知人的动作，从而优化运动规划并在动态环境中提升安全性与作业效率；具体方法的优劣仍需依据后续实验数据判断。

Abstract: This paper compares three human motion prediction methods - GMM-based clustering, TransFusion, and Graph-Mixer - evaluating their effectiveness in human-robot collaboration. These predictions are valuable for improving robot motion planning by anticipating human movements and enhancing safety and efficiency in dynamic environments.

</details>


### [32] [Proactive Motion Planning for Human-Robot Cooperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32354)
*Elena Basei,Edoardo Lamon,Matteo Saveriano,Daniele Fontanelli,Luigi Palopoli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将基于图深度学习的行人运动预测集成到主动式人机共融运动规划框架中，利用静态路网图与时间变化A*算法实时调整UR5e机械臂轨迹，实现主动避碰并显著改善人机交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向人机安全协作需求，传统被动或反应式避障规划在人机共享工作空间中响应滞后、效率低。作者希望把前瞻性的人类运动预测引入动态、主动的人体感知运动规划，使机器人能提前预判人类意图并主动规避冲突。

Method: 采用基于图的深度学习模型预测人类未来运动；将预测结果嵌入运动规划框架，框架基于静态路网图（roadmap），结合时间变化的A*算法（time-variant A*）在时间维度上对UR5e机械臂的轨迹进行修正与重规划，形成预测—规划的闭环。

Result: 将精确的运动预测与自适应轨迹规划相结合，显著提升了人机交互质量，并实现了主动式碰撞避免（proactive collision avoidance），使机械臂能提前调整轨迹以规避潜在冲突。

Conclusion: 把图深度学习的人体运动预测与时间变化A*规划统一在同一框架中，是提升人机协作安全性、实现主动避碰的有效途径；该方法在UR5e平台上验证了可行性。

Abstract: This abstract addresses the incorporation of human motion prediction into proactive and dynamic human-aware motion planning, with the goal of enabling safe collaboration between humans and robots. A deep learning, graph-based model is used to forecast human motion and is integrated into a planning framework. This framework employs a static roadmap along with a time-variant A* algorithm to modify the trajectory of a UR5e manipulator. This method greatly improves human-robot interaction and enables proactive collision avoidance by combining precise motion forecasts with adaptive trajectory planning.

</details>


### [33] [RE-0: Verified Recursive Improvement of Embodied Code-as-Policy Agents through Local On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32416)
*Jiawei Zhang,Xiangrong Zhang,Rui Song,Huanbin Zhou,Chengye Song,Hongzhou Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 RE-0 与 RE-OPD 框架，通过反事实验证教师干预来改进 Code-as-Policy 智能体，实现师生协同进化并具有理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Code-as-Policy 智能体可完成长程具身任务，但教师模型虽更强却并非全局可靠。现有蒸馏方法将教师完整行为作为监督目标，会在教师失败的轨迹上错误分配训练信用，导致监督不可靠。

Method: RE-0：不假设教师全局优于学生，而是在学生自身失败历史处请求教师的局部纠正，并在环境中验证纠正是否真正有益，经验证的纠正带来即时改进。RE-OPD：将验证过的干预转为 on-policy 蒸馏的监督信号，仅对反事实验证通过的教师干预提供分布级监督，权重为其测得的局部收益，并将改进投影回独立学生模型，使监督位置与教师信用的分配随学生策略共同演化。

Result: 理论上证明了学生每轮收益以经验证干预收益为下界（至多相差验证与投影误差项）。在多个 Code-as-Policy 具身任务上的实验表明，RE-0 同时提升了教师辅助执行与独立学生策略的能力，并可泛化到新机器人平台与新场景。

Conclusion: 通过局部纠正验证与反事实监督分配，可在教师非全局可靠的情况下实现持续的策略改进，兼顾理论保证与跨具身泛化。

Abstract: Code-as-Policy agents accomplish long-horizon embodied tasks by generating and executing code, yet continually improving them with teachers that are stronger but not globally reliable remains a key challenge. Existing distillation methods typically treat the teacher's complete behavior as the supervision target and thus misassign training credit on states where the teacher fails. We propose RE-0, a recursively verified policy improvement framework: rather than assuming that the teacher globally outperforms the student, RE-0 requests local corrections from the teacher on the student's own failure histories and checks in the environment whether each correction is genuinely beneficial; verified corrections yield immediate improvement. Building on this, we propose RE-OPD, which turns verified interventions into supervision for on-policy distillation. Only counterfactually verified teacher interventions provide distribution-level supervision, weighted by their measured local benefit, and the improvement they induce is projected back into the standalone student, so both where supervision is applied and how much credit the teacher receives co-evolve with the student policy. We further prove that the student's per-round gain is lower-bounded by its verified intervention gain up to verification and projection error terms. Experiments across multiple Code-as-Policy embodied tasks show that RE-0 improves both teacher-assisted execution and the standalone student, and generalizes to novel robots and scenes.

</details>


### [34] [DRAM: Delta-rule Recurrent Associative Memory for Robot Manipulation Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32453)
*Xinyu Zhao,Yixiang Shan,Tao Yang,Runyu Lei,Yiming Zhao,Jiaxin Fan,Zongbao Feng,Peng Jia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DRAM——一种可插拔的固定大小循环联想记忆模块，为预训练机器人策略赋予长时程记忆，无需修改架构或重训主干。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作本质上是历史依赖的，但多数预训练策略仅以当前观测或短时窗口为条件。已有长时记忆方案存在缺陷：多帧观测窗口显著提高推理成本；预定义语义特征限制任务通用性，且可能需重训主干才能适配记忆。

Method: DRAM采用门控delta-rule线性注意力维护固定大小联想记忆，其更新方式被修改为并行纳入每帧内所有token；并设计架构无关的读出机制，将历史上下文注入不同策略架构的动作预测。训练时冻结主干，仅对记忆模块与动作专家做任务特定的后训练（post-training）。

Result: 实验表明，DRAM在冻结的预训练策略上稳定优于短上下文基线及替代性的紧凑记忆设计，验证其作为固定大小、后置式（post-hoc）记忆模块的有效性。

Conclusion: DRAM可作为即插即用模块为多种预训练机器人策略赋予长时程记忆，兼具固定内存开销、无需架构修改与主干重训的优势，是一种有效的后置记忆方案。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation is inherently history-dependent, yet most pretrained robotic policies condition on only the current observation or a short temporal window. Equipping such policies with long-term memory remains challenging: existing approaches either feed the backbone multi-frame observation windows, which substantially increase inference cost, or rely on pre-defined semantic features, which limit task generality and may also require the retraining of the backbone to adapt to the memory. We introduce DRAM (Delta-rule Recurrent Associative Memory), a plug-and-play memory module that can be attached to a wide range of pretrained robotic policies, endowing them with long-horizon memory without architectural modification or backbone retraining, requiring only task-specific post-training of the memory module and action expert. DRAM maintains a fixed-size associative memory using gated delta-rule linear attention, with a modified update that incorporates all tokens within each frame in parallel. An architecture-agnostic readout integrates historical context into action prediction across different policy architectures. Experiments show that DRAM consistently improves frozen pretrained policies over short-context baselines and alternative compact memory designs, validating its effectiveness as a fixed-size, post-hoc memory module trained with the backbone frozen.

</details>


### [35] [GlowTact: Simple and Compact Vision-Based Tactile Sensing with High Sensitivity and Spatial Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32471)
*Yuxiang Ma,Megha Tippur,Pengfei Ye,Sandra Q. Liu,Haonan Chen,Francis Richard Cottrell,Edward Adelson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GlowTact是一种压力响应型视觉触觉传感机制，通过单色非定向照明直接将接触压力可视化为原始触觉图像，无需几何重建，实现紧凑、可定制、高灵敏度的触觉感知，适用于人形指尖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉触觉传感器（VBTS）设计复杂，难以简化并适配人形指尖的尺寸与约束条件，需要一种结构简单、体积紧凑但信息丰富的触觉传感方案。

Method: 提出GlowTact压力响应型视觉触觉传感机制：仅需单色、非定向照明，原始触觉图像直接表征压力分布，无需显式几何重建；采用耐用丁腈膜与简单光学设计，实现紧凑且可定制的传感器结构。

Result: 实现克级接触检测、精确法向力估计以及精细接触几何重建（包括M1螺纹），同时保持高灵敏度与丰富空间细节。

Conclusion: GlowTact是一种面向紧凑人形指尖的实用新型传感技术，将耐用丁腈膜和简单光学设计与灵敏且信息丰富的触觉感知相结合。

Abstract: Vision-based tactile sensors (VBTS) provide rich contact information for robotic manipulation, but existing designs can be hard to simplify and adapt to the size and constraints of humanoid fingertips. We introduce \textbf{GlowTact}, a pressure-responsive vision-based tactile sensing mechanism that directly visualizes contact pressure. \textbf{GlowTact} requires only single-color, non-directional illumination, and the raw tactile image directly represents the pressure distribution without explicit geometry reconstruction. This simple sensing principle enables compact, customizable tactile sensors while preserving high sensitivity and rich spatial detail. We demonstrate gram-scale contact detection, accurate normal-force estimation, and reconstruction of fine contact geometry, including M1 screw threads. These results establish \textbf{GlowTact} as a practical new sensing technology for compact humanoid fingertips, combining a durable nitrile membrane and simple optical design with sensitive and information-rich tactile perception.

</details>


### [36] [Assisting for Open-Ended Tasks: Goal-Oriented Shared Autonomy as a Particle Filter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32576)
*Mengxue Fu,Ethan Xu,Sam Iyer-Singh,Yinlong Dai,Michael Hagenow,Dylan P. Losey*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于粒子滤波的开放式目标共享自主框架，利用基础模型动态生成候选语义目标并实时更新信念，通过用户实验验证其能减少遥操作时间并提升满意度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共享自主方法大多假设候选目标集合是先验已知且静态的，这限制了其在非结构化辅助场景中的适用性。论文旨在解决开放式、动态变化目标下的共享自主问题。

Method: 将目标导向的共享自主建模为粒子滤波，粒子表示候选人类目标。与人固定目标集不同，其转移模型随交互演化动态提出新候选目标，人类动作实时更新目标信念。框架结合基础模型（如视觉 grounding 与大语言模型）来提出上下文相关的语义目标、由低层技能原语生成目标条件辅助，并从人类纠正中优化技能。

Result: 通过一项包含12名参与者的用户研究，在多种桌面操作任务上将该方法与当前最优共享自主基线进行对比。结果表明该方法减少了用户遥操作系统的时间并提高了用户满意度。

Conclusion: 基于粒子滤波并结合基础模型的共享自主框架能够有效支持开放式、动态变化的目标，在非结构化辅助场景中提升交互效率与用户体验。

Abstract: A common approach for shared autonomy blends human inputs with autonomous assistance based on the human's likely goal. However, most existing approaches assume that a static set of possible goals is known a priori, which limits the use of such methods in unstructured assistive settings. We instead investigate how to enable shared autonomy with open-ended and dynamically changing goals. We formulate goal-oriented shared autonomy as a particle filter in which particles represent candidate human goals. Unlike conventional approaches with a fixed goal set, our transition model dynamically proposes new candidate goals as the interaction evolves, and human actions update the belief over these goals in real time. We instantiate this framework with foundation models (e.g., vision grounding and large language models) that propose context-relevant semantic goals, generate goal-conditioned assistance from low-level skill primitives, and refine those skills from human corrections. We assess our approach through a user study where 12 participants perform a variety of tabletop manipulation tasks with our method and state-of-the-art shared autonomy baselines. The results show that our particle filter-based approach reduces the amount of time users spend teleoperating the system and improves user satisfaction. User study videos: https://youtu.be/Ii26XuRqm9c

</details>


### [37] [Think Fast, Plan Selectively: Adaptive Deliberation for Efficient Data-Driven MPC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32591)
*Yi Xian Goh,Sze Jue Yang,Hao Luan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Fast-TD-MPC，一种在快速策略执行与测试时规划之间自适应路由的轻量框架，性能可比，推理最高快约 4 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动 MPC 每步需采样并评估数百条候选轨迹，计算开销大，导致控制频率远低于实时机器人需求。受人类认知双过程理论启发（System 1 快速直觉、System 2 慢速审慎），作者质疑是否每次决策都需要同等程度的计算审慎。

Method: Fast-TD-MPC 采用轻量框架，在快速策略执行与测试时规划之间进行自适应路由，仅在最需要的状态上保留高开销的审慎规划。

Result: 在 103 个连续控制任务上取得有竞争力的任务性能，同时推理速度最高提升约 4 倍；在外部扰动下，Fast-TD-MPC 选择性地回退到规划，鲁棒性与原规划器相当。

Conclusion: 并非所有决策都需要同等计算量；通过双过程式的自适应路由，可在基本不损失性能与鲁棒性的前提下显著降低 MPC 推理成本。
