Abstract: Data-driven model predictive control (MPC) combines learned world models with online trajectory optimization, achieving strong performance in continuous control. However, the per-step cost of sampling and evaluating hundreds of candidate trajectories restricts deployment to control frequencies well below what real-time robotics demands. Motivated by the dual-process theory of human cognition, which distinguishes between fast, intuitive processing (System 1) and slower, deliberative reasoning (System 2), we ask whether every decision requires the same degree of computational deliberation. We propose Fast-TD-MPC, a lightweight framework that adaptively routes between fast policy execution and test-time planning, reserving costly deliberation for states where it is most needed. Fast-TD-MPC delivers competitive task performance across 103 continuous control tasks while achieving up to ~4x faster inference. Under external disturbances, Fast-TD-MPC selectively falls back to planning, maintaining robustness comparable to the original planner.

</details>


### [38] [RECAST: Recasting Vision-Language Semantics into an Actionable Cost Map for Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32595)
*Incheol Cho,Jintae Park,Jinkyu Kim,Jungbeom Lee,Jaegul Choo,Seokha Moon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RECAST：融合VLM推理与视觉基础模型空间定位，构建可通行性/风险/朝向/间隙四类判断的可行动成本图，用于机器人导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全鲁棒的机器人导航需要高层场景理解与稳定运动的结合。学习型模型在训练分布外失效；VLM方法虽具理解力，却难以将理解落地到场景中，也无法与动作对齐。

Method: 给定机器人前视图与用户指令：先用VLM分解场景，判断哪些表面可通行、哪些物体有风险、偏好哪个朝向、哪些间隙可通过；再由视觉基础模型在图像中定位这些表面与物体；将四类判断空间化重投射为一张紧凑成本图。该图既作为轨迹解码器的条件，又用于对其候选轨迹打分以选择执行方案。因VLM回答滞后于实时场景，两步骤均使用两个时刻的成本图：VLM所判断的枢轴帧（含全部四类判断）与当前帧（其地形与碰撞成本由当前图像重建）。

Result: 相较最强先前方法，仿真中成功率提升13.3个百分点，真实四足机器人上提升31.4个百分点；碰撞率分别降低9.6与14.3个百分点，达到所有方法中最低碰撞率。

Conclusion: 将VLM的语义推理与视觉基础模型的空间定位结合为统一的成本图，可有效弥合高层理解与底层运动之间的鸿沟，并在仿真与真实平台上同时提升导航成功率与安全性。

Abstract: Safe and robust robot navigation across diverse environments requires a high-level understanding of complex scenes and the ability to carry it into stable motion. Recent works tackle this with learning-based models trained at scale and with approaches built on vision-language models (VLMs). However, learning-based models break down outside their training distribution, while VLM-based approaches bring that understanding but rarely ground it in the scene or align the action with it. To address these limitations, we present RECAST, a robot navigation framework that combines the reasoning of a VLM with the spatial grounding of vision foundation models to build an Actionable Cost map. Given the robot's front view and the user's instruction, we first decompose the scene with the VLM, judging which surfaces are traversable, which objects pose a risk, which heading to prefer, and which gaps are passable. Vision foundation models then ground these surfaces and objects in the image, and all four judgments are spatially recast into one compact cost map. This map both conditions the trajectory decoders and scores their proposals to select the one to execute. As the VLM's answers trail the live scene, both steps draw on cost maps from two points in time: the pivot frame the VLM judged, which carries all four judgments, and the current frame, whose terrain and collision costs are rebuilt from the current image. RECAST improves success over the strongest prior method by 13.3 points in simulation and 31.4 points on a real quadruped, and reduces the collision rate relative to it by 9.6 and 14.3 points, reaching the lowest collision rate among all methods. The project page is available at https://recast-nav.github.io/

</details>


### [39] [World SLAM Model: Joint World Modeling for SLAM and Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32626)
*Minghui Qin,Yijun Yuan,Weicheng Zheng,Kenan Li,Weibang Wang,Chang Sun,Junhao Huang,Anmin Liu,Yicheng Yao,Hang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出World SLAM Model（WSM），将SLAM范式从上游模块内化为导航系统的核心机制，通过增量状态更新、持久记忆与后端优化维持一致的世界状态，联合预测未来视觉状态及其相机运动与稠密几何，实现闭环导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法仅将SLAM视为提供位姿、地图或token的上游模块，未能利用其状态维护与误差优化机制来支撑长时程导航；作者希望把SLAM的核心机制直接引入下游导航，以维持交互过程中的一致性世界状态。

Method: WSM采用SLAM核心机制（含持久记忆的增量状态更新与累积误差的后端优化）；给定当前观测与导航目标，联合预测未来视觉状态并估计其相机运动与稠密几何，将视觉预测锚定在演化的空间世界状态上；该状态随新观测持续更新，作为动作生成与闭环导航的基础；模型以导航—SLAM联合目标端到端训练。

Result: 实验表明导航性能得到提升，同时保持较强的SLAM精度。

Conclusion: SLAM可作为长时程世界建模与具身交互的内在机制，WSM验证了将SLAM范式融入导航以直接受益于状态维护与优化的可行性。

Abstract: We introduce World SLAM Model (WSM), a unified framework that brings the SLAM paradigm directly into downstream navigation. Rather than treating SLAM merely as an upstream module that provides poses, maps or tokens, WSM adopts its core mechanisms, including incremental state updates with persistent memory and backend refinement of accumulated errors, to maintain a consistent world state during interaction. Given the current observation and a navigation goal, WSM predicts future visual states and jointly estimates their camera motion and dense geometry, grounding visual prediction in an evolving spatial world state. This spatial state is continuously updated as new observations arrive and provides the basis for action generation and closed-loop navigation. WSM is trained end-to-end with a joint navigation--SLAM objective, enabling downstream navigation to benefit directly from SLAM-style state maintenance and refinement while preserving accurate geometric estimation. Experiments demonstrate improved navigation performance together with strong SLAM accuracy, highlighting the potential of SLAM as an intrinsic mechanism for long-horizon world modeling and embodied interaction.

</details>


### [40] [PF-RL: Progress Field Reinforcement Learning via Goal-Conditioned Value Geometry for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32634)
*Yunpeng Qing,Yilun Kong,Sixu Lin,Ming Zhou,Yiming Fei,Shuang Luo,Yixiao Chi,Haoming Gu,Jingyuan Liu,Changxu Wei,Zhi Hou,Changqing Zou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Progress Field Reinforcement Learning (PF-RL)，在预训练VLA特征上学习结构化的目标条件进度表征，并将其转化为稠密信用用于策略优化，在LIBERO、RoboTwin2.0及真实双臂任务上稳定超越SFT、RFT与进度感知基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化微调（RFT）虽能提升VLA策略，但任务级稀疏奖励难以为中间转移提供信用，长时程操作尤甚。已有的进度感知方法多将进度建模为显式标量预测，缺乏刻画中间观测与任务目标关系的结构，限制了转移级的有效信用分配。

Method: PF-RL在预训练VLA特征上学习目标条件进度表征并转为稠密信用。采用轻量共享的Progress Field头，将当前与目标表征映射到紧凑进度空间，以几何距离诱导目标条件价值；辅以时序目标与目标结构目标塑造所学几何；转移级价值变化自然产生稠密进度优势，用于离线策略改进与在线强化微调。

Result: 在LIBERO、RoboTwin2.0以及真实世界双臂操作任务上的大量实验表明，PF-RL在策略性能上持续优于强监督微调、强化微调以及进度感知基线。

Conclusion: 将进度建模为结构化的目标条件表征场（而非标量预测）可提供转移级稠密信用，是提升VLA策略在长时程操作中离线改进与在线微调效果的有效范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement Fine-Tuning~(RFT) has emerged as a promising paradigm for improving Vision-Language-Action~(VLA) policies, yet sparse task-level outcomes provide limited credit for intermediate transitions, especially in long-horizon manipulation. A natural approach is to model intermediate task progress and use it as dense feedback for policy improvement. Despite their architectural differences, existing progress-aware methods commonly formulate task progress as an explicit scalar prediction, providing limited structure for modeling how intermediate observations relate to the task goal, which may hinder effective transition-level credit assignment. We introduce Progress Field Reinforcement Learning (PF-RL), which learns a structured goal-conditioned progress representation over pretrained VLA features and converts it into dense credit for policy optimization. A lightweight shared Progress Field head maps current and goal representations into a compact progress space, where geometric distance induces goal-conditioned value, while complementary temporal and goal-structure objectives shape the learned geometry. Transition-level value changes naturally yield dense progress advantages, enabling fine-grained credit assignment for both offline policy improvement and online reinforcement fine-tuning. Extensive experiments on LIBERO, RoboTwin2.0, and real-world bimanual manipulation tasks show that PF-RL consistently improves policy performance over strong supervised fine-tuning, reinforcement fine-tuning, and progress-aware baselines.

</details>


### [41] [SEES: A Self-Evolving Embodied System via Failure-Guided VLA Policy Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32698)
*Ziwen Li,Hanlue Zhang,Zhenyang Ren,Tianyu Huang,Runqi Lin,Haoyu Wang,Zhengqing Gao,Yandong Guo,Fakhri Karray,Tongliang Liu,Chris Russell,Mingming Gong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出自演化具身系统SEES，利用失败自动识别瓶颈技能，通过仿真RL更新共享适配器，无需新增专家演示即可持续提升VLA策略的长程任务表现并泛化到未见任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA策略在短程任务上泛化良好，但在长程任务上不可靠，原因之一是训练数据偏向演示成本较低的单阶段操作任务，导致某些弱原子技能在多阶段任务中反复引发失败。已有改进方法依赖专家定位瓶颈并提供额外演示，成本高且部署后难以实施。

Method: SEES将长程任务分解为原子任务并路由到相应的族策略，每个族由共享同一VLA适配器的相关原子技能组成。执行时自动监测原子任务结果，将失败最频繁的原子技能识别为当前瓶颈。针对瓶颈，SEES在仿真中重建此前遇到的状态并借助LLM生成任务专属成功判据，构建定制RL任务；通过在线RL更新共享族适配器，促进相关原子技能间的正迁移与跨演化轮次的累积提升。

Result: 大量实验表明，SEES可结合不同VLA骨干网络，逐步提升其长程任务性能；同时在未见任务上也观察到持续改进，说明存在超出演化设定范围的迁移能力。

Conclusion: SEES提供了一种无需新增专家演示、从失败中自我演化的VLA改进范式，通过瓶颈定位与仿真在线RL实现族内正迁移和长程性能的累积提升，并具备向未见任务泛化的潜力。

Abstract: Recent vision-language-action (VLA) policies demonstrate promising generalization across diverse short-horizon tasks. However, they remain unreliable on long-horizon tasks, partly because the large-scale training data is biased toward single-stage manipulation tasks that are cheaper to demonstrate. A single weak atomic skill can cause failures across multiple multi-stage tasks. To address such failures, existing methods often require experts to identify the bottleneck and provide additional demonstrations, making the improvement costly and potentially impractical after deployment. To this end, we present a Self-Evolving Embodied System (SEES) that learns from failures and improves the VLA policy without additional expert demonstrations. SEES decomposes long-horizon tasks into atomic tasks and routes them to corresponding family policies. Each family consists of related atomic skills that share one VLA adapter. During execution, the system automatically monitors atomic-task outcomes to identify the most frequently failing atomic skills as the current bottlenecks. To overcome these bottlenecks, SEES constructs tailored RL tasks in simulation by restoring previously encountered states and generating task-specific success criteria with an LLM. Online RL updates the shared family adapters to promote positive transfer among related atomic skills and cumulative improvement across evolution rounds. Extensive experiments show that SEES can be integrated with different VLA backbones to progressively improve their long-horizon performance. We also observe continued improvement on unseen tasks, providing evidence of transfer beyond the evolution settings.

</details>


### [42] [MORPH: Self-Organising Multi-Robot Task Allocation via Neuroplasticity-Inspired Adaptive Topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32745)
*Xuezhi Niu,Didem Gürdür Broo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MORPH: 一种无需训练的多机器人任务分配框架，通过4条局部可塑性规则在线学习有向配对偏好矩阵，在动态环境中实现高效、鲁棒的分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态环境下的多机器人任务分配（MRTA）存在根本矛盾：有效协调需要学习到的结构，但该结构必须随条件变化而适应。现有方法依赖先验任务知识、效用函数、代价矩阵或已训练策略，一旦缺少这些知识或任务分布发生偏移就变得脆弱。

Method: 提出MORPH（Multi-agent Online Rewiring through Plasticity-guided Hierarchy），一种无需训练的MRTA框架。全局分配质量由4条局部可塑性规则（突触、稳态、结构、元可塑性）涌现，作用于基于运行时共现与任务完成反馈更新的有向配对偏好矩阵。无需任务模型、无需竞价计算、无需离线训练；响应决策基于学习到的AGV-拣选员偏好，而非固定邻近规则。

Result: 在TA-RWARE仓库基准（8-24个智能体、4张地图、每回合800步、5个随机种子）上，N=24时达到全连接吞吐量的110%，仅使用21%的可能协调链路，且效率优势随集群规模单调增长。空间任务分布偏移下，性能退化比邻近方法少3倍，且学到的偏好与曼哈顿距离不相关。系统性消融证实4条可塑性规则均有可测量贡献。

Conclusion: 无需编程即涌现两种分配特性：跨类型偏好主导与渐进式偏好稀疏化，类比生物神经回路的发展性精炼。所学偏好由任务共现历史驱动，而非空间邻近。在Gerkey-Mataric分类中，MORPH是单任务、单机器人、瞬时分配类别中首个在线学习有向配对分配偏好的方法。

Abstract: Multi-robot task allocation (MRTA) in dynamic environments faces a fundamental tension: effective coordination requires learned structure, but that structure must adapt when conditions change. Existing methods resolve this by assuming prior task knowledge, a utility function, a cost matrix, or a trained policy making them brittle when deployed without such knowledge or when task distributions shift. We present MORPH(Multi-agent Online Rewiring through Plasticity-guided Hierarchy), a training-free MRTA framework where global allocation quality emerges from 4 local plasticity rules (synaptic, homeostatic, structural, and metaplasticity) applied to a directed pairwise preference matrix updated from runtime co-occurrence and task-completion feedback. MORPH requires no task model, no bid computation, and no offline training; response decisions use learned AGV-to-Picker preferences rather than a fixed proximity rule. Within the Gerkey-Mataric MRTA taxonomy, MORPH is the first method in the single-task, single-robot, instantaneous-assignment class to learn directed pairwise allocation preferences online. Evaluated on the TA-RWARE warehouse benchmark (8-24 agents, 4 maps, 800 steps per episode, 5 seeds), MORPH achieves 110% of all-to-all throughput at N=24 while using only 21% of possible coordination links as an efficiency advantage that grows monotonically with fleet size. Under spatial task distribution shift, MORPH degrades 3x less than proximity-based methods while its learned preferences remain uncorrelated with Manhattan distance. Systematic ablation confirms all four plasticity rules contribute measurably. Two allocation properties emerge without programming: cross-type preference dominance and progressive preference sparsification, mirroring the developmental refinement of biological neural circuits. Learned preferences are driven by task co-occurrence history, not spatial proximity.

</details>


### [43] [An Empirical Study on What Matters for Viewpoint-Generalizable Policies in Visual Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32762)
*Mino Nakura,Sriram Krishna,Yufei Wang,Shubham Tulsiani,Zackory Erickson,David Held*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过受控实证研究发现，保留稠密视觉token并让动作头参与几何推理，可显著提升视觉运动策略的视角泛化能力，并实现从仿真到真实世界的零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉模仿学习在机器人操作任务中应用广泛，但现有策略对视角扰动十分脆弱，难以在多样化环境中部署。因此需要明确哪些设计选择能够提升视角泛化能力。

Method: 开展受控的实证研究（controlled empirical study），系统考察不同设计决策对视角泛化的影响。关键设计包括：保留稠密视觉token（dense visual tokens），以及让动作头参与几何推理（action head participates in geometric reasoning）。在多视角仿真任务套件上进行评测。

Result: 在上述设计选择下，策略在覆盖广泛相机姿态的仿真任务中保持稳定性能；并且该策略在随机相机配置下，能够从仿真环境零样本迁移（zero-shot transfer）到真实世界。

Conclusion: 稠密视觉token与动作头几何推理是提升视觉运动策略视角泛化的重要设计要素，二者结合不仅增强仿真中的视角鲁棒性，还带来仿真到真实的零样本迁移能力。

Abstract: Visual imitation learning is a promising approach to training robot manipulation policies capable of completing a wide variety of tasks. However, policies today remain brittle to viewpoint perturbations, making deployment in diverse environments a challenge. We present a controlled empirical study of which design choices allow visuomotor policies to generalize across viewpoints. We find that viewpoint generalization improves when dense visual tokens are retained and the action head participates in geometric reasoning. On a suite of simulated tasks that span a wide range of camera poses, we show that these design choices yield a policy that remains performant across viewpoints. As a practical consequence, a policy trained with these design choices also transfers zero-shot from simulation to the real world under random camera configurations.

</details>


### [44] [CLAP: Closed-Loop Alignment with Pressure for Precise Suction Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32767)
*Yixian Zou,Chongyang Xu,Yuling Xin,Ziliang Feng,Fanman Meng,Shuaicheng Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CLAP：将真空压力信号接入VLA策略，使吸附状态可观测并支持执行中中断重推理，实现高精度堆叠与码垛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 堆叠与码垛对放置精度要求极高，单层误差会被上层继承，且平整垫面无几何特征可纠正错误位姿；自上而下吸盘适合密集排布，但现有VLA工作均未报告以实测真空信号为条件、或据其中途放弃动作的策略。视觉在此失效，因为关键时刻杯体与被吸附面相互遮挡。

Method: CLAP在真空管路中接入压力模块，使吸附状态可观测。解码读数替代策略本体感知中的吸盘指令，与视觉特征融合，并用于终止开环执行窗口，使策略基于新观测重新推理。数据方面，在真实机器人上录制目标态拆解过程，离线反转关节状态序列，无需仿真回放；针对性阶段示范仅占训练帧的8.3%，覆盖吸附状态切换与抓取中断后的构型。

Result: 在真实Unitree Z1上，单个多任务检查点在两种颜色设置下平均成功率达96.67%，分别高出最强基线16.67与10.00个百分点；单色四块堆叠平均误差15.92 mm。四个消融设置分别落后5.00至13.33个百分点。

Conclusion: 将实测真空压力信号引入VLA策略的本体感知并用于中断开环执行，可显著提升真实机器人堆叠码垛的成功率与放置精度，且对颜色变化保持鲁棒。代码与训练权重将开源。

Abstract: Stacking and palletising demand precise placement: error left in one layer is inherited by the next, and a flat pad offers no feature to funnel a wrong pose into the right one. Top-down suction suits such dense arrangements, and suction has already been brought into vision-language-action (VLA) policies. What that work does not report, however, is a policy conditioned on a measured vacuum signal, or one that uses it to abandon an action already under way. Vision does not settle the question here, because at the moment it matters the cup and the face it holds occlude each other. We present CLAP, which makes the attachment state observable through a pressure module tapped into the vacuum line. The decoded reading replaces the suction command in the policy's proprioception, is fused with the visual features, and terminates the open-loop execution window so that the policy re-infers from a fresh observation. For data, we record goal-state disassembly on the physical robot and reverse the joint-state sequence offline, without a simulation replay. Targeted phase demonstrations, 8.3% of the training frames, cover the suction transitions and the configurations an interrupted grasp leaves behind. On a real Unitree Z1, one multi-task checkpoint reaches 96.67% average success in both colour settings, 16.67 and 10.00 points above the strongest baseline, its monochromatic four-block successes averaging 15.92 mm of error. Four ablation settings fall 5.00 to 13.33 points short. We will release code and trained weights.

</details>


### [45] [Copper-Policy: Focus on the Representation for Robust Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32779)
*Zexin Feng,Yixu Feng,Lingyu Xiao,Shang Su,Kexin Zheng,Chang Xu,Mengkai Shi,Shuo Feng,Xintao Yan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Copper-Policy 通过与策略联合学习紧凑的世界表示（预测未来观测嵌入而非重建像素），兼顾控制性能与训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型通过建模未来场景演化获取行为先验，但在像素或潜空间预测细节成本高昂。近期证据显示无需测试时生成也能保持协同训练收益，这引出一个问题：WAM 究竟需要学习什么才能改进控制？本文据此提出不依赖预定义目标空间的紧凑世界表示学习。

Method: 提出 Copper-Policy：通过时间联合嵌入预测，在任务意图条件下预测未来观测嵌入，而不重建像素；该预测与动作解码联合塑造表示，同时策略保留对当前帧空间细节的访问。

Result: 表示分析显示所学特征能更好区分任务驱动变化与扰动，并为控制提供互补信息；紧凑预测目标减少每样本训练 token，使 2B 参数模型在 8×RTX 5090 上 9.67 小时完成训练，在匹配 A100 上比 Fast-WAM 快 6 倍。在 RoboTwin 上无具身预训练即超越所有对比方法，在 LIBERO-Plus 上达 80.85%，超过若干具身预训练 VLA；在三个真实机器人任务上表现与 π0.5 相当且平均分更高。

Conclusion: Copper-Policy 表明，将紧凑的世界表示学习与策略联合进行，可以在不重建像素、不依赖预定义目标空间的情况下，兼得强控制性能与高效训练。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) acquire behavioral priors by modeling future scene evolution, but predicting detailed futures in pixel or latent space incurs substantial cost. Recent evidence that co-training gains persist without test-time generation raises a question: what must a WAM learn to improve control? We introduce Copper-Policy, which learns a compact World representation with the policy rather than relying on a predefined target space. Through temporal joint-embedding prediction, it predicts future observation embeddings conditioned on task intention without reconstructing pixels. This prediction and action decoding shape the representation jointly, while the policy retains access to current-frame spatial detail for execution. Representation analyses show that the learned features better separate task-driven change from perturbations and provide complementary information for control. Compact prediction targets reduce training tokens per sample, enabling a 2B-parameter model trained in 9.67 hours on 8$\times$ RTX 5090 GPUs and 6$\times$ faster than Fast-WAM on matched A100 GPUs. Copper-Policy outperforms every compared method without embodied pretraining on RoboTwin and several embodied-pretrained VLAs on LIBERO-Plus (80.85%). On three challenging real-robot tasks, it performs comparably to $π_{0.5}$ and attains a higher average score. Together, these results show that Copper-Policy combines strong control performance with efficient training.

</details>


### [46] [CollisionGAT: Controller-Agnostic One-Step Collision Screening for Multi-Agent Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32783)
*Alan Debbas,Edwin Meriaux,Gregory Dudek*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 CollisionGAT，一种图注意力网络，输入移动智能体当前与提议状态及邻近静态障碍物，输出每个智能体的碰撞风险分数，供控制器接受、修复、重规划或推迟提议步，并在连续路径跟随控制器与 GATeD 规划器上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人团队移动前需检查提议步与其它机器人和障碍物的碰撞；随着智能体数量增加，精确几何碰撞检测代价高，需要可学习的风险预测以支持实时决策。

Method: CollisionGAT 为图注意力网络，将移动智能体与局部相关静态障碍物构图，读取当前与提议状态，输出每个移动智能体的碰撞风险分数；训练标签由精确几何碰撞检查生成，每一步执行也由几何检查独立审核。该模块可挂载于任意控制器，实验集成到连续路径跟随控制器与 GATeD（障碍盲 D* Lite 规划器，使用类型化否决更新规划图）中。

Result: CollisionGAT 能为每个移动智能体给出碰撞风险分数，并成功驱动接受、修复、重规划或推迟的决策流程；在连续路径跟随控制器与 GATeD 规划器两类下游任务上均可用，且所有执行步均通过独立几何检查审核。

Conclusion: CollisionGAT 提供了一种通用、可即插即用的碰撞风险评分机制，使控制器与规划器可在提议执行前评估碰撞风险；几何检查既提供监督标签又作为执行审计，保证可靠性，但摘要未给出定量性能与对比结果。

Abstract: Before a team of robots moves, each proposed step must be checked for collisions with other robots and with obstacles. We present CollisionGAT, a graph-attention network that reads the current and proposed states of moving agents together with locally relevant stationary obstacles and returns one collision-risk score per moving agent. Any controller can use these scores to accept, repair, replan, or postpone a proposed step. We mount CollisionGAT on a continuous path-following controller and on GATeD, an obstacle-blind D* Lite planner that uses typed vetoes to update its planning graphs. Exact geometric checks supply the training labels and independently audit every executed step.

</details>


### [47] [Towards Kinematic Actionable Infeasibility Detection in Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32806)
*Aayush Rath,Lakshya Jindal,Antony Thomas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种几何驱动的运动规划不可行性认证框架，通过符号距离场在构型空间中追踪障碍边界诱导的分离流形，结合GPU加速的并行前沿扩展算法，在4-DOF和5-DOF场景中实现秒级至分钟级的不可行性认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动规划不仅需要计算无碰撞路径，还需要在无路径存在时给出不可行性认证；完整方法受限于低维空间，采样规划器虽可高效扩展却无法提供有限时间的不可行性证书，该问题在高维空间中基本未解决。

Method: 基于显式的、依赖分辨率的构型空间拓扑分析；利用符号距离场表示，直接在构型空间中追踪由障碍边界诱导的分离流形，从而检测不可行性并识别具体几何原因；为应对计算挑战，开发了利用GPU加速的并行前沿扩展算法，实现高维空间中的高效单纯形重建。

Result: 在4-DOF和5-DOF机器人场景中验证，4-DOF情形可在数秒内认证不可行性，5-DOF情形在四分钟内完成。

Conclusion: 该几何驱动框架能够为高维运动规划提供不可行性认证，并可识别几何成因；作者进一步讨论了提升向更高维空间可扩展性的途径。

Abstract: Motion planning in robotics requires not only computing collision-free paths but also certifying infeasibility when no such path exists. Complete methods are limited to low-dimensional spaces, while sampling-based planners scale efficiently but cannot provide finite-time infeasibility certificates, leaving this problem largely unresolved in high-dimensional spaces. In this letter, we present a geometry-driven framework for certifying infeasibility through an explicit resolution-dependent analysis of configuration space topology. Leveraging signed distance field representations, the proposed method traces separating manifolds induced by obstacle boundaries directly in configuration space, enabling both detection of infeasibility and identification of the specific geometric cause. To address computational challenges, we develop a parallel frontier-expansion algorithm that exploits GPU acceleration for efficient simplicial reconstruction in high-dimensional spaces. We validate the approach on 4-DOF and 5-DOF robot scenarios, certifying infeasibility within seconds for 4-DOF cases and under four minutes for 5-DOF cases. We further discuss avenues for improving scalability to higher-dimensional spaces.

</details>


### [48] [Scanning While Imagining: A Scene-Graph World Model for Robotic Ultrasound Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32837)
*Xuesong Li,Shuai Chen,Feng Li,Zhongliang Jiang,Nassir Navab,Yuan Bi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SonoGraph-WM：动作与目标条件化的世界模型，以场景图（SG）表示解剖结构，联合预测未来SG与探头位姿，并用滚动时域规划器进行前瞻式超声探头导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超声（US）采集高度依赖操作者对解剖结构的理解及对探头运动下视野变化的预判。现有机器人超声导航方法在选择动作时并未显式预测这些解剖变化，因此缺乏前瞻性。

Method: 将解剖结构表示为场景图（SG），编码可见结构、几何及空间关系，不合成超声图像。输入SG历史与探头位姿，用统一Transformer联合预测未来SG与位姿。滚动时域规划器递归想象候选轨迹，选取到达目标图的最短预测路径并执行短时域动作后重规划。为减少对已跟踪且带解剖标注的超声序列的依赖，从CT标签图沿表面约束的探头轨迹生成对齐的SG–位姿训练数据。

Result: 在四个留出CT病例上，20步预测中空间关系F1保持93%以上；使用标注派生的SG，闭环导航在胆囊与胰腺上成功率分别为77.50%与75.00%。在机器人–体模实验中，使用标签图派生的SG，规划器73.7%的试验到达目标视图。

Conclusion: 结果支持CT监督的解剖世界建模用于探头规划，并强调频繁观测更新对可靠导航的重要性。

Abstract: Ultrasound (US) acquisition depends on the operator's ability to interpret anatomy and anticipate how the view will change with probe motion. Many robotic US navigation methods select actions without explicitly predicting these anatomical changes. We propose SonoGraph-WM, an action- and goal-conditioned world model for anticipatory probe navigation. The model represents anatomy as scene graphs (SGs), capturing visible structures, their geometry, and spatial relationships without synthesizing US images. Given a history of SGs and probe poses, a unified Transformer jointly predicts future SGs and poses. A receding-horizon planner recursively imagines candidate trajectories, selects the shortest predicted path reaching a goal graph, and follows it over a short execution horizon before replanning from new observations. To reduce reliance on tracked and anatomically annotated US sequences, we generate aligned SG--pose training data from computed tomography (CT) label maps along surface-constrained probe trajectories. On four held-out CT cases, spatial relation F1 remains above 93% over 20 prediction steps, and closed-loop navigation achieves 77.50% and 75.00% success for the gallbladder and pancreas, respectively, using annotation-derived SGs. In robot--phantom navigation experiments with label-map-derived SGs, the planner reached the target view in 73.7% of trials. These findings support CT-supervised anatomical world modeling for probe planning and highlight the importance of frequent observation updates for reliable navigation. Project Page: https://noseefood.github.io/us-sonograph-wm/

</details>


### [49] [FINE: Future-Informed Navigation Encoding for Data-Efficient Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32855)
*Khang H. Nguyen,Hoang Pham Quang Nguyen,Ha Phuong Nguyen,Khanh Dinh Binh,Xuan Ha Nguyen,Vien Ngo,Duy Ho Nguyen Minh,Huan Nguyen,An T. Le*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FINE 是一个面向视觉语言导航（VLN）的未来感知导航编码框架，通过从已有示范轨迹中挖掘未来观测的潜在监督信号，提升策略在新环境中的泛化能力，尤其在示范数据受限时增益显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将 VLN 策略适配到新环境成本高昂，因为每条新增路线与指令都需要具身示范。而标准的"观测到动作"训练仅利用了每条轨迹中已含信息的一小部分。未来观测其实揭示了智能体将要遇到的关键地标信息，包括其外观与在 3D 中的排布方式，这些信息尚未被充分利用。

Method: 提出 FINE（Future-Informed Navigation Encoding）框架，为 VLN 主干网络配备两种互补的辅助表示：(1) 显式地标 token，按指令指定的有序地标，在语义 2D 图块特征空间与视角相关的 3D 几何特征空间中预测未来的地标区域；(2) 隐式未来 token，通过视频世界模型生成同场景的合理反事实未来，学习区分实际到达的地标状态与这些反事实未来。

Result: 在 R2R-CE 与 RxR-CE 的 val-unseen 上，使用全量训练数据时，FINE 将 InternVLA-N1 的成功率分别提升 2.6 与 4.5 个百分点。在示范数据受限时增益更明显：在 70% 示范预算下，成功率提升 6.8 个百分点，约弥补了因减少示范数据所损失性能的三分之一。

Conclusion: FINE 通过显式地标预测与隐式反事实未来判别两种辅助监督，从现有示范中提取未来信息，有效提升 VLN 策略性能，并在示范数据稀缺场景下展现出更强的鲁棒性与数据效率。

Abstract: Adapting vision-language navigation (VLN) policies to new environments is expensive because every additional route and instruction requires an embodied demonstration. Yet standard observation-to-action training uses only a small fraction of the information already contained in each trajectory. In particular, future observations reveal the instruction-relevant landmarks that the agent will encounter, including what they look like and how they are arranged in 3D. We introduce FINE, a Future-Informed Navigation Encoding framework that extracts this latent supervision from existing demonstrations. FINE equips a VLN backbone with two complementary auxiliary representations. First, explicit landmark tokens follow the ordered landmarks specified by the instruction and are trained to predict future landmark regions in both semantic 2D patch-feature space and viewpoint-dependent 3D geometric feature space. Second, an implicit future token learns to distinguish the landmark state that is actually reached from plausible same-scene counterfactual futures generated by a video world model. On R2R-CE and RxR-CE val-unseen, FINE improves InternVLA-N1 by 2.6 and 4.5 success-rate points, respectively, at full training data. More importantly, as demonstrations become limited, the benefit grows: at a 70% demonstration budget, FINE improves success rate by 6.8 points, recovering roughly one-third of the performance lost by reducing the training demonstrations. Project page is available at https://finevln.github.io/.

</details>


### [50] [RoboFoundry: System-as-Policy Evolution for Self-Learning Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32862)
*Jingsong Liang,Shuhao Liao,Shizhe Zhang,Diyuan Hou,Yuxin Cai,Xinjian Deng,Chengyang He,Wenhui Huang,Runjia Tan,Zhidong Wang,Lan Yu,Xuesong Tian,Guillaume Sartoretti,Jie Luo,Yao Mu,Wenjun Wu,Wanhua Li,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 RoboFoundry——首个具身智能体框架，将支撑系统本身建模为统一策略，通过"自演化系统即策略"实现跨机器人迁移与在线进化，在多个基准上取得最优表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法多优化智能体栈中的单个组件（记忆、上下文、技能、动作接口），未将支撑系统作为统一策略；且单纯交互无法自提升，除非把执行经验转化为持久的、经校验的系统变更。

Method: 将过程形式化为自演化系统即策略（Self-Evolving System-as-Policy）：诊断决策与记忆管理的能力缺口，将执行轨迹转换为经校验的任务专用系统更新，并把重复性改进提升为通用系统。演化覆盖两个表面：管理活动内部上下文与持久文件系统记忆的上下文系统，以及组织原子技能、可复用组合与失败条件恢复的分层技能系统。共享语义接口分离具身无关决策与具身相关执行。

Result: EmbodiedBench 上达 SOTA，使 GPT-5.5 提升 27.8%，Qwen3.7-Plus 接近 GPT-5.5（70.3% vs 72.7%）；RoboMemArena 长时程记忆上超所有基线至少 39.0%；LIBERO-PRO 上各类扰动均超 Cap-Agent0 243.8%–679.7%；真实部署中实现零样本迁移与跨机器人、跨任务在线演化。

Conclusion: 把支撑系统作为统一策略并持续自演化，可带来跨基础模型的一致增益，并支持具身智能体的零样本迁移与在线进化，展现全自主具身智能体潜力。

Abstract: A foundation model should not act in isolation as an embodied agent. Yet, existing methods often optimize individual components of the agent stack, such as memory, context, skills, or action interfaces, rather than treating the supporting system itself as a unified policy. Moreover, interaction alone does not yield self-improvement unless execution experience is converted into persistent, validated system changes. We therefore propose RoboFoundry, the first embodied agentic framework that formulates this process as Self-Evolving System-as-Policy. RoboFoundry diagnoses capability gaps in decision-making and memory management, converts execution traces into validated task-specific system updates, and promotes recurring improvements to the general system. Evolution operates over two complementary surfaces: a context system that manages active internal context and persistent file-system memory, and a hierarchical skill system that organizes atomic skills, reusable compositions, and failure-conditioned recovery. A shared semantic interface separates embodiment-invariant decisions from embodiment-specific execution, allowing evolved system capabilities to transfer across heterogeneous robots. On EmbodiedBench, RoboFoundry achieves state-of-the-art performance, notably improving GPT-5.5 by 27.8%. It also brings Qwen3.7-Plus to near parity with GPT-5.5 (70.3% vs. 72.7%), showing consistent gains from system-as-policy evolution across foundation models. For long-horizon memory, RoboFoundry outperforms all baselines on RoboMemArena by at least 39.0%, even against methods assisted by external foundation models. On LIBERO-PRO, it further outperforms Cap-Agent0 by 243.8%-679.7% across all perturbation types. In real-world deployments, RoboFoundry demonstrates zero-shot transfer and online evolution across robots and tasks, highlighting its potential for fully autonomous embodied agents.

</details>


### [51] [Communication-Aware Heterogeneous Graph Learning for Decentralized Multi-Human Multi-Robot Task Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32935)
*Ziqin Yuan,Ruiqi Wang,Baijian Yang,Byung-Cheol Min*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CommHG——通信感知异构图学习框架，用于去中心化多人类-多机器人任务分配，联合优化分配与通信，在含16机器人、6人类、112任务的异构团队上优于最强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多人类-多机器人（MH-MR）团队需协调稀缺且动态可用的人类支持与分布式机器人执行，但机器人-机器人与人类-机器人通信受限，导致信息交换与监督干预延迟，加剧了任务分配与人类支持的耦合难度。

Method: CommHG以局部图表示耦合的人-机器人-任务交互，并以信息可用性与信息年龄（age）为条件；学习通信动作，决定机器人何时、向哪位操作员查询，以及何时与同伴共享信息；分配与通信通过协作式多智能体强化学习联合优化。同时构建了整合异构人类与机器人、动态任务与运行状态、受限机器人-机器人及双向人类-机器人链路的基准。

Result: 在最多16个机器人、6个人类、112个任务的异构团队实验中，随着协调规模增大，CommHG提升了带时限的加权任务完成度，并在Large场景中优于最强基线。

Conclusion: 通信感知的异构图学习与分配-通信联合优化能有效应对MH-MR团队中的通信受限与人类资源稀缺问题，且随团队规模扩大优势更明显。

Abstract: Multi-human multi-robot (MH-MR) teams combine robotic autonomy with human expertise, but effective task allocation requires coordinating scarce, dynamically available human support with distributed robot execution. Limited robot-robot and human-robot communication further complicates this coupling by delaying information exchange and supervisory intervention. We introduce CommHG, a communication-aware heterogeneous graph learning framework for decentralized MH-MR task allocation. CommHG represents coupled human-robot-task interactions through local graphs conditioned on information availability and age. Learned communication actions enable robots to decide when and which operator to query, and when to share information with peers. Allocation and communication are jointly optimized through cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning, allowing the team to acquire useful information while managing limited communication and supervisory resources. We also introduce a benchmark integrating heterogeneous humans and robots, dynamic tasks and operational states, and constrained robot-robot and bidirectional human-robot links. Experiments across heterogeneous teams with up to 16 robots, 6 humans, and 112 tasks show that CommHG improves timely weighted mission completion as coordination scale increases, outperforming the strongest baseline in the Large scenario.

</details>


### [52] [Learning Geometry-Aware Virtual Fixtures From Sparse Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32958)
*Maximilian Mühlbauer,Marcella Piacentino,Raffaella Mancino,Paolino De Risi,Bernhard Weber,Margarida Campos,Thomas Hulin,Sylvain Calinon,Freek Stulp,Alin Albu-Schäffer,Julian Klodmann,Fanny Ficuciello,João Silvério*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出利用线性二次跟踪（LQT）引入运动先验，仅需少量via点即可生成虚拟夹具轨迹；并通过在黎曼流形上重构LQT以纳入姿态引导，基于概率解释导出协方差用于调节夹具刚度与有效作用区域，在dVRK微创手术切割任务中验证了框架有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统概率式示教学习（LfD）需要大量示教数据，而在许多遥操作应用中难以采集足够数量的演示。因此需要一种能让操作者凭直觉、用极少示教快速创建虚拟夹具（VF）轨迹的方法。

Method: 采用线性二次跟踪（LQT）作为运动先验，使用户仅通过少量via点示教即可定义引导轨迹；为处理姿态引导，将经典LQT重构到黎曼流形上，引入额外几何先验；对LQT解进行概率解释，在轨迹各点导出协方差估计，用以调制夹具刚度（via点附近强引导、远离处弱引导），并定义夹具有效区域，允许操作者离开影响区执行未建模任务。先在玩具示例上评估黎曼LQT公式，再在dVRK微创手术高精度切割任务上验证完整框架。

Result: 在玩具示例上评估了所提黎曼LQT形式；并在da Vinci研究套件（dVRK）上、以需要高精度的微创手术切割任务验证了完整框架。

Conclusion: 借助LQT运动先验与黎曼流形上的几何先验，仅需少量via点示教即可构建带姿态引导的虚拟夹具；由概率解释得到的协方差可自适应调节刚度并提供有效区域，从而兼顾强引导与操作者可自由执行未建模任务的需求，在微创手术场景中具备可行性。

Abstract: In many teleoperation applications, collecting a large number of demonstrations as required for traditional probabilistic learning from demonstration (LfD) approaches may not be feasible. To still give operators the ability to intuitively create trajectories as Virtual Fixtures (VFs), we propose to leverage a motion prior in the learning process. Particularly, by using Linear Quadratic Tracking (LQT), users are able to define guiding trajectories from the demonstration of just a few via points. To account for orientation guidance, we further reformulate classical LQT on Riemannian manifolds, introducing an additional geometric prior. Through a probabilistic interpretation of the LQT solution, we derive a covariance estimate at each trajectory point which we use to modulate the stiffness of the resulting fixture, resulting in strong guidance around the via points and softer guidance when far away. The covariance information is also used to define a validity region of the fixture, allowing the operator to leave its influence area and conduct unmodeled tasks. We evaluate the proposed Riemannian LQT formulation in a set of toy examples and the full framework on a cutting task requiring high precision in a minimally invasive surgery setting on the da Vinci Research Kit (dVRK).

</details>


### [53] [TriDrive: Joint Driver, Vehicle, and Road Modeling for Forecasting and Driver Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33000)
*Yuhang Wang,Jingxin Yang,Chuheng Wei,Yuechen Guo,Jinghan Xu,Zhao Han,Hao Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TriDrive是首个联合预测驾驶员运动学、车辆动力学与道路需求的统一框架，采用自动化条件化转移模型，在AIDE、BATON及实车部署中均取得领先结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驾驶员监控的核心在于预测驾驶员、车辆与道路场景如何交互演化。既有工作要么孤立建模舱内活动，要么仅建模受交通条件影响的驾驶员运动，缺乏联合建模与实时车载评估，因此需要将三者统一建模并进行实时车上验证。

Method: 提出TriDrive统一框架，通过自动化条件化的转移模型联合预测驾驶员运动学、车辆动力学与道路需求。采用模态专属编码器：驾驶员采用锚定的运动学表示，车辆采用因果CAN总线动力学，道路采用冻结的V-JEPA 2潜变量并引入结构化道路边界（road margins）；各编码器经有向残差连接相连，由驾驶员与道路上下文细化车辆预测。为实时部署，对道路编码器进行蒸馏，模型运行于comma four加外接8 GB GPU上。

Result: 在公开AIDE基准上，其运动学编码方案取得已发表基线中的全数据集SOTA（All-MPJPE 48.05 对 71.47）。在197.2小时自然驾驶BATON子集上，有向连接与道路边界将转向起始的辅助介入PR-AUC提升0.084，将碰撞时间下降的PR-AUC提升0.286。实时部署中，轻量当前状态预警探针以5 Hz更新，p95延迟177 ms，同时联合模型并行预测。在人工标注的手动驾驶预警上，探针AUROC为0.725，优于基于openpilot的基线0.563；在配对实车研究中，14名驾驶员评价其预警在恰当性（+1.79）与及时性（+2.67）上优于openpilot驾驶员监控系统。

Conclusion: TriDrive验证了联合驾驶员—车辆—道路建模的有效性与实时可行性，在公开基准、自然驾驶数据与实车用户评价中全面优于现有基线，为车载驾驶员监控提供了统一且可部署的解决方案。

Abstract: Predicting how drivers, vehicles, and road scenes interact and evolve together is central to driver monitoring. Prior work models in-cabin activity or traffic-conditioned driver motion in isolation, motivating joint driver, vehicle, and road modeling with real-time on-vehicle evaluation. We introduce TriDrive, to our knowledge the first unified framework that jointly forecasts driver kinematics, vehicle dynamics, and road demands through an automation-conditioned transition model. Modality-specific encoders (an anchored kinematic representation of the driver, causal CAN-bus dynamics, and frozen V-JEPA 2 road latents with structured road margins) are connected by directed residual connections through which driver and road context refine vehicle forecasts. We evaluate TriDrive on three downstream tasks. On the public AIDE benchmark, its kinematic encoder recipe sets a new full-set state of the art (SOTA) among published baselines (48.05 versus 71.47 All-MPJPE). On 197.2 hours of naturalistic BATON subset, directed connections and road margins raise assistance-engaged PR-AUC by 0.084 for steering onset and 0.286 for time-to-collision drops. For real-time use, we distill the road encoders and run TriDrive on a comma four with an external 8 GB GPU, where a lightweight current-state warning probe updates at 5 Hz with 177 ms p95 latency while the joint model forecasts concurrently. The probe is above an openpilot-based baseline on human-labeled manual-driving warnings (AUROC 0.725 versus 0.563), and in a paired on-road study 14 drivers rate its warnings as more appropriate (+1.79) and timely (+2.67) than those of openpilot's driver-monitoring system.

</details>


### [54] [CAPEX: Efficiently Distilling Foundation Model Behavior into Deployable Robot Policies through Experience-Adaptive Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33007)
*Shivam Aarya,Zhang Xi-Jia,Chengyue Huang,Junhyun Kim,Huishu Xue,Hrishit Leen,Roman Yakunin,Animesh Garg,Zsolt Kira*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CAPEX：以通用多模态基础模型作为自主示范者，利用历史执行经验自适应调节基础模型的观测与重规划频率，从而低成本、可扩展地采集机器人示范数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习长期依赖人类遥操作示范，但采集过程不直观、难以规模化且与执行天然异步。基础模型虽能零样本生成操作轨迹，但执行中反复调用速度慢、成本高，难以作为可扩展的数据生成器。

Method: 提出经验条件化的示范采集框架 CAPEX：依据先前尝试的执行经验，自适应调整基础模型进行观测、推理与重规划的频率，从而在保持任务成功率的同时减少模型调用与开销。

Result: 在 RoboCasa 任务以及 Franka 与双臂 YAM 实体平台上评测，统计任务成功率、模型调用次数、token 用量、采集时间与成本；成功示范数提升 4.3 倍，单个成功示范成本降低 80%。用 CAPEX 数据训练的 Diffusion Policy 与 ACT 接近匹配的人类遥操作数据训练效果，延长训练后从零训练的策略差距基本消失。

Conclusion: 基础模型可作为可扩展的可复用机器人经验来源，自主采集的数据在下游策略学习中具有与人类示范相当的价值。

Abstract: Robot learning has largely relied on human-teleoperated demonstrations to acquire effective learnable behaviors. However, human-operated data collection processes can be unintuitive, difficult to scale, and inherently asynchronous. We explore an alternative: distilling physical behavior from general-purpose multimodal foundation models into deployable robot policies by using the foundation model itself as an autonomous demonstrator. While sufficiently capable models can generate successful zero-shot manipulation trajectories, repeatedly invoking them during physical execution is slow and expensive, limiting their utility as scalable data generators. As a solution, we introduce CAPEX, an experience-conditioned demonstration collection framework that uses execution experience from previous attempts to adapt how frequently the foundation model must observe, reason, and replan. We evaluate across RoboCasa tasks and on physical Franka and bimanual YAM-arm platforms, measuring task success, model calls, token usage, collection time, and cost. We further train Diffusion Policy and ACT on matched sets of human-teleoperated and foundation-model-generated demonstrations to evaluate the downstream learning value of autonomously collected data. We find that CAPEX increases the number of successful demonstrations by 4.3x while reducing the cost per successful demonstration by 80%. Policies trained on CAPEX-generated data approach the performance of those trained on matched human demonstrations; with longer training, this gap largely closes for policies trained from scratch. These results suggest that foundation models can serve as scalable sources of reusable robot experience. Project page: https://capex-paper.github.io/

</details>


### [55] [Is Online Interaction Necessary for Recovery? A Minimalist Approach to Robust Planning via Perturbation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33049)
*Bumgeun Park,Donghwan Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PARS，仅用现有示范数据即提升行为克隆在闭环执行中的恢复能力与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为克隆在闭环执行中易受协变量偏移影响，微小预测或执行误差会使机器人偏离示范数据覆盖的状态。现有缓解方法多需额外收集纠错示范，依赖环境交互或专家策略。

Method: PARS在训练时扰动机器人本体感知状态，并只将预测轨迹的后段锚定到原始示范，允许前段自由生成纠错动作，从而在规划时域内恢复至示范行为。与传统输入噪声增强在整个预测时域保留原监督目标不同，PARS提供轨迹级的恢复监督。方法无需额外环境交互或专家查询，只需对各类动作序列策略的BC目标做少量修改即可适配。

Result: 在51个RLBench操作任务上，基于流模型、Transformer和扩散策略的实验表明，PARS提升了闭环执行中对协变量偏移的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: PARS是一种简单、通用的训练方法，利用现有示范实现轨迹级恢复监督，在不增加环境交互或专家依赖的前提下增强行为克隆策略的闭环鲁棒性。

Abstract: Behavior cloning (BC) is vulnerable to covariate shift during closed-loop execution, where small prediction or execution errors can drive the robot toward states poorly covered by the demonstration data. We focus on action-sequence planning, where a policy predicts a finite-horizon sequence of actions as a reference trajectory for robot execution. Existing approaches to covariate shift often rely on collecting additional corrective demonstrations, requiring further environment interaction and access to an expert or reference policy. We propose Perturbation-Augmented Recovery Supervision (PARS), a simple training approach for improving recovery using only existing demonstrations. PARS perturbs the robot's proprioceptive state and anchors only the later portion of the predicted trajectory to the original demonstration, leaving the earlier portion free to generate corrective motion and recover toward the demonstrated behavior within the planning horizon. Unlike conventional input-noise augmentation, which preserves the original supervision target over the entire prediction horizon, PARS explicitly provides trajectory-level supervision for recovery from perturbed states. PARS requires neither additional environment interaction nor expert queries and can be instantiated across different action-sequence policy classes with only minor modifications to their BC objectives. Experiments on 51 RLBench manipulation tasks with flow-based, transformer-based, and diffusion-based policies demonstrate that PARS improves robustness to covariate shift during closed-loop execution.

</details>


### [56] [SwingRL: Adaptive Observation Reinforcement Learning with World-Model Prediction for Cable-Suspended Hoisting Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33053)
*Guangming Wang,Xiaoyu Zhang,Yucheng Xin,Wanli Ma,Jiucai Liu,Yunxiang Ma,Joe Ingham,Haibing Wu,Yixiong Jing,Olaf Wysocki,Brian Sheil*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SwingRL残差强化学习框架，融合年龄感知世界模型、经典抗摆先验与循环残差策略，解决延迟/丢失观测下的吊挂载荷精确插入问题，仿真最难题成功率达69.5%/77.3%，实物平台零微调达90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 柔性缆索吊装载荷运动欠驱动、外部扰动多变，且视觉观测延迟或丢失导致控制执行时感知状态陈旧，这些问题在将载荷套筒精确插入钢筋销的施工任务中尤为关键。

Method: SwingRL由三部分组成：(1) 年龄感知世界模型，利用已执行指令将最新接收的载荷观测前推至当前控制步，在延迟和有损感知下给出时间对齐的状态估计；(2) 经典抗摆先验，提供标称跟踪与摆动阻尼；(3) 循环残差策略，仅学习对先验的有界修正，补偿参数变化、外部扰动及残余状态估计误差。

Result: 在涵盖系统参数变化、风扰动、感知退化和强阵风的递进难度阶梯上，最困难设定下SwingRL取得69.5%严格成功率和77.3%宽松成功率，较所有基线至少高出60个百分点；世界模型消融验证了时间对齐状态估计在观测丢失增加时维持插入成功率的作用；无需实物微调即在物理平台上达到90%成功率。

Conclusion: 将年龄感知世界模型与抗摆先验、循环残差策略相结合，可有效应对陈旧反馈与不确定动力学，实现吊挂载荷的高精度插入，并具备较强的仿真到实物迁移能力。

Abstract: Cable-suspended hoisting is widely used to move heavy or bulky payloads that cannot be handled conveniently by rigid pick-and-place systems, for example in crane-assisted construction. Robotic hoisting using flexible cables is challenging because payload motion is underactuated, external disturbances vary, and delayed or lost visual observations can make the perceived payload state stale at control execution. These effects are particularly critical during precise insertion of a suspended payload's sockets onto rebar pins, which is a very common task in construction environments. We present SwingRL, a residual reinforcement-learning (RL) framework that combines an age-aware world model, a classical anti-swing prior, and a recurrent residual policy to address two coupled problems: stale feedback and uncertain dynamics. The world model propagates the newest received payload observation to the current control step using the executed commands, providing a time-aligned state estimate under delayed and lossy sensing. The prior supplies nominal tracking and swing damping. The residual policy learns bounded corrections to the prior rather than the complete control law, compensating for system-parameter variation, external disturbances, and remaining state-estimation errors. We evaluate SwingRL against classical and learning-based baselines across a cumulative difficulty ladder covering system-parameter variation, wind disturbance, degraded sensing, and strong gusts. Under the most difficult setting, SwingRL achieves 69.5% strict and 77.3% broad success, exceeding all baselines by at least 60 percentage points, respectively. World-model ablations support the role of time-aligned state estimation in maintaining insertion success as observation loss increases. Finally, without real-robot fine-tuning, SwingRL achieves 90% success on the physical rig.

</details>


### [57] [REALM: A Coarse-to-Fine Generative Framework for Embodied Reactive Listening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33095)
*Peizhen Li,Longbing Cao,Yang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出REALM，一个从粗到细的音频驱动反应式聆听面部动作生成框架，结合历史感知时序对齐与随机表情细化，在ViCo和L2L上优于基线，并部署于Ameca机器人验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成具身对话AI中聆听者的反应式面部动作存在两大建模挑战：一是需兼顾说话者线索的时序（响应常带时间滞后）与聆听者自身动作的连续性；二是既要捕捉整体运动轨迹，又要刻画局部多变的短暂面部事件（如表情、眨眼），这类事件难以确定性预测。

Method: REALM采用粗到细框架：Reactive Gated Speaker-Listener Fusion模块通过以延迟为中心的注意力先验和自适应门控，将聆听者动作历史与说话者音频融合；粗解码器预测基础运动轨迹，再在表情子空间中用音频条件化的随机残差进行增强，同时保留粗姿态参数。

Result: 在ViCo和L2L数据集上，多项运动质量指标均优于所评估的基线；额外分析考察了延迟敏感性、门控行为与眨眼动态；并在Ameca人形机器人上部署，结合感知用户研究验证了生成行为在物理具身中的适用性。

Conclusion: REALM通过结合历史感知的时序对齐与随机表情细化，有效解决了聆听者反应式面部动作生成中的时序滞后与局部可变事件两个核心难题，且方法可迁移至实体机器人，具备实际具身应用价值。

Abstract: Generating responsive listener facial motion is an important task for embodied conversational AI. Two modeling challenges are central: accounting for the timing of speaker cues while maintaining continuity with the listener's ongoing motion, and capturing locally variable facial events alongside the overall motion trajectory. Listener responses may follow preceding cues with a temporal lag, while brief expressions and blinks introduce variation that is difficult to predict deterministically. These challenges motivate a framework that combines history-aware temporal alignment with stochastic expression refinement. We propose REALM (Reactive Embodied Audio-driven Listening Model), a coarse-to-fine framework for audio-driven reactive listening. A Reactive Gated Speaker-Listener Fusion module combines listener motion history with speaker audio through a delay-centered attention prior and adaptive gating. A coarse decoder predicts a base motion trajectory, which is augmented by audio-conditioned stochastic residuals in the expression subspace while retaining the coarse pose parameters. Evaluations on ViCo and L2L show improvements over the evaluated baselines across multiple motion-quality metrics. Additional analyses examine delay sensitivity, gate behavior, and blink dynamics. Finally, deployment on an Ameca humanoid robot and a perceptual user study demonstrate the applicability of the generated behavior to physical embodiment.
  Code: https://github.com/lipzh5/REALM Demo: https://youtu.be/Tf5mpd5S8VQ

</details>


### [58] [Humanoids for Robot-Assisted Surgery: Bimanual Base Placement and Tool-Mount Optimization via Capability Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33096)
*Peihan Zhang,Zekai Liang,Florian Richter,Nikita Thareja,Ryan Broderick,Shanglei Liu,Michael Yip*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于能力地图的机器人设置框架，联合优化人形机器人基座位置与器械安装方向，以在满足工具末端运动学与RCM约束下最大化双臂可达性，并定量评估三款人形平台在三种腹腔镜手术中的工作空间覆盖能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人应用于医疗与临床任务的兴趣日益增长，但其运动学能力能否满足机器人辅助腹腔镜手术的需求尚不明确；同时，如何最优地摆放机器人与配置设置也缺乏定量分析。

Method: 构建基于能力地图（capability map）的机器人设置框架，联合优化人形基座放置与器械安装朝向，在考虑工具末端运动学及远程运动中心（RCM）约束的条件下最大化双臂可达性；对三款具有不同体型尺寸与运动学冗余的人形平台，就胆囊切除术、腹股沟疝修补术和袖状胃切除术三种代表性普外科手术进行工作空间可达性评估，并与仅优化基座及启发式基线方法对比。

Result: 联合优化基座放置与器械安装的方案持续优于仅基座优化和启发式基线。对于工作空间较小且重叠极少的胆囊切除术与腹股沟疝修补术，人形可达性接近90%；而对于工作空间更大且多孔臂重叠的袖状胃切除术，人形覆盖率显著偏低。

Conclusion: 研究量化了人形机器人对部分选定腹腔镜手术的近期可行性，同时明确了其广泛部署前需解决的关键限制。

Abstract: Rapid advances in humanoid robotics have motivated growing interest in the application of humanoids for healthcare and clinical tasks. However, it remains unclear how close contemporary humanoids are to meeting the kinematic demands of robot-assisted laparoscopic surgery. In this work, we address the question of optimal robot positioning through a quantitative analysis of workspace and robot setup configurations. We present a capability-map-based robot setup framework that optimizes humanoid base placement and tool mounting orientation to maximize bimanual humanoid reachability while accounting for tool-tip kinematics and remote-center-of-motion (RCM) constraints. We evaluate three humanoid platforms spanning different body dimensions and kinematic redundancy on workspace reachability for three representative general surgery procedures: cholecystectomy, inguinal hernia repair, and sleeve gastrectomy. The proposed joint optimization of base placement and tool mounting consistently outperforms base-only optimization and heuristic baselines. For cholecystectomy and inguinal hernia repair, which are characterized by relatively small and minimally overlapping workspaces, humanoid reachability approached 90%. For the larger, overlapping multi-port arm workspace of sleeve gastrectomy, humanoids yield substantially lower coverage. These results quantify the near-term promise of humanoids for selected laparoscopic procedures and clarify key limitations that must be addressed for broader deployment.

</details>


### [59] [Evolving Dexterous Robots from Scratch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33101)
*Zihan Guo,Shuzhe Zhang,Muhan Li,Peiyang Li,Sam Kriegman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种端到端可进化自由形态机器人抓取手的方法，不预设身体任何部件的存在、关节或几何结构，融合对比学习、自回归发育模型、进化策略与强化学习，进化出可抓取、保持、旋转并使用多样物体的机械手，并以零样本方式制造并在现实中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关于如何手工设计具备灵巧操作能力的智能体，目前所知甚少。从动物操作物体的行为中推断出的设计原理难以被仿生复现，且对人工机器而言未必是最优的。因此作者希望摆脱预设结构，让机器人形态自由进化以发现更优或全新的操作方案。

Method: 作者让自由形态机器人进化以完成抓取、保持、旋转和使用多种物体的任务，且不预设身体任何部位的存在、关节或几何结构。方法上采用对比学习构建高度可搜索的设计空间遗传嵌入，用以自回归发育模型解码设计，再以进化策略搜索优良设计，并用强化学习训练每个被进化出的设计。优胜设计被自动转换为可制造蓝图，打印、组装并完成真实世界零样本测试。

Result: 在某些条件下会自发涌现出诸如尾部、喙、爪、爪形等熟悉的抓握形态，但这些条件对进化生物学家可能更有意义；而从头设计操作器还可揭示被忽视或未知的全新结构，这些结构可能更适配当前任务。优胜设计被自动转为可制造蓝图、打印组装，并在真实世界中以零样本方式完成测试。

Conclusion: 该方法在性能、多样性和复杂度方面代表了进化机器人学的最先进水平。

Abstract: Little is known about how to manually design agents capable of dexterous manipulation. Some design principles have been inferred from close examination of how animals manipulate objects, but these structures and behaviors have so far resisted biomimicry and may not be optimal for artificial machines. Here we evolve freeform robots to pick up, hold, rotate, and use diverse objects. Unlike other approaches to optimizing robot hands, we do not presuppose the presence, articulation, or geometry of any part of the body. Although familiar prehensile forms such as tails, beaks, paws and claws may emerge spontaneously under certain conditions--and while such conditions could be of interest to evolutionary biologists--de novo manipulator design can also reveal whole new solutions, overlooked or unknown structures which may be better suited for the task at hand. We use contrastive learning to create a highly searchable genetic embedding of design space, an autoregressive developmental model to decode designs, evolutionary strategies to find good designs, and reinforcement learning to train each evolved design. Winning designs were automatically converted into a manufacturable blueprint, printed, assembled and tested in the real world in a zero-shot manner. The results represent the state-of-the-art in evolutionary robotics in terms of performance, diversity and complexity.

</details>


### [60] [VPTwin: Real-Sim-Real Video Prediction for Robotic Manipulation Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33104)
*Zhenghao Xiao,Minting Pan,Nantian He,Dongzhan Zhou,Yunbo Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VPTwin，一个Real-Sim-Real的视频预测框架，利用真实同步的仿真孪生来锚定真实世界未来预测，从而缓解长时程展开中的误差累积与物理幻觉，并提升下游操作规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯数据驱动的动作条件视频预测虽为机器人提供直观世界模型，但在长时程展开中常出现误差累积和物理不合理的幻觉，严重影响下游动作规划。核心痛点在于真实视频缺乏物理约束，而仿真又难以建模真实接触交互。

Method: VPTwin采用Real-Sim-Real闭环：1）针对目标操作任务，由VLM从真实演示片段中重建可执行的数字孪生；2）针对未观测物理属性估计的病态性问题，在Isaac Sim中对候选动作轨迹在随机化物理配置下模拟多条前向动力学展开；3）以这些展开作为上下文参考，融合两个域——用仿真动力学保证物理合理性，同时从真实视频中捕获未建模的接触交互；4）构建基于VPTwin的预测规划循环，对VLM提出的动作进行视觉验证并指导可靠的实世界执行。

Result: 评估显示，视频预测中的物理幻觉显著减少，操作规划性能明显提升。

Conclusion: 通过将真实视频与真实同步的仿真孪生相结合，VPTwin有效抑制了长时程视频预测中的误差累积和物理幻觉，并借助预测规划循环实现了更可靠的真实世界操作执行，验证了Real-Sim-Real范式的有效性。

Abstract: While action-conditioned video prediction provides an intuitive world model for robotics, purely data-driven predictors often suffer from compounding errors and physically implausible hallucinations in long-horizon rollouts, severely undermining downstream action planning. We propose VPTwin, a Real-Sim-Real video prediction framework that anchors real-world future prediction using real-synchronized simulation twins. For a target manipulation task, a VLM reconstructs an executable digital twin from a real demonstration episode. To accommodate the ill-posed estimation of unobserved physical properties, Isaac Sim simulates multiple forward dynamic rollouts across randomized physical configurations under candidate action trajectories. Using these rollouts as in-context references, VPTwin harmonizes both domains, using simulation dynamics to enforce physical plausibility while capturing unmodeled contact interactions from real video. Furthermore, we establish a predictive planning loop using VPTwin to visually verify VLM-proposed actions and guide reliable real-world execution. Evaluations show substantial reductions in physical hallucinations during video prediction and marked improvements in manipulation planning performance.

</details>


### [61] [Multi-Modal Non-Prehensile Estimation of Physical Parameters via Press-and-Pull Tipping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33138)
*Steven M. Hyland,Jing Xiao,Cagdas D. Onal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出多模态非抓取交互估计框架，结合滑动与按压-拉拽倾倒恢复物体质量、质心高度和摩擦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一非抓取操作无法揭示全部物理参数：平面推动使质量与摩擦耦合，传统倾倒无法恢复摩擦，且对低摩擦或曲面底座物体因滑动或旋转而失败。

Method: 融合滑动交互与"按压-拉拽"倾倒原语；按压-拉拽提高滑动阈值并稳定支点，实现无需先验几何模型的受控倾倒；融合腕部力/力矩传感、RGB-D感知与机器人本体感知，从两种互补交互模式中估计物理参数。

Result: 在ABB IRB120上对四个几何、质量、质心和摩擦各异的物体实验，相对误差较低，并成功处理在传统前向倾倒中失败的曲面底座物体。

Conclusion: 互补的非抓取交互可在不抓取、无先验物体模型、无学习交互动力学的前提下恢复一组紧凑的物理参数。

Abstract: Recovering physical properties of unknown objects through non-prehensile interaction is challenging because no single manipulation primitive reveals all relevant parameters. Planar pushing couples mass and friction, while conventional tipping cannot recover friction and may fail entirely when low-friction or curved-base objects slide or rotate instead of tipping. We introduce a multi-modal estimation framework that combines a sliding interaction with a press-and-pull tipping primitive to recover object mass, center of mass height, and surface friction. The press-and-pull interaction increases the object-table sliding threshold and stabilizes the pivot, enabling controlled tipping without any prior geometric object model. Wrist force/torque sensing, RGB-D perception, and robot proprioception are fused to estimate the physical parameters from the two complementary interaction modes. Experiments on an ABB IRB120 across four objects with varied geometry, mass, center of mass, and friction achieve low relative error, while successfully operating on curved-base objects that fail under conventional forward tipping. The results demonstrate that complementary non-prehensile interactions can recover a compact set of physical parameters without grasping, a prior object model, or learned interaction dynamics.

</details>


### [62] [Beyond State-as-Action: Exploiting Command-State Discrepancy for Robot Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33145)
*Peiyan Li,Yueran Tao,Enhao Zhang,Zhixuan Zhao,Chenghao Yue,Hao Wang,Lei Lv,Wentao Zhao,Jiahao Chen,Xin Liu,Kangyao Huang,Yu Luo,Huaping Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究交互约束下的命令-状态差异问题，提出CSDW（命令-状态差异加权）方法，将机器人响应时间、后续进展、持续未满足需求及需求变化综合为连续权重用于命令监督，在受限任务上优于均匀命令监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习中从实测机器人运动构建动作目标是成熟做法，但在交互约束下，命令-状态差异可能反映运动本身无法捕捉的控制需求。作者希望探究这种信息何时重要以及如何加以利用。

Method: 首先在三个真实机器人任务上通过任务与阶段分析，揭示受限交互下存在更大的监督差距，并用选择性命令保留证明局部有用命令信息的存在；据此提出CSDW方法，考虑机器人响应时间，将后续进展、持续未满足需求与需求变化组合为连续权重，用于命令监督。该方法无需任务阶段标注，也不改变策略架构或推理过程。

Result: CSDW在受限任务上优于均匀命令监督；在约束较少的任务上，各方法表现相近。

Conclusion: 命令-状态差异在交互约束下包含运动无法捕捉的有用控制信息；通过CSDW对命令监督进行加权可利用该信息，提升受限任务性能，且实现简单、无需额外标注或架构改动。

Abstract: Constructing action targets from measured robot motion is an established approach in imitation learning. Under interaction constraints, however, command-state discrepancy may reflect control demands that motion alone does not capture. We investigate when this information matters and how to exploit it. Across three real-robot tasks, task and phase analyses reveal larger supervision gaps under constrained interaction, while selective command retention provides evidence of locally useful command information. Building on these findings, we propose Command-State Discrepancy Weighting (CSDW), which accounts for robot response times and combines subsequent progress, persistent unmet demand, and demand changes into continuous weights for command supervision. The method requires no task-phase annotations or changes to policy architecture or inference. CSDW improves over uniform command supervision on constrained tasks, while methods perform similarly in the less constrained task.

</details>


### [63] [TimelyDAgger: Timing-Aware Expert Querying for VLA Policy Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33157)
*Zhixuan Zhao,Peiyan Li,Enhao Zhang,Yueran Tao,Hao Wang,Chenghao Yue,Lei Lv,Wentao Zhao,Jiahao Chen,Xin Liu,Kangyao Huang,Yu Luo,Huaping Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TimelyDAgger，通过结合内部VLA特征的Bridge-PCA监控与基于专家行为的反馈引导阈值自适应来优化接管时机，并构建了连接故障检测、接管时机与策略改进的评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人门控DAgger主要关注"是否需要帮助"的检测，而忽视了接管时机对专家演示内容质量及策略学习价值的影响。接管时机决定了在什么状态上收集专家监督，从而影响监督数据的质量与有效性。

Method: 提出TimelyDAgger，包含两个核心组件：(1) Bridge-PCA监控视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的内部特征；(2) 基于专家行为的Feedback-guided Threshold Adaptation进行阈值自适应。同时引入评估框架，将故障检测、接管时机与策略改进联系起来，并提出Target-Aligned Supervision Ratio（TASR）用于无需重训练即可评估监督质量。

Result: 实验表明接管时机确实影响策略学习效果；在匹配的专家动作预算下，TimelyDAgger在多数评估设置中实现了有竞争力的故障检测性能，并取得更高的训练后成功率。

Conclusion: 接管时机是机器人门控DAgger中被忽视但重要的设计维度；通过监控内部VLA特征并结合反馈引导的阈值自适应可有效改善接管时机，进而提升策略学习效果。

Abstract: DAgger improves robot policies by aggregating expert supervision from states visited during policy execution. Robot-gated DAgger automates expert queries, allowing the robot to decide when to request expert takeover. While existing gates emphasize detecting the need for assistance, takeover timing also shapes the content of these demonstrations and their value for policy learning. We propose TimelyDAgger, combining Bridge-PCA monitoring of internal vision-language-action (VLA) features with Feedback-guided Threshold Adaptation based on expert behavior to improve takeover timing. We introduce an evaluation framework linking failure detection, takeover timing, and policy improvement, including Target-Aligned Supervision Ratio (TASR) for assessing supervision quality without retraining. Experiments show that takeover timing affects policy learning, with TimelyDAgger achieving competitive failure detection and higher post-training success in most evaluated settings under matched expert-action budgets.

</details>


### [64] [Beyond Tasks: A Vision for Reproducing an Animal-like Behavioral Substrate Using Modern Robot Learning Techniques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33165)
*Samiyuru Menik,Hemadri Jayalath*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出以"习性行为基质"为概念视角，研究机器人在物理、环境与社会需求变化下持续保持情境化与行为连贯的能力，并以机器人动物伴侣作为研究场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习已产出能力日益增强的具身智能体，但对其"持续性行为胜任力"——即在需求不断变化时仍保持情境化、响应性与行为连贯——关注不足，这一能力正是动物持续展现的基本能力。

Method: 概念性/立场性论文。提出"习性行为基质"作为分析透镜，主张不应将动物行为视为孤立技能集合，而应关注其在竞争性需求下的持续协调；并提出以机器人动物伴侣作为研究长期交互与适应性的实验场景。

Result: 无数值实验结果。论证指出机器人动物伴侣系统为研究社会行为、记忆与持续学习如何在长期交互中发展提供了机会。

Conclusion: 该视角呼吁进一步探究这种持久行为胜任力如何补充具身智能体的高层能力，是机器人学习中被忽视的重要研究方向。

Abstract: Recent advances in robot learning have produced increasingly capable embodied agents. Yet comparatively less attention has been given to a more basic form of competence that animals exhibit continuously: the ability to remain situated, responsive, and behaviorally coherent as physical, environmental, and social demands change over time. We propose the ethological behavioral substrate as a conceptual lens for studying this form of competence in artificial agents. Rather than treating these behaviors that animals exhibit as a set of isolated skills, we argue that their continual coordination under competing demands constitutes an important and underexplored target for modern robot learning. We further propose robotic animal companions as a useful research setting for studying sustained interaction and adaptation in human-centered environments. Such systems provide an opportunity to investigate how social behavior, memory, and continual learning develop over long periods of interaction. This perspective motivates further investigation of how such persistent behavioral competence may complement higher-level capabilities in embodied agents.

</details>


### [65] [Dynamic Manipulation with World-Action Models via Counterfactual Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33172)
*Sunwoo Park,Wonbin Lee,Seonghyun Jin,Youngmin Kim,Jangho Park,Jong Chul Ye*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出动态预测规划（DPP），通过将动态操作建模为反事实规划，解耦"规划生成用的上下文"与"执行用的物理状态"，解决World-Action模型在操作移动目标时的"目标响应坍缩"问题，无需动态数据再训练即可在单张消费级GPU上实现实时动态操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于静态示教训练的World-Action模型（WAMs）即便具备所需操作技能，也难以操作移动目标。作者将这一失败归因于"目标响应坍缩"（target-response collapse）：随着执行推进，策略越来越偏向于已学到的、对当前行为的延续，而对目标位置的改变响应性下降。因此需要弥合"模型已学到的内容"与"其能从当前上下文生成的内容"之间的差距。

Method: DPP首先利用WAM的预测式rollout估计交互预计发生的时间点，并结合观测到的目标运动，预测目标未来的交互位置；随后构造一个"反事实观测"——将预测的目标位置置于熟悉的机器人上下文中，使模型调用已有的操作技能，而非在不熟悉的机器人-目标构型下生成恢复行为；最后将生成的计划在执行阶段与机器人真实状态相连接。

Result: DPP无需在动态数据上额外训练，即可在单张消费级GPU上实现实时动态操作。仿真与真实机器人实验表明，在多种目标运动下均取得一致的性能提升，仿真性能超过所有对比基线，包括额外在动态数据上训练的方法。

Conclusion: 将动态操作重构为反事实规划、并解耦规划上下文与执行物理状态，是缓解目标响应坍缩的有效途径，使静态示教训练的WAM无需动态数据即可胜任动态操作任务。

Abstract: World-Action models (WAMs) trained on static demonstrations often fail to manipulate moving targets even when they possess the required manipulation skills. We attribute this failure to target-response collapse: as execution advances, the policy becomes increasingly biased toward the learned continuation of its ongoing behavior and less responsive to target relocation.
  To bridge the gap between what the model has learned and what it can generate from the current context, we formulate dynamic manipulation as counterfactual planning by decoupling the context used for plan generation from the physical state used for execution. Our framework, Dynamic Predictive Planning (DPP), first uses the WAM's predictive rollout to estimate when an interaction is expected to occur, and combines this timing estimate with observed target motion to predict the target's future interaction position. DPP then constructs a counterfactual observation that places this predicted target position in a familiar robot context, allowing the model to invoke an existing manipulation skill rather than generate a recovery behavior from an unfamiliar robot-target configuration. The resulting plan is connected to the robot's actual state during execution.
  DPP enables real-time dynamic manipulation on a single consumer GPU without additional training on dynamic data. Experiments in simulation and on a real robot demonstrate consistent improvements across diverse target motions, with simulation performance surpassing all evaluated baselines, including methods additionally trained on dynamic data.
  Project page: https://methoder00.github.io/DPP/

</details>


### [66] [Multi-Terrain Mastery: A Comprehensive Controller for Bipedal Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33174)
*Oluwami Dosunmu-Ogunbi,Aayushi Shrivastava*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种适用于20自由度Cassie双足机器人的多地形统一控制器，通过引入新的impact map，实现对斜坡、湿滑不平草地、沙地、碎石、岩石及楼梯等多种地形的稳定行走。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双足机器人在多样化地形上行走仍是难题。传统步态控制器仅在特定表面表现良好，难以适应多变环境，实际应用受限。由于真实环境具有不可预测性，理想方案是使用单一控制器应对多种地形，从而免去多个专用控制器的需求。

Method: 在既有基于支撑相踝关节电机实现斜坡与崎岖表面稳定的工作基础上，扩展控制器能力。针对新地形的特殊需求，引入一种新的impact map（碰撞/冲击映射），以维持性能和对未见地形的鲁棒性，并详细讨论了用于机器人实时部署的控制结构。

Result: 控制器覆盖湿滑不平陡坡草地，以及沙地、砾石、岩石等柔性地形和楼梯等受限地形，并在20自由度Cassie双足机器人上完成验证。

Conclusion: 所提出的多地形控制器提升了双足运动的通用性与鲁棒性，为单一控制器应对多样复杂地形提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Advancing bipedal robots to navigate diverse terrains remains a significant challenge in robotics. Traditional locomotion controllers excel on specific surfaces but struggle across varied environments, limiting their practical applications. Given the unpredictable nature of real-world environments, a single controller capable of handling multiple terrains is ideal, eliminating the need for multiple specialized controllers. We propose a multi-terrain controller to enhance the versatility and robustness of bipedal locomotion. Building on previous work with a stance ankle motor for stability on inclined and rough surfaces, this paper extends capabilities to steep wet uneven grassy slopes, and compliant terrains such as sand, gravel, rocks, and constrained terrains like staircases. To address the unique demands of these terrains, we introduce a new impact map that is essential for maintaining performance and robustness against unseen terrains. We also discuss in detail the control structure for real-time deployment on the robot. We validate our controller on the 20 degree-of-freedom Cassie bipedal robot.

</details>


### [67] [DeltaWAM: Change-Centric Visual Foresight via Delta Tokens for an Efficient World-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33177)
*Tianyun Jiang,Wenrui Bao,Bingxin Xu,Yu Tian,Yuzhang Shang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DeltaWAM，以“变化量token”为未来预测单元的世界-动作模型，在LIBERO上达92.8%平均成功率，推理142.1 ms/动作块，峰值内存3.86 GB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 像素空间世界-动作模型需反复重建完整未来场景，计算开销大且存在时空冗余；而在物理操作中相邻帧的大部分视觉上下文是共享的，真正需要被动作策略预测的是帧间变化。

Method: 构建以变化为中心的WAM，用单个向量（delta token）编码相邻稠密DINO特征图之间的变化；基于在大规模视频上预训练的潜在世界模型DeltaWorld，自回归地逐帧预测一个delta token；随后由流匹配动作专家以预测的transition与当前DINO特征作为空间锚点，生成动作块。

Result: 在2张H100上训练256 GPU小时，模型0.725B参数；LIBERO平均成功率92.8%；在LIBERO-Pro的程序性扰动下具有稳健泛化；推理每动作块142.1 ms，峰值内存3.86 GB。

Conclusion: 以帧间变化而非像素重建作为未来预测单元，可在显著降低计算与时空冗余的同时保持高操作成功率与泛化能力，为高效世界-动作模型提供了一条有效路径。

Abstract: World-Action Models (WAMs) offer visual foresight for robotic manipulation, but pixel-space models repeatedly reconstruct entire future scenes, incurring high computational cost and spatio-temporal redundancy. In physical manipulation, consecutive frames often share most of their visual context; the changes between them are what an action policy needs to anticipate. We introduce DeltaWAM, a change-centric WAM that makes a compact delta token the unit of future prediction. Each token is a single vector encoding changes between consecutive dense DINO feature maps. DeltaWAM builds on DeltaWorld, a latent world model pretrained on large-scale videos, to autoregressively predict one delta token per future frame. A flow-matching action expert then conditions on the predicted transitions and current DINO features, which serve as spatial anchors, to generate action chunks. Trained for 256 GPU hours on two H100 GPUs, DeltaWAM has 0.725B parameters and achieves 92.8% average success on LIBERO. It also shows robust generalization under procedural perturbations on LIBERO-Pro. Inference takes 142.1 ms per action chunk with 3.86 GB peak memory.

</details>


### [68] [TAO-DA: Towards Autonomous Operation--A Dual-Arm Vision-Language-Action Model for Coordinated Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33197)
*Yongsheng Zhao,Han Gao,Baoping Cheng,Jingyao Tang,Dian Zhou,Deng Liang,Ji Ge,Xuanzhang Wen,Lei Zhao,Ye Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出对称双臂专家（DAE）架构，在共享VLM骨干上解耦双臂专家塔，通过两阶段意图路由与任务进度预测模块，缓解双臂交叉干扰并支持多机器人协同调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型缺乏对双臂状态与意图的显式解耦机制，导致非预期的跨臂干扰，降低任务执行成功率。

Method: 对称双臂专家（DAE）架构：共享VLM骨干 + 解耦的臂专属专家塔；两阶段双臂意图路由（第一阶段依显式语言指令，第二阶段依隐式视觉语义）；轻量任务进度预测模块，通过预分块时序特征与本体感知/视觉观测语义表示的交叉注意力估计逐帧任务完成进度，用于任务进度同步与多机器人协同调度。

Result: 实验验证了模型在双臂意图路由与跨臂干扰解耦方面的有效性；初步显示单臂到双臂（及反向）任务的涌现技能泛化能力，以及跨臂运动域技能迁移。

Conclusion: DAE通过专家解耦与两阶段路由有效抑制跨臂干扰，任务进度预测支持协同调度，并展现出技能泛化与跨臂迁移潜力。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models provide a unified framework for grounding high-level semantic information into low-level robot actions, enabling scalable robotic manipulation across diverse tasks. However, existing VLA models lack explicit mechanisms to disentangle the states and intents of the two arms, leading to unintended cross-arm interference that degrades task execution success. To address this issue, we propose a symmetric Dual-Arm Expert (DAE) architecture built upon a shared Vision-Language Model (VLM) backbone with decoupled, arm-specific expert towers. Expert selection is carried out through a two-stage dual-arm intent routing scheme, in which experts are routed either by explicit language instructions in the first stage or by implicit visual semantics in the second stage. Moreover, we introduce a lightweight task progress prediction module that leverages cross-attention between the pre-chunk temporal features and semantic representations of proprioceptive and visual observations to accurately estimate frame-wise task completion progress. This module facilitates task progress synchronization to support coordinated scheduling for collaborative multi-robot tasks. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of our model in dual-arm intent routing and the disentanglement of cross-arm interference, and further provide preliminary evidence of emergent skill generalization from single- to dual-arm tasks (as well as the reverse), together with cross-arm motion-domain skill transfer.

</details>


### [69] [Learning with Object-centric Representations of Tactile Interactive Perception for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33235)
*Xinyi Yang,Zilin Si,Zhuowei Xu,Zeynep Temel,Oliver Kroemer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出以物体为中心、情境感知的机器人操作框架，通过触觉探索学习任务无关的物体表征，并作为语义情境指导操作策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料、容器内容物、软硬度等隐式物体属性难以从视觉直接推断，需借助触觉感知与探索性交互获取；但触觉信号瞬态且稀疏，如何提取有效触觉事件并融入机器人操作仍是挑战。

Method: 框架包含：token learner 在长时程探索中自主选取代表性触觉片段；通过对比学习与描述性文本嵌入对齐，构建可捕捉多种物理属性的隐空间；将学习到的表征作为语义情境，指导以物体为中心的操作策略，并依据物体属性自适应调整策略。

Result: 学到的表征在已见与未见物体上分别达到 93% 和 84% 的属性估计准确率；在多物体重排、倒液、开箱三个涉及视觉歧义物体的任务上，相较无物体情境条件的基线策略，整体任务成功率在已见物体上从 41% 提升至 92%，在未见物体上从 19% 提升至 67%。

Conclusion: 触觉探索驱动的任务无关物体表征可有效补足视觉歧义，显著提升目标选择与依赖属性的操作适应能力，并在已见与未见物体上均带来大幅性能提升。

Abstract: Implicit object properties that are difficult to directly infer from vision, such as material, container contents, or softness, can be revealed through tactile sensing and exploratory interactions. However, because tactile signals are transient and sparse, extracting informative tactile events and effectively incorporating them into robotic manipulation remains a challenge. In this work, we present an object-centric context-aware manipulation framework that learns task-agnostic object representations through tactile exploration. A token learner autonomously selects representative tactile segments from long-horizon exploration, while contrastive alignment with descriptive text embeddings enables a latent space that captures multiple physical object properties. These learned representations are then used as semantic context to guide object-centric manipulation policies and adapt strategies based on object properties. Experiments show that the learned representations achieve 93% and 84% property estimation accuracy on seen and unseen objects. Evaluated on three tasks involving visually ambiguous objects, i.e. multi-object rearrangement, pouring, and box opening, the proposed framework improves both target selection and property-dependent manipulation adaptation, raising task success, aggregated over all evaluation trials, from 41% to 92% on seen objects and from 19% to 67% on unseen objects over baseline policies without object-context conditioning. Videos and additional results are available at https://xinyiyxyx.github.io/tactile-object-centric/.

</details>


### [70] [SurgFlow: 3D Object-Centric Contact Flow for Surgical Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33237)
*Changwei Chen,Xiao Liang,Yinuo Yang,Nicole Shen,Peihan Zhang,Sara Wickenhiser,Zekai Liang,Soofiyan Atar,Michael Yip*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SurgFlow从双目手术视频中无动作标签地学习3D物体接触流（预测未来3D轨迹与接触分数），并驱动抓取/释放与末端运动，在dVRK上37/39阶段评估成功，零样本迁移到人形腹腔镜机器人达85%/70%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人仅完成约1%的手术，配对的视频-动作数据稀缺；仅视频数据丰富但缺少动作标签。现有3D物体流方法虽具具身无关性，但只指定物体如何运动，未指定工具何时何处接触物体，而这一点在手术中至关重要。

Method: 提出SurgFlow，从立体手术视频中学习3D物体中心的接触流。对每个物体点预测未来3D轨迹与接触分数；通过3D跟踪与工具-物体接近度提取目标，训练流匹配生成器进行预测；利用预测的接触触发抓取与释放，并由流优化末端执行器运动。

Result: 在dVRK上，SurgFlow在组织牵拉、双手显露、拾针与递针等任务的39次阶段评估中成功37次，优于使用同等数据（有或无动作标签）训练的基线。零样本迁移至人形腹腔镜机器人，在相似与新颖相机视角下平均成功率分别为85%和70%。

Conclusion: 将物体流扩展为物体中心的接触流，可在无动作标签下同时回答“如何移动”“何时何处接触”，为手术机器人从丰富视频数据中习得自主行为提供了具身无关且可跨平台迁移的有效路径。

Abstract: Paired video-action demonstrations enable autonomous surgical behavior, but such data is scarce: robots perform roughly 1% of surgeries, while video-only data is abundant. Learning 3D object flow offers an embodiment-agnostic way to utilize video data, but flow alone specifies how an object should move, not where and when the tool should engage it, a distinction that is critical in surgery. We introduce SurgFlow, a framework that learns 3D Object-Centric Contact Flow from stereo surgical video without action labels. For each object point, it predicts a future 3D trajectory and contact scores. We extract targets via 3D tracking and tool-object proximity, train a flow matching generator to predict them, and use predicted contact to trigger grasp and release while optimizing end effector motion from flow. On the da Vinci Research Kit (dVRK), SurgFlow succeeds in 37 of 39 stage evaluations across tissue retraction, bimanual reveal, needle pickup, and handover, outperforming baselines trained on equal data with or without action labels. Zero-shot transfer to a humanoid-based laparoscopic robot achieves 85% and 70% average success under similar and novel camera viewpoints, respectively.

</details>


### [71] [ActionGround: Training-Free Runtime Refinement of Frozen VLA Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33256)
*Namai Chandra,Madhur Thareja,Shriram Damodaran,Addison Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ActionGround 是一种无需训练的神经符号运行时层，包裹冻结的 VLA 策略，通过符号化阶段状态机与惯性加权欧拉-拉格朗日项修正控制指令，在毫秒级开销下提升操作成功率、稳定性与轨迹效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型直接将视觉与语言映射为动作，未显式建模操作任务的阶段结构和刚体动力学，导致执行过程中的阶段混淆与动力学不一致。

Method: 提出 ActionGround：无需重训练、微调或访问权重，单步开销低于 1 ms。其包含两部分：（1）符号化阶段感知有限状态机，识别 approach、grasp、transport、place 四个阶段并施加阶段专属的规则修正；（2）始终启用的惯性加权欧拉-拉格朗日项，将机器人运动方程引入控制步，其动力学残差作为一致性诊断记录而非门控条件。

Result: 在 7-DoF Franka Panda 上，针对 OpenVLA、OpenVLA-OFT、Force-VLA、Generalist-VLA 四个骨干与十个 LIBERO-Spatial 抓放任务评估：成功率最高提升 6 个百分点，稳定性最高提升 19.3 个百分点，轨迹效率最高提升 15%。Robosuite 噪声扫描中轨迹抖动鲁棒性约提升 10 倍；匹配随机种子的仿真对照中，基线成功率由 35% 提升至 95%。

Conclusion: ActionGround 以极低开销有效增强冻结 VLA 策略的阶段一致性与动力学一致性。评估限于刚体抓放操作，真实机械臂实验仅为定性部署演示，逐次试验的定量成功率留待后续工作。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models map visual observations and language instructions directly to robot actions, but they do not explicitly represent the phase structure of manipulation tasks or the rigid-body dynamics governing execution. We present ActionGround, a neuro-symbolic, training-free runtime layer that wraps a frozen VLA policy without retraining, fine-tuning, or weight access, adding less than 1 ms of overhead per control step. A symbolic phase-aware finite-state machine identifies the manipulation phase (approach, grasp, transport, or place) and applies a phase-specific rule-based correction. In parallel, an always-on, inertia-weighted Euler-Lagrange term incorporates the robot's equations of motion into each control step, while its dynamics residual is logged as a consistency diagnostic rather than used as a gate.
  We evaluate ActionGround across OpenVLA, OpenVLA-OFT, Force-VLA, and Generalist-VLA on ten LIBERO-Spatial pick-and-place tasks using a 7-DoF Franka Panda. With fixed parameters across tasks and backbones, ActionGround improves success rate by up to 6 percentage points and stability by up to 19.3 percentage points, while improving trajectory efficiency by up to 15%. In a separate Robosuite noise sweep, ActionGround provides approximately a 10x improvement in trajectory-jerk robustness under injected action noise. In a matched-seed Robosuite simulation companion to a real Agilex Piper trial, simulated baseline success increases from 35% to 95%. The physical-hardware experiment is presented as a qualitative deployment demonstration; quantitative per-trial success on the real arm is left for future work. Our evaluation is limited to rigid-object pick-and-place manipulation.

</details>


### [72] [PORTER: Edge-Cloud Residency for Persistent 3D Scene Graph Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33258)
*Yue Chang,Yifan Tian,Jiajing Peng,Dazhi Huang,Rufeng Chen,Zhaofan Zhang,Li Chen,Sihong Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PORTER 将 3DSG 的持久化与驻留解耦：轻量锚点留在边缘机器人内存，重型对象载荷在边缘与云之间迁移，并用 ISE 指标按字节收益评估卸载优先级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有任务驱动与即时（just-in-time）3DSG 方法只按任务相关性构建/激活信息，虽缩小了单任务工作集，但无法约束机器人全生命周期的机载内存：任务切换后，早期任务的载荷当前无关却未来可能复用，保留则本地内存持续增长，丢弃则需昂贵重建。

Method: 提出 PORTER，实现持久化与驻留解耦：轻量锚点常驻边缘内存，重型对象载荷在边缘与云之间迁移。因相关性不足以决定驻留（多个相关载荷可能信息冗余），将任务分解为功能需求，提出 Irreplaceable Support Erasure (ISE)，度量卸载导致的需求覆盖损失；ISE 对可替代支撑做折扣，并在需求剩余覆盖较弱时对损失施加更强惩罚。基于此，反复选择“每字节边际 ISE 最小”的载荷卸载，构建预算感知的本地工作集。
