Result: 在 JITOMA-Bench 上对四种 3DSG 构建器进行评估；在渐进压缩设置下，卸载 91% 载荷字节时，池化相对 mR@3 仍保持 100%。

Conclusion: 以 ISE 驱动的载荷级驻留决策可在显著降低边缘内存占用（载荷字节卸载至 91%）的同时保持检索性能不降，为长期运行的机器人 3DSG 提供了内存可扩展的边缘-云协同方案。

Abstract: Recent task-driven and just-in-time 3D Scene Graph (3DSG) methods reduce per-task representations by constructing or activating only task-relevant information. Yet sparse per-task working sets do not bound onboard memory usage over a robot's lifetime: as tasks change, payloads accumulated for earlier tasks may become irrelevant to the current task but can be useful again in future tasks. Over repeated task switches and expanding environments, retaining such reusable payloads causes local memory to grow, whereas discarding them entirely can lead to costly repeated construction of the same payloads later. We introduce PORTER, which decouples persistence from residency: lightweight anchors remain in the limited memory of the edge robot while heavy object payloads migrate between the edge and the cloud. Relevance alone is insufficient for deciding residency because multiple relevant payloads may provide redundant information. We therefore decompose each task into functional requirements and introduce Irreplaceable Support Erasure (ISE), which measures the loss in requirement coverage caused by offloading. ISE discounts replaceable support and penalizes losses more strongly when the remaining coverage of a requirement is weak. PORTER constructs a budget-aware local working set by repeatedly offloading the payload with the smallest marginal ISE per byte. Experiments on JITOMA-Bench evaluate PORTER across four 3DSG builders. Under progressive compression, pooled relative mR@3 remains at 100% through 91% payload-byte offloading.

</details>


### [73] [Q-WAM: 4-Bit Quantization of World Action Models with Action-Subspace Protection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33269)
*Arash Akbari,Arman Akbari,Jingwu Luo,Yuhao Lei,Yi Gao,Weiwei Chen,Xuan Zhang,Zhenman Fang,Geng Yuan,Yanzhi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Q-WAM，一种面向世界动作模型（WAM）的4比特权重-激活后训练量化方法，通过动作可观测格拉姆矩阵与动作子空间保护，在保持动作生成精度的同时显著降低显存与计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WAM通过迭代扩散联合生成视频与机器人动作，在操作任务上表现优异，但计算与内存开销巨大，部署困难。现有PTQ方法（如平滑、旋转）难以维持动作生成的精度，因此需要针对动作生成特性设计专门的量化方案。

Method: 提出Q-WAM：1）引入动作可观测格拉姆矩阵（AOG），度量每层输入通道加权组合的舍入误差经所有去噪步骤后对最终动作的影响；2）设计动作子空间保护（ASP），将少数最动作敏感的信道组合保留在16比特低秩分支中，其余权重与激活量化为4比特，均为可在GPU上高效运行的稠密矩阵乘法；3）通过聚合各专家层上的AOG动作质量，识别对生成动作最重要的专家，仅对这些专家应用ASP以最小化开销。

Result: 在三个WAM上于仿真与真实部署中评估。RoboTwin 2.0基准上平均成功率89.6–93.0%，距16比特模型仅1.1个百分点，量化块内存降低3.1–3.4倍；优于最强基线SVDQuant 2.5–8.7个百分点。在Unitree G1人形与双臂UR3机器人上，成功率较SVDQuant提升12.8–17.6个百分点。

Conclusion: Q-WAM通过动作敏感度驱动的4比特量化，在保持动作生成质量的同时大幅降低WAM部署开销，并在仿真与真实机器人上验证了其优于现有PTQ基线的有效性。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) jointly generate video and robot actions through iterative diffusion and perform strongly in robotic manipulation. However, their prohibitive compute and memory costs pose substantial deployment challenges. Post-training quantization (PTQ) can reduce these costs, but existing PTQ methods such as smoothing and rotation are insufficient to maintain the precision of action generation. To overcome this limitation, we propose Q-WAM, a new 4-bit weight-activation quantization for WAMs that preserves the actions the model generates. Specifically, we introduce the \textit{Action Observability Gramian (AOG)}, which measures how much rounding errors in each weighted combination of a layer's input channels change the final action through all denoising steps. We also develop Action-Subspace Protection (ASP), which keeps the few most action-sensitive channel combinations in a tiny 16-bit low-rank branch and quantizes the complementary weights and activations to 4 bits, both as dense matrix multiplications that run efficiently on GPUs. Finally, to preserve action quality with minimal overhead, we identify the experts that matter most for the generated action by aggregating the AOG-derived action mass across the layers of each expert and apply ASP only to those experts. We evaluate Q-WAM on three WAMs, both in simulation and in real-world deployment. On the RoboTwin 2.0 benchmark, it reaches 89.6--93.0\% average success rate, within 1.1 percentage points of the 16-bit models, while reducing the memory of the quantized blocks by 3.1--3.4$\times$. Our method outperforms the strongest baseline, SVDQuant, by 2.5--8.7 percentage points. On a Unitree G1 humanoid and a bimanual UR3 robot, it improves success over SVDQuant by 12.8-17.6 percentage points.

</details>


### [74] [AquaWAM: A Dynamics-aware World Action Model for Underwater Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33299)
*Cunhao Zhu,Yifeng Wang,Dongliang Xu,Yunzhong Hou,Yue Yao,Chi Harold Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 AquaWAM，这是首个面向水下具身智能体的世界动作模型，通过建模动作条件与被动物理动力学（推进器死区、惯性滑行、环境水流），在降低模型规模与计算成本的同时提升了水下任务成功率与决策速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAMs）主要用于预测动作条件下的视觉观测，未显式建模水下机器人常见的被动动力学，如惯性、浮力、水动力阻力与持续漂移，这些效应在动作完成后仍影响载体运动。因此，水下具身智能体亟需能够同时捕捉动作条件与被动物理动力学的世界动作模型。

Method: AquaWAM 不预测未来图像，而是建模动作条件与被动物理动力学，包括推进器死区、超出指令时长的惯性滑行以及环境水流。感知方面，通过 DVL、IMU、压力传感器与关节编码器进行状态感知，相机仅提供目标理解与位姿估计所需的语义信息。通过建模紧凑的导航状态而非高维视觉观测，显著降低模型规模与计算成本。

Result: 在 USIM 基准的 20 项水下任务中，AquaWAM 取得 72.6% 的任务成功率，优于现有方法；在 NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin 上，其动作决策速度比 U0 快 2.7 倍。在部分机载传感器测量不可用时仍保持有效性，例如缺少 DVL 速度测量时，成功率仍达 61.6%，而 U0 仅为 39.4%。

Conclusion: AquaWAM 作为首个面向水下具身智能体的世界动作模型，通过显式建模被动动力学与紧凑导航状态，实现了优于现有方法的任务成功率与更高的决策效率，并在传感器缺失条件下表现出鲁棒性，为水下具身智能提供了一种高效且可扩展的世界动作建模范式。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) are becoming increasingly important and useful for embodied intelligence, as they enable robots to anticipate the consequences of candidate actions before interacting with the physical environment. However, underwater robots are usually subject to passive dynamics, such as inertia, buoyancy, hydrodynamic drag, and persistent drift, which can continue to affect the vehicle even after an action is completed. Existing WAMs, which primarily predict action-conditioned visual observations, are not explicitly designed to capture such passive motion dynamics. In this paper, we present AquaWAM, the first World Action Model designed for underwater embodied agents. Instead of predicting future images, AquaWAM models both action-conditioned and passive physical dynamics, including the thruster dead band, the inertial glide that outlasts each command, and ambient currents. Specifically, it senses through the DVL, IMU, pressure sensor and joint encoders, while cameras supply only semantics for understanding goals and target pose. By modeling compact navigation states rather than high-dimensional visual observations, AquaWAM substantially reduces the model size and computational cost compared with conventional WAMs. Experimentally, AquaWAM achieves a 72.6% task success rate across 20 underwater tasks on the USIM benchmark, outperforming existing methods while making action decisions 2.7x faster than U0 on an NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin. Our model also remains effective when some onboard sensor measurements are unavailable. For example, without DVL velocity measurements, our method still achieves a 61.6% success rate, compared with 39.4% for U0.

</details>


### [75] [CompliantWBC: Whole-Body Compliance for Heavy Humanoids via Force Latent Estimation and Residual Impedance Targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33310)
*Tan-Dzung Do,Cuc T. Trinh,Tuan Dat Phuong,Chien Le,Thanh Ly,Vien Anh Ngo,An Thai Le*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CompliantWBC，一种面向重型人形机器人的全身柔顺控制框架，通过RL基策略、有界残差策略与力原点采样课程，实现任意部位受扰下的高保真柔顺，仿真偏差2.58cm并在真实平台上完成多任务验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重型人形机器人在以人为中心的环境中承载大载荷时，全身柔顺控制至关重要。但现有基于学习的力感知流水线多聚焦于末端阻力、上半身逐连杆弹簧或末端刚度调制，未解决重型平台上任意部位扰动且需下肢参与的问题。

Method: CompliantWBC包含三部分：(1) 以多部位全身阻抗参考控制器引导，用RL训练基策略最大化柔顺保真度奖励，将经典笛卡尔阻抗扩展到任意受控连杆；(2) 冻结基策略上的有界残差策略，编辑逐连杆阻抗平衡点，修正由梯度屏障后协同训练的力编码器提供的粗但有结构的力旋量估计；(3) 采用Phong加权力原点采样器与轴解耦骨盆锚点，通过两个可解释参数实现含下肢的柔顺课程训练。

Result: 在仿真中与柔顺及刚性基线对比，取得最佳柔顺保真度，相对解析解偏差2.58cm；在真实重型人形机器人上展示静态/动态力反应、擦板、负重深蹲与协同载荷运输等任务。

Conclusion: CompliantWBC填补了重型平台任意部位扰动与下肢参与柔顺控制的空白，兼具高保真度与可解释性，并通过实物多任务验证了实用性与泛化能力。

Abstract: Whole-body compliant control is essential for deploying heavy humanoids under high payload in human-centric environments. Most prior force-aware learning-based pipelines focus on end-effector resistance, per-link upper-body springs, or end-effector stiffness modulation, leaving arbitrary-site perturbations on heavy platforms with lower-body engagement largely unaddressed. We close this gap with CompliantWBC comprising: (1) A base policy trained with RL to maximize compliance-fidelity reward, guided by a multi-site whole-body impedance reference controller, extending classical Cartesian impedance to any controlled link; (2) A bounded residual policy that edits the per-link impedance equilibrium over a frozen base, correcting the coarse but structured wrench estimate supplied by a force encoder co-trained behind a gradient barrier; (3) A Phong-weighted force-origin sampler with an axis-decoupled pelvis anchor induces lower-body-inclusive compliance curriculum training via two interpretable parameters. We evaluate CompliantWBC in simulation against both compliant and stiff baselines, achieving best compliant fidelity of 2.58cm deviation from analytical solutions, and demonstrate it on a real heavy humanoid across static/dynamic force reaction, board wiping, squat under payload, and cooperative payload transport. Project website: https://dotandung.github.io/compliantwbc/

</details>


### [76] [SocialHumanoid: Towards Expressive Humanoid Behavior via One-Step Co-Speech Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33311)
*Chengqun Yang,Tengjie Zhu,Liang Xu,Fulong Liu,Guanzhu Ren,Yitong Xing,Xuefeng Lu,Fei Shi,Siyuan Fan,Weijie Dong,Yao Mu,Xiaokang Yang,Yichao Yan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 SocialHumanoid：基于一步式（one-step）共语语音动作生成的人形机器人表达性行为系统，配合自建 4 小时 AffectMoCap 情感动作数据集，实现情感可控、低延迟、可实机连续执行的全身动作生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人需在面对面交互中生成与语音同步、具情感表达力且可实时执行的躯体行为。但现有共语语音（co-speech）方法主要面向数字人，缺乏对情感控制与低延迟连续生成的联合支持，且难以部署到物理实体。

Method: 1) 以回复语音与指定情感条件为输入，单次前向传播生成完整全身动作窗口，并通过动作历史条件（motion-history conditioning）串联相邻窗口；2) 将生成的人体动作在线转换为与机器人本体兼容的参考轨迹，由全身控制器跟踪执行；3) 构建 AffectMoCap 数据集：两名专业演员、4 小时，含同步语音、身体动作、精细手部动作及情感标注，用于情感躯体表达的显式监督。

Result: 在 BEAT2 上取得对比方法中最优 FGD，语音-动作同步性具竞争力，相同协议下推理速度约为 GestureLSM 的 6 倍；感知实验表明 AffectMoCap 训练可提升生成动作的情感识别率；真机实验验证了连续的情感条件化行为与稳定的长时程执行。

Conclusion: 通过一步式共语动作生成与情感标注数据集，SocialHumanoid 弥合了数字人动作生成与物理人形机器人执行之间的差距，实现情感可控、低延迟、可长时程稳定运行的表达性人形机器人行为。

Abstract: Humanoid robots are increasingly expected to serve as embodied social agents that communicate naturally with humans through face-to-face interaction. During such communication, humanoid robots require body behaviors that are synchronized with speech, affectively expressive, and suitable for real-time execution. However, existing co-speech methods are primarily developed for digital humans and lack joint support for affective control and low-latency continuous generation on physical embodiments. To bridge this gap, we present SocialHumanoid, a system for expressive humanoid behavior via one-step co-speech motion generation. Given response speech and a specified affective condition, SocialHumanoid generates each full-body motion window in a single forward pass and connects successive windows through motion-history conditioning. The generated human motion is further converted online into embodiment-compatible robot references and tracked by a whole-body controller for physical execution. To provide explicit supervision for affective body expression, we further introduce AffectMoCap, a 4-hour dataset captured from two professional actors, containing synchronized speech, body motion, fine-grained hand motion, and emotion annotations. On BEAT2, SocialHumanoid achieves the best FGD among the compared generation methods, competitive speech-motion synchrony, and approximately $6\times$ faster inference than GestureLSM under the same protocol. Perceptual evaluations further show that training with AffectMoCap improves affect recognition from generated body motion, while real-robot experiments demonstrate continuous affect-conditioned behavior and stable long-horizon execution. Our project page is https://rex0191.github.io/SocialHumanoid/.

</details>


### [77] [Traceable Human-to-Humanoid Sign Language Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33354)
*Ao Liu,Shengeng Tang,Lechao Cheng,Yanbin Hao,Bingkun Bao,Richang Hong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 HumanoidCSL-20K，一个包含 20,648 条句级中文手语序列的数据集与基准，每条序列提供源视频、修复后人体运动、直接机器人参考及几何修复后机器人参考四种对齐版本，实现跨表征的可溯源转换，并配套评估人体运动连续性、机器人参考可行性与实际执行性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手语数据采集成本高、遥操作难以规模化，因此需要复用大规模视频语料；但将视频导出的人体运动转换为机器人轨迹时，拟合、人体运动修复、重定向、机器人几何修复与控制等环节的误差难以仅凭最终轨迹区分，缺乏可溯源的数据与评测手段。

Method: 构建 HumanoidCSL-20K 数据集与基准，每一步转换保留可溯源记录：对每条手语句子生成源、修复后人体运动、直接机器人参考、几何修复后机器人参考四个对齐版本；采用观测支持的局部人体运动修复与全机器人几何修复；设计配对评估，分别度量人体运动连续性、内容保持、机器人参考可行性与物理执行；提出手语专用运动学参考协议，在完整规划运动上评估手形、位置、掌心朝向与双手关系。

Result: 全语料实验显示，修复后异常手臂/手部步态减少，双手间及手-身体穿透降低；控制实验将参考可学习性与课程学习效应分离；分量得分揭示了仍存在的执行误差。

Conclusion: 该数据集与基准通过跨表征溯源与多层次配对评测，为从视频到人形机器人手语生成的各处理阶段提供了可分解的误差分析框架，有助于复用视频语料并改进机器人手语执行质量。

Abstract: Sign data collection is costly, and teleoperation scales poorly, motivating reuse of large video corpora. Humanoid signing requires converting video-derived human motion into robot trajectories while preserving linguistic motion cues. Errors from fitting, human-motion repair, retargeting, robot geometry repair, and control are hard to separate from the final trajectory alone. We introduce HumanoidCSL-20K, a dataset and benchmark of 20,648 sentence-level Chinese Sign Language sequences, each with four aligned versions: the source, the repaired human motion, the direct robot reference, and the geometry-repaired robot reference. Observation-supported local human-motion repair, full-robot geometry repair, and cross-representation provenance make each transformation traceable. Paired evaluations measure human-motion continuity and content preservation, robot-reference feasibility, and physical execution. A sign-specific kinematic-reference protocol scores handshape, location, palm orientation, and inter-hand relation over the full planned motion. Full-corpus results show fewer abnormal arm / hand steps and less inter-hand and hand-body penetration after repair. Control experiments separate reference learnability from curriculum effects, while component scores expose remaining execution errors.

</details>


### [78] [Recursive Harness Distillation across Agents for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33378)
*Seungyeon Kim,Junhoo Lee,Minkyu Kim,Baekseung Kim,Nojun Kwak*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出递归式工具带蒸馏（Recursive Harness Distillation）方法，将强智能体的干预经验蒸馏为可复用的"playbook"，通过轻量智能体的执行反馈递归精炼，在不更新模型参数的情况下提升VLA模型的操作成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型具备广泛的操作能力，但在执行需要诊断失败并自适应调整行为时表现不佳。因此需要一种机制，让智能体通过交互发现有效的干预手段，并将这类经验沉淀下来以便在新任务中复用。

Method: 递归式工具带蒸馏：先由一个强智能体将其干预经验蒸馏成 playbook 交给轻量智能体；再利用轻量智能体的执行反馈递归精炼该 playbook。playbook 作为可复用指导，使智能体在新任务实例中无需更新模型参数即可复用积累的干预知识。

Result: 真实世界操作任务成功率由 37.3% 提升至 64.0%；在 SimplerEnv Bridge 上，搭载 playbook 的轻量智能体达到 66.7% 成功率，而仅用 GR00T 的基线为 41.7%，且超过无 playbook 的强智能体；同一 playbook 也使强智能体提升至 79.2%。

Conclusion: 结果验证了工具带蒸馏在机器人领域的可行性：干预经验能够被积累、经执行反馈精炼，并跨智能体复用，从而提升操作性能。

Abstract: A central goal in robotics is to enable manipulation across changing tasks and environments. Vision-language-action (VLA) models provide broad manipulation capabilities but can struggle when execution requires diagnosing failures and adapting behavior. Strong agents can discover effective interventions through interaction with these policies. We propose Recursive Harness Distillation to accumulate this experience as reusable guidance across agents. A strong agent distills its experience into a playbook for a light agent, then recursively refines the playbook using the light agent's execution feedback. The resulting playbook enables agents to reuse accumulated intervention knowledge in new task instances without updating model parameters. In real-world manipulation, the harness improves success from 37.3% to 64.0%. On SimplerEnv Bridge, the light agent with the playbook achieves 66.7% success, compared with 41.7% for the GR00T-only baseline, and outperforms the strong agent without a playbook. The same playbook also benefits the strong agent, which reaches 79.2% success. These results demonstrate the feasibility of harness distillation for robotics: intervention experience can be accumulated, refined through execution, and reused across agents to improve manipulation.

</details>


### [79] [Large Language Models for Model-Based Robot Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33423)
*Andrew Wilhelm,Angelina Zhao,Nils Napp*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种框架：用LLM构建显式工程模型（物理关系、兼容性约束、目标函数），使建模假设可被检视与修订，再进行多目标优化以获得可行性与Pareto最优性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM可直接为机器人设计提供工程知识，但其生成的设计依赖隐式假设，无法保证可行性或最优性；不同合理的建模选择会实质改变可行与最优设计集合，因此需要将建模选择显式化以便检视。

Method: 框架利用LLM构建包含物理关系、兼容性约束与目标的显式工程模型；模型可在形式化优化前补充工程、制造商或系统特定信息，随后通过多目标优化在最终模型与指定设计空间上给出可行性与Pareto最优性保证。在四旋翼与循迹机器人部件选型问题上进行评估。

Result: 30次直接LLM设计试验中，无一能在对应最终模型下被验证为可行；与独立开发的专家模型及模型逐步精化阶段的对比表明，建模假设变化会显著改变预测的可行设计与Pareto最优设计集合。

Conclusion: 以LLM构建显式工程模型可使底层设计选择在决定优化设计之前被检视与修订，为融合LLM工程知识、系统特定信息与形式化设计优化提供了接口。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) can contribute useful engineering knowledge to robot design, but directly generated designs may rely on implicit assumptions and provide no guarantees of feasibility or optimality. These assumptions are critical because different reasonable modeling choices can materially change which designs are predicted to be feasible or optimal. We therefore present a framework that uses LLMs to construct explicit engineering models containing physical relationships, compatibility constraints, and objectives, allowing these modeling choices to be inspected and revised before formal optimization. The model can then be updated with additional engineering, manufacturer, or system-specific information before formal multi-objective optimization provides feasibility and Pareto-optimality guarantees with respect to the finalized model and specified design space. We evaluate the framework on quadcopter and line-following robot component-selection problems. Across 30 direct LLM design trials, none could be verified as feasible under the corresponding finalized model. Comparisons with an independently developed expert model and successive stages of model refinement further showed that changes in modeling assumptions substantially altered the predicted feasible and Pareto-optimal design sets. Together, these results show that using LLMs to construct explicit engineering models makes the underlying design choices available for inspection and revision before those assumptions determine the optimized designs. Explicit modeling therefore provides an interface for combining LLM-generated engineering knowledge, system-specific information, and formal design optimization.

</details>


### [80] [VIDEAS: Distilling Explicit Action Semantics from Demonstration Videos for World Models via Prior-Guided Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33464)
*Jianan Wang,Haoquan Zhai,Siyang Zhang,Bin Li,Juan Chen,Jingtao Qi,Zhuo Zhang,Enze Wang,Haoxiang Jin,Chen Qian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VIDEAS数据蒸馏框架，将操作视频中的连续物理动态转化为显式动作语义，用于训练语言世界模型VIDEAS-WM（8B/9B），在高层次具身动作语义推理上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型需要内部化环境动态以预测未来状态，但现有方法难以真正内化底层因果物理规律，无法显式推理动作前提条件与后续状态转移，这仍是开放挑战。

Method: VIDEAS将视觉演示解构为离散动作轨迹，利用先进VLM提取包含动作前提与效果的结构化知识；引入基于文本环境的先验引导轨迹模拟机制验证知识物理一致性；加入负轨迹丰富知识完整性并提升数据多样性以缓解认知偏差。基于AgiBot-World数据集蒸馏出34K高质量样本，训练8B/9B参数的VIDEAS-WM语言世界模型套件。

Result: 大量实验表明VIDEAS-WM在高层次具身动作语义推理上取得SOTA性能，展现出深刻的物理理解能力与对未见场景的鲁棒泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过将连续物理动态蒸馏为显式动作语义并结合先验引导验证，可有效提升语言世界模型的物理因果理解与泛化能力，为具身智能动作推理提供了新范式。

Abstract: World models learn internal representations of environment dynamics to predict future states, enabling agents to optimize action plans without physical interactions. However, developing world models that genuinely internalize underlying causal physical laws to explicitly reason about action preconditions and subsequent state transitions remains an open challenge. In this paper, we propose VIDEAS, a data distillation framework that transforms continuous physical dynamics from operational videos into explicit action semantics for foundation models. Specifically, it deconstructs visual demonstrations into discrete action trajectories and utilizes advanced vision-language models (VLMs) to extract structured knowledge encapsulating action preconditions and effects. To ensure physical consistency, we introduce a prior-guided trajectory simulation mechanism grounded within a text-based environment to rigorously validate the extracted knowledge. Notably, we incorporate negative trajectories to enrich knowledge completeness and enhance data diversity to mitigate cognitive bias. Furthermore, we present VIDEAS-WM, an 8B/9B-parameter suite of language-based world models trained on 34K high-quality samples derived from AgiBot-World dataset. Extensive experiments demonstrate that VIDEAS-WM establishes state-of-the-art performance in high-level embodied action semantic reasoning, exhibiting profound physical understanding and robust generalization across unseen scenarios.

</details>


### [81] [AMBIT: Anticipatory Multimodal Body Recruitment for Bimanual Tracking on a Humanoid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33484)
*Hanlong Li,Sihan Tan,Takeshi Ashizawa,Benjamin Yen,Kazuhiro Nakadai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出AMBIT框架：用CVAE生成多样化的全身协同策略，配合非学习选择器与滚动时域滞回提交，解决5-DoF人形手臂无法独立跟踪双臂末端轨迹的问题（MuJoCo下85%成功率）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 5-DoF人形手臂无法独立跟踪通用双臂末端执行器轨迹，必须引入骨盆与腰部运动；但这些运动的选择与时机并非唯一确定，即存在多样化的连续有效策略流形。确定性回归器在该流形上会发生"模式平均"，学到的策略仅35%时间有效，因此需要保留多样性并在此基础上筛选可行策略。

Method: AMBIT由四部分构成：(1) CVAE基于指令轨迹的预览（preview）提出候选策略集合；(2) 非学习的选择器对候选策略进行过滤、排序与验证；(3) 滚动时域循环配合滞回机制提交单一策略；(4) 提交的策略作为全身微分IK二次规划（QP）的参考，与反应式跟踪器共用同一QP，跟踪器保留残余误差的修正权。方法在Unitree R1（固定双足支撑）与Unitree G1上验证，超参数不变。

Result: 在160个存在有效策略的留出episode上（MuJoCo完整动力学+力矩控制器）：AMBIT在3 cm/15°容差下成功率85%，跟踪器为74%（置信区间不重叠）；在48%的episode中先调用身体运动再使手臂饱和，跟踪器为35%。CVAE有效性52%，16个样本中最佳达82%，均优于确定性回归器的35%。五组零样本偏移下AMBIT全面优于热启动跟踪器，并与计算量高17倍的测试时重优化基线持平。在Unitree G1上复现了有效集的结构，相对跟踪器的差距扩大至0.85对0.53。五个策略在外部支撑的实体R1上执行，骨盆行程各异，编码器正运动学显示末端轨迹跟踪中位误差11 mm，验证了运动学可实现性（不涉及平衡）。

Conclusion: 保留策略多样性并以选择代替训练时约束，可使未知约束仅通过选择环节强制满足；AMBIT在仿真与实体平台上均优于纯反应式跟踪器，并在零样本偏移下具备与昂贵重优化相当的性能，证明运动学可实现性。

Abstract: A humanoid with 5-DoF arms cannot track generic bimanual end-effector trajectories with its arms alone; pelvis and waist motion must be recruited, but which motion, and when, is not uniquely determined. On a Unitree R1 in fixed double support, the set of dynamically valid recruitment strategies (pelvis pose and waist trajectories) for a task is a diverse continuous manifold, and a deterministic regressor trained on it mode-averages into strategies valid only 35% of the time, against 52% for a conditional variational autoencoder (CVAE) and 82% for the best of 16 CVAE samples. We introduce AMBIT: the CVAE proposes strategies from a preview of the commanded trajectory, a non-learned selector filters, ranks and verifies them, and a receding-horizon loop commits to one with hysteresis. The committed strategy is the reference of the same whole-body differential-IK QP a reactive tracker runs, which keeps authority over residual error. On 160 held-out episodes that admit a valid strategy, in full MuJoCo dynamics under a torque controller, AMBIT reaches 85% success at a 3 cm/15 deg tolerance against 74% for the tracker (disjoint confidence intervals) and recruits the body before the arms saturate in 48% of episodes against 35%. Because diversity is preserved, constraints unknown at training time are enforced by selection alone: under five zero-shot shifts AMBIT beats the warm-started tracker on every shift and matches a test-time re-optimisation baseline 17x more expensive. On a Unitree G1, with hyperparameters unchanged, the protocol reproduces the structure of the valid set and widens the gap over the tracker to 0.85 against 0.53. Five selected strategies execute on the externally supported physical R1, distinct in pelvis excursion and tracking the planned end-effector motion to a median of 11 mm by encoder forward kinematics, which establishes kinematic realisability, not balance.

</details>


### [82] [AI-Driven Collaborative Assembly Line Inspection: System Integration and Deployment Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33522)
*Asya Ünal,Amr Okasha,Ege Çırakman,Perin Ünal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文报告了在Silverline厨房家电工厂部署的AI辅助协作检测单元：UR 10e协作机器人（机器视觉缺陷检测）与Comau Racer-5（功能测试）通过ROS 2 Humble协同，多模态数据本地记录并Grafana实时可视化，并分析部署挑战与工程解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 装配线人工目视检测是长期存在的制造瓶颈：操作员长时间轮班产生疲劳，导致缺陷检出率下降。

Method: 在一个AI-PRISM项目框架下设计并集成协作检测单元：UR 10e协作机器人搭载机器视觉缺陷检测流程，Comau Racer-5协作机器人执行功能测试；基于Ubuntu 22.04 LTS服务器上的ROS 2 Humble进行协调；采集Basler相机图像、TIA麦克风声学与SPS电气安全测量等多模态数据，本地记录并通过Grafana实时可视化；采用四级人机交互（HRI）分析组织集成，并总结近距离安全、眩光与反射下AI鲁棒性、双机器人ROS 2命名空间冲突及操作系统与依赖问题等挑战及工程解决方案。

Result: 单元部署后：单件质检时间由82 s缩短至61 s（约25%）；最终控制资源效率由0.75提升至0.88；操作员目视检测观看时间减少82%；NASA-TLX显示操作员心理负荷显著降低（p = 0.005）。

Conclusion: 该AI辅助协作检测单元在实际工厂环境中可有效缓解人工目视检测瓶颈，在提升质检效率与资源利用率的同时显著降低操作员视觉负担与心理负荷，验证了所提集成方案与工程解决措施的实用性。

Abstract: Manual visual inspection on assembly lines is a persistent manufacturing bottleneck: operator fatigue over extended shifts lowers defect-detection rates. This paper presents the design, integration, and field deployment of an AI-assisted collaborative inspection cell at the Silverline kitchen-appliance factory, developed within the AI-PRISM project. The cell couples a Universal Robots UR 10e cobot carrying a machine-vision defect-detection pipeline with a Comau Racer-5 cobot for functional tests, coordinated through ROS 2 Humble on an Ubuntu 22.04 LTS server. Multi-modal data (Basler camera imagery, TIA microphone acoustics, and SPS electrical-safety measurements) are logged locally and visualised in real time with Grafana. We report the practical deployment challenges (close-proximity safety, AI robustness under glare and reflections, ROS 2 namespace collisions across two cobots, and operating-system and dependency issues) together with the engineering solutions adopted, and structure the integration through a four-level Human-Robot Interaction analysis. The deployed cell cuts per-unit quality-check time from 82 s to 61 s (about 25%), raises final-control resource efficiency from 0.75 to 0.88, reduces operator visual-inspection viewing time by 82%, and significantly lowers operator mental demand (p = 0.005, NASA-TLX).

</details>


### [83] [A Disk-Shaped Magnetoelastic Torque Sensor for Robotic Joints Using Permanent Magnetization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33542)
*Bruno Brajon,Enrico Gasparin,Nicole Yazigy,Lisa Salamin,Florian Copt,Guzmán Borque Gallego,Elias Klauser,Gaël Close*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于机器人关节的盘状磁致伸缩扭矩传感器，轴向厚度约1 cm，采用四磁力计架构抑制寄生横向力，并通过定制磁屏蔽将外部杂散场降低7倍，在50 Nm量程下精度达1.34 %FS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人领域对直接扭矩传感的需求日益增长，尤其在对精度和交互控制要求较高的场景中，仅依靠电机电流估计扭矩已不够充分；同时传感器需满足机器人关节对紧凑轴向尺寸的集成要求。

Method: 设计盘状磁致伸缩扭矩传感器，轴向封装约1 cm；采用四磁力计架构测量被应力调制的磁场，同时抑制寄生交叉力的影响；对狭窄磁化区域进行磁化；并加入定制磁屏蔽以提升扭矩灵敏度、降低外部杂散场。

Result: 定制磁屏蔽使外部杂散场降低7倍并提升扭矩灵敏度；传感器在50 Nm满量程（FS）下扭矩测量精度为1.34 %FS。文中还给出了机械设计、磁化流程与实验验证。

Conclusion: 结果表明该盘状磁致伸缩扭矩传感器具备用于机器人应用的潜力，兼顾紧凑集成、抗干扰与较高测量精度。

Abstract: Direct torque sensing is a growing need in the robotics community to enable precise control and interactions where torque estimation from motor current is not sufficient. This paper presents a novel disk-shaped magnetoelastic torque sensor with a compact axial envelope of about 1 cm, suitable for integration in robotic joints. A four-magnetometer architecture is used to measure the field modulated by the stress affecting a narrow magnetized region while rejecting the effects of parasitic cross forces. A custom-designed magnetic shield enhances the torque sensitivity while reducing the external stray fields by a factor 7x. The device measures the torque with an accuracy of 1.34 %FS relative to the 50 Nm full scale (FS). The paper details the development of the sensor through the mechanical design, the magnetization procedure, and the experimental validation. The results demonstrate the potential of the proposed sensor for robotic applications.

</details>


### [84] [Steer2Grasp: Inference-Time Embodiment-Aware Steering for Diverse Physically Feasible Grasp Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33546)
*Vignesh Vembar,Ayush Kaura,A Padmaprabhan,Siddharth Sinha,Kailash Nagarajan,Keshab Patra,Md Faizal Karim,K Madhava Krishna*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Steer2Grasp，一种免训练、与具体机器人无关的推理期抓取引导框架，通过Feynman-Kac启发的粒子重加权与重采样，将冻结的笛卡尔抓取扩散模型的概率质量从不可行模式迁移至高奖励可行模式，并统一处理单/双臂抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有抓取扩散模型以物体为中心，生成时易违背机器人本体与环境施加的运动学及碰撞约束；已有的本体感知方法多依赖梯度引导或优化在生成抓取附近做局部修正，难以从根本不可行的模式中恢复。

Method: Steer2Grasp为免训练、本体无关的推理期引导框架，在冻结的笛卡尔抓取扩散模型上使用部署特定奖励。核心机制为Feynman-Kac启发的粒子重加权与重采样，实现群体层面的模式转移，无需修改预训练模型或可微约束。通过可达性与碰撞感知奖励统一处理单臂与双臂抓取，随后进行免梯度的夹爪级局部精修。

Result: 在多样物体、多种机器人本体及受限环境中，方法显著提升可行抓取的生成能力，同时保持与底层抓取先验的接近性。

Conclusion: 通过推理期粒子群体重加权实现模式级转换，可在不重训练、不依赖可微约束的前提下有效注入本体与环境约束，兼顾可行性与先验保真度，并统一支持单/双臂抓取。

Abstract: Current grasp diffusion models provide rich priors for generation, yet their object-centric approach can violate the kinematic and collision constraints imposed by the embodiment and the environment. Existing embodiment-aware methods primarily perform local corrections around generated grasps through gradient guidance or optimization, making it difficult to recover from fundamentally infeasible modes. We present Steer2Grasp, a training-free, embodiment-agnostic framework for inference-time grasp steering that adapts a frozen Cartesian grasp diffusion model using deployment-specific rewards. Through Feynman-Kac (FK) inspired particle reweighting and resampling, the method reallocates population mass from infeasible to high-reward grasp modes, enabling population-level mode transitions without modifying the pretrained diffusion model or requiring differentiable constraints. The framework enables a unified treatment for single and dual arm grasping through reachability and collision aware rewards, followed by gradient free gripper level local refinement. Across diverse objects, robot embodiments, and constrained environments, our method substantially improves feasible grasp generation while maintaining proximity to the underlying grasp prior.

</details>


### [85] [FoLD: Force-Informed Learning for Dexterous Articulated Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33551)
*Haowei Shen,Tingai Li,Yumeng Liu,Wenyuan Guang,Xuanze Yang,Qing Fang,Kai Xu,Ligang Liu,Ruizhen Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FoLD：通过显式力引导实现人类演示向灵巧机器人操作技能迁移的框架，在公开基准与真机上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于人手与机器人手在形态学和接触动力学上的差异，将人类演示迁移到灵巧机器人时，重定向后的运动往往无法产生预期的物体行为，导致操作失败。

Method: FoLD从人类演示与机器人当前交互状态中计算补偿力场，形成促进所演示物体运动先验（force prior）；该力先验用于引导一个残差策略，使重定向后的手部运动适应任务的接触需求。

Result: 在公开的关节物体操作基准上，FoLD在不同任务与不同机器人本体上持续优于最先进基线；并在真实灵巧机器人平台上成功实现人类操作技能向机器人执行的迁移。

Conclusion: 显式力引导可有效弥合形态与接触差异，FoLD为人类演示到灵巧机器人操作技能的迁移提供了可行方案，并具备真机应用能力。

Abstract: Transferring human demonstrations to dexterous robots remains challenging because differences in hand morphology and contact dynamics often cause retargeted motions to fail at producing the intended object behavior. We present \textbf{FoLD}, a framework for learning dexterous manipulation of articulated objects through explicit force guidance. FoLD compute compensatory force fields from human demonstrations together with the robot's current interaction state, yielding a force prior that promotes the demonstrated object motion. This force prior informs a residual policy that adapts retargeted hand motions to the contact requirements of the task. We evaluate FoLD on a public benchmark for articulated object manipulation, where it consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across tasks and embodiments. We further validate FoLD on real dexterous robot platforms, demonstrating successful transfer of human manipulation skills to robot execution. Here is the link of our project page: https://gghgghgghgg.github.io/FoLD-project-page/.

</details>


### [86] [SLIP-VLA: Single-Step Latent Imagination for Policy Learning in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33575)
*Tianfu Li,Haoxuan Xu,Wenbo Chen,Haitian Li,Changchuan Yang,Xinhu Zheng,Jun Ma,Yuan Liu,Lujia Wang,Haoang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SLIP-VLA 提出单步潜在想象框架，为 VLA 模型引入未来感知的动作预测，在仿真与真实任务中达到 SOTA，同时单步潜在想象仅耗时 12 ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型多直接从当前观测预测动作，缺乏对未来场景演化的显式建模；引入未来预测的方法中，稠密未来建模依赖昂贵的迭代去噪，而单步替代方案性能又不及多步版本。因此需要兼顾高效未来建模与强动作性能。

Method: SLIP-VLA 通过单次去噪更新获得时间上稠密的未来潜在表示；通过对齐中间潜在表示与未来几何、语义特征提升其感知充分性；并通过动作条件潜在世界建模与逆动力学建模，显式耦合潜在状态转移与机器人动作，提升控制充分性。

Result: 在多种仿真基准和真实世界操作任务上取得 state-of-the-art 性能，单步潜在想象耗时仅 12 ms。

Conclusion: SLIP-VLA 以单步潜在想象在效率与性能间取得平衡，验证了未来感知潜在建模对 VLA 策略学习的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action models are increasingly effective for robotic manipulation, yet most predict actions directly from current observations without explicitly modeling future scene evolution. Recent methods introduce future prediction to improve action generation, but dense future modeling often requires expensive iterative denoising, while one-step alternatives can underperform their multi-step counterparts. To reconcile efficient future modeling with strong action performance, we present SLIP-VLA, a policy learning framework that equips VLA models with a Single-Step Latent Imagination for future-aware action prediction. SLIP-VLA obtains temporally dense future latent representations with a single denoising update, and we improve the perceptual sufficiency of these representations by aligning intermediate latents with future geometric and semantic features. We further improve their control sufficiency through action-conditioned latent world modeling and inverse dynamics modeling, explicitly coupling latent transitions with robot actions. SLIP-VLA achieves state-of-the-art performance across diverse simulation benchmarks and real-world manipulation tasks, while its single-step latent imagination takes only 12 ms.

</details>


### [87] [Beyond One-Step Accuracy: State-Affine Latent Transition for Reliable Visual Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33595)
*Boyuan Zhang,Yingjun Du,Xiantong Zhen,Ling Shao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出SALT（State-Affine Latent Transition），一种动作条件的状态仿射潜空间动力学模型，通过递归多步展开监督训练，使训练与规划使用方式一致，在四个视觉规划环境中以更差的一步预测精度换取显著更优的闭环规划成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联合嵌入世界模型通常仅以单步预测（在编码状态上）为目标训练，但规划时是递归地把学到的转移作用到自身预测上，因此单步精度无法反映递归展开下的误差传播，需要研究多步展开误差的构成与传播机制。

Method: 作者将多步展开误差分解为各步引入的误差及其后续转移中的传播；证明状态仿射动力学正是具有状态无关雅可比的可微转移，可消除非线性传播残差，使误差传播算子仅依赖动作序列。据此提出SALT：动作同时调制状态变换与加性更新，并以递归多步展开监督训练，将每一步预测潜状态反馈回转移中。

Result: 在四个视觉规划环境中，SALT的一步预测误差比匹配的LeWM基线高1.48–2.19倍，却在每个环境中都提升了闭环成功率，平均提升10.0个百分点；在OGBench-Cube上，执行后模型预测代价急剧上升的失败回合比例从23.3%降至2.0%。

Conclusion: 单步预测精度并非世界模型用于规划时的可靠指标；采用状态仿射动力学并配合递归多步展开监督，可控制误差传播、缩小训练与规划的差距，从而在闭环规划中取得更优表现。

Abstract: Joint-embedding world models enable visual planning by learning action-conditioned dynamics in latent space. Yet they are commonly trained for one-step prediction on encoded states, while planning recursively applies the learned transition to its own predictions. One-step accuracy therefore does not capture how prediction errors propagate under recursive rollout. We decompose multi-step rollout error into the errors introduced at individual steps and their propagation through subsequent transitions. We show that state-affine dynamics are precisely the differentiable transitions with state-independent Jacobians, eliminating the nonlinear propagation residual and making the error propagation operators depend only on the action sequence. Guided by this result, we introduce SALT (State-Affine Latent Transition), an action-conditioned state-affine dynamics model in which the action modulates both the state transformation and the additive update. We train SALT through recursive multi-step rollout supervision, feeding each predicted latent state back into the transition so that training matches how the model is used during planning. Across four visual planning environments, SALT exhibits $1.48$--$2.19\times$ higher one-step prediction error than the matched LeWM baseline, yet improves closed-loop success in every environment by $10.0$ percentage points on average. On OGBench-Cube, the fraction of episodes that fail with a sharp rise in model-predicted cost after execution decreases from $23.3%$ to $2.0%$.

</details>


### [88] [InfraVLA: Extending Vision-Language-Action Navigation with Infrastructure Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33647)
*Lukas Vierling,Benjamin Ramtoula,Luke Robinson,Ronald Clark,Daniele De Martini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 InfraVLA，将静态基础设施（CCTV）视角通过编码器接入预训练导航 VLA，并用两阶段微调使策略真正利用这些视角，在仿真与真实四足平台上大幅超越仅依赖机载视觉的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仓库、办公室、医院等室内环境通常已安装摄像头，可观测机器人站位看不到的区域，但现有导航策略（含近期 VLA 模型）均未利用这些静态基础设施视角，导致关键决策信息缺失。

Method: 端到端方法 InfraVLA：用 CCTV 编码器把每个外部视角转成输入序列 token，接入预训练导航 VLA；由于外部视角仅在少数决策点起作用，单纯微调无法让策略使用之，故采用两阶段训练——先在带过采样反事实数据的示范上训练，再在恢复（recovery）数据上训练。

Result: 在两个仿真仓库任务（按指令寻找指定物体、绕行被堵塞通道）上：分布内成功率均为 100%，基线（无 CCTV 输入）分别为 34.0% 与 73.6%；分布外测试集为 88.2% 与 88.9%。真实四足机器人用不到 10 分钟示范微调后达 83.3%，仅机载视觉基线为 29.2%。

Conclusion: 静态基础设施相机是导航 VLA 被忽视但高价值的感知来源；配合针对稀疏决策点的两阶段数据训练，可显著提升分布内、分布外及真实场景的导航成功率。

Abstract: Many indoor environments in which robots operate, such as warehouses, offices, and hospitals, already have cameras installed. They observe parts of the building that the robot cannot see from where it stands, yet navigation policies, including recent vision-language-action (VLA) models, do not use them. We propose InfraVLA, an end-to-end method that adapts a pretrained navigation VLA to such static infrastructure views: a closed-circuit television (CCTV) encoder turns each external view into tokens of the input sequence. Because the views matter only at rare decision points, fine-tuning alone did not make the policy use them in our experiments; we therefore train in two stages, on demonstrations with upsampled counterfactual data and then on recovery data. We evaluate on two simulated warehouse tasks, finding an object named in the instruction and rerouting around blocked aisles, where the deciding information is often visible only to the infrastructure cameras. Tested in distribution, InfraVLA reached a success rate of 100% on both, against 34.0% and 73.6% for a baseline without CCTV input. On out-of-distribution test sets it reached 88.2% and 88.9%. On a real quadruped fine-tuned with under 10 minutes of demonstrations, the policy reached 83.3% against 29.2% for the on-board-only baseline.

</details>


### [89] [Demonstration-Free Success-Probability Reward Learning for Generalist Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33653)
*Duo Wu,Haifeng Wang,Rongwei Lu,Jinghe Wang,Tianyi Xiong,Zhimin Wang,Chao Yu,Shuai Ma,Zhi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出无需专家演示的奖励学习范式eVTA₀及闭环框架RLER，通过时序差分自举从稀疏任务结果学习稠密成功概率奖励，在LIBERO与真实操作任务上显著提升策略性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习受限于稀疏任务奖励。现有通用奖励模型依赖专家演示学习任务进度，但与策略优化中混合质量的rollout存在分布不匹配，导致对次优和失败行为（恰是策略需要学习之处）的估计不可靠。

Method: 理论证明终端任务结果隐式定义了中间时刻的稠密成功概率反馈，可通过自举递归学习。据此提出eVTA₀，用时序差分式自举从混合质量策略rollout中学习成功概率，无需专家演示或中间标注；并提出RLER闭环框架，随策略演化用新采集的rollout自适应更新eVTA₀。

Result: eVTA₀提供比SOTA奖励模型更具信息量的奖励，在相同RL训练预算下于所有LIBERO任务套件取得最佳平均策略性能，成功率较初始策略提升5.4%-13.8%。真实世界操作中RLER进一步提升总体成功率20%-26%，分布外条件下提升35%-36%。

Conclusion: 无需演示的奖励学习以及随策略演化自适应奖励是有效的，可缓解奖励模型与策略rollout的分布不匹配问题，提升策略优化效果与泛化能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) enables generalist robot policies to improve through trial-and-error interaction, yet its effectiveness is fundamentally constrained by sparse task rewards. Existing general-purpose reward models typically alleviate this issue by learning task progress from expert demonstrations, but introduce a distribution mismatch with the mixed-quality rollouts encountered during policy optimization, making their estimates unreliable on suboptimal and failed behaviors from which the policy must learn. In this work, we introduce a demonstration-free reward learning paradigm where dense reward feedback can be learned directly from sparse task outcomes and policy experience. We theoretically show that terminal task outcomes implicitly define dense success-probability feedback at intermediate timesteps, which can be recursively learned through bootstrapping. Based on this insight, we introduce eVTA$_0$, which learns success probabilities from mixed-quality policy rollouts through temporal-difference-style bootstrapping, without expert demonstrations or intermediate annotations. We further introduce RL with Evolving Rewards (RLER), a closed-loop framework that adapts eVTA$_0$ using newly collected rollouts as the policy evolves. Experiments show that eVTA$_0$ provides more informative rewards than state-of-the-art reward models and achieves the best average policy performance across all LIBERO task suites under the same RL training budget, improving success rates by 5.4%-13.8% over the initial policy. In real-world manipulation, RLER further improves overall success rates by 20%-26%, with 35%-36% gains under out-of-distribution conditions. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of demonstration-free reward learning and adapting rewards as the policy evolves. Project webpage: https://duowuyms.github.io/evta0.

</details>


### [90] [Observability-Informed Optimal Sensor Placement for Soft Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33681)
*Samuel Smocot,James Richard Forbes,Audrey Sedal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出并实验评估了一种软体机器人最优传感器布置的系统性方法，利用凸优化最大化基于可观性格拉姆矩阵的指标，并在软体连续臂上验证其优于基线的状态重建效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软体机器人传感器布置方法存在两类不足：一是缺乏跨不同软体机器人形态的通用性，二是未考虑系统动力学特性。因此需要一种既通用又考虑动力学的系统性最优布置方法。

Method: 采用凸优化方法求解最大化基于可观性格拉姆矩阵（observability Gramian）指标的最优传感器配置；在软体连续臂上使用位置和应变测量进行实验评估，并利用卡尔曼滤波进行状态估计。

Result: 在所有正弦输入试验中，基于最优传感器布置的卡尔曼滤波状态估计重建误差均低于基线，改进量级为毫米级。

Conclusion: 该案例研究表明线性控制理论工具可指导软体机器人的最优传感器布置，提供了一种具有可解释性的传感器布置思路，并有望推广至其他形态的软体机器人。

Abstract: This paper presents the application and experimental evaluation of a systematic method for optimal sensor placement in soft robots. Existing methods either lack generalizability across different soft robot morphologies or do not account for system dynamics. The applied method uses convex optimization to find the optimal sensor configuration that maximizes an observability Gramian-based metric. The framework is experimentally evaluated using position and strain measurements on a soft continuum arm. Kalman filter state estimates using optimal sensor placements yield lower reconstruction error than a baseline across all sinusoidal input trials, with improvements on the order of millimeters. This case study shows that linear control theory tools can guide optimal sensor placement in soft robots, suggesting an interpretable approach to sensor placement that may extend to other morphologies.

</details>


### [91] [MomWorld: Momentum-Aware Latent World Model for Long-Horizon Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33737)
*Ziying Song,Shengkai Zhang,Lei Yang,Haozhuang Chi,Yuchen Liu,Jiangtao Su,Lin Liu,Ziyang Liu,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MomWorld，一种动量感知的隐式世界模型，用于自动驾驶长时域规划，通过传播场景运动趋势并预测未来构型与动量状态，配合MoFlow流匹配模块精化轨迹，在NAVSIM等基准上将6秒时域平均碰撞率较MomAD降低12.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时域规划要求自动驾驶车辆能预判场景演变与潜在风险，以在复杂交互中做出安全稳定决策。但现有方法难以将观测历史中的运动趋势有效传播至未来：基于单一隐状态的长时间rollout会衰减有用动态、保留过时的运动模式，并破坏可靠的近期规划。

Method: MomWorld从历史到当前观测中提取场景运动趋势，并将隐式动量传播至未来时域，联合预测未来构型状态与动量状态。机制上包含三个组件：可学习的动量持久性机制（保持稳定趋势）、场景条件化的动量更新（自适应调整未来动态）、场景自适应重置门（在突变情形下抑制过时动量）。此外提出MoFlow——一种动量条件化的流匹配模块，仅需少量积分步即可将基础轨迹精化为与预测未来场景演变对齐的轨迹，并采用时域感知的残差融合，在保持近期规划稳定性的同时允许更强的远距离修正。

Result: 在NAVSIM、nuScenes与Bench2Drive上的大量实验表明，MomWorld提升了长时域规划一致性，在6秒规划时域上相较MomAD将平均碰撞率降低12.2%。

Conclusion: 动量感知的隐式世界模型能有效弥补现有方法在长时域规划中运动趋势传播不足的问题，通过显式建模动量并配合流匹配轨迹精化，兼顾近期稳定性与长程修正能力，显著提升长时域规划的安全性与一致性。

Abstract: Long-horizon planning enables autonomous vehicles to anticipate scene evolution and potential risks, supporting safe and stable decisions in complex interactions. However, existing methods struggle to propagate motion trends from observed history into the future. Long rollouts based on a single latent state may further attenuate useful dynamics, retain stale motion patterns, and disrupt reliable near-term plans. We introduce MomWorld, a momentum-aware latent world model for long-horizon planning. MomWorld extracts scene motion trends from historical-to-current observations and propagates latent momentum into future horizons, jointly predicting future configuration and momentum states. A learnable momentum persistence mechanism preserves stable trends, scene-conditioned momentum updates adapt future dynamics, and a scene-adaptive reset gate suppresses stale momentum under abrupt changes. We further propose MoFlow, a momentum-conditioned flow-matching module that refines a base trajectory to align with the predicted future scene evolution in only a few integration steps, with a horizon-aware residual fusion that preserves near-term planning stability while permitting stronger long-range corrections. Extensive experiments on NAVSIM, nuScenes and Bench2Drive demonstrate that MomWorld improves long-horizon planning consistency and reduces the average collision rate by 12.2% relative to MomAD over a 6-second planning horizon.

</details>


### [92] [AnyStep-WAM: Budget-Aligned Distillation and Adaptive Inference for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33748)
*Rui Wang,Xiangyu Wang,Donglin Yang,Yibo Li,Canyang Chen,Zhongrui Wang,Xiaojuan Qi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 AnyStep World Action Model，一个支持可变去噪预算与场景自适应算力分配的世界-动作模型框架。通过预算对齐的教师轨迹蒸馏（区间条件流映射 + 共享低秩适配器）实现从一步到多步的动作生成，并用轻量级风险-收益调度器选择最小可行预算。在 RoboTwin 2.0 上对三种 WAM 平均去噪步数分别减少 60.2%、49.8%、85.28%，同时保持成功率；一步预算下成功率提升 7.07%、12.08%、8.94%，并在六个真实操作任务上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型（WAMs）通常依赖固定去噪步数的迭代去噪，但操作任务中的动作块对生成误差的敏感度不同：关键动作需要高精度，而低敏感动作可用更少步数快速生成。固定的计算预算既造成算力浪费，也无法在关键处提供足够精度，因此需要可变预算预测与场景相关的算力分配。

Method: 1) 预算对齐的教师轨迹蒸馏：利用显式的冻结教师转移与共享低秩适配器，训练区间条件流映射（interval-conditioned flow maps），使同一模型支持从一步预测到多步细化的动作生成。2) 轻量级风险-收益调度器：基于单次一步预览，预测教师曲率难度与各预算下的师生保真度，选择能满足风险自适应保真度要求的最小预算。

Result: 在 RoboTwin 2.0 上对 Motus、FastWAM、LingBotVA 三个 WAM 进行评估：平均去噪步数分别减少 60.2%、49.8%、85.28%，同时保持基线任务成功率。一步去噪预算下，任务成功率分别提升 7.07%、12.08%、8.94%。六个真实世界操作任务的实验进一步验证了有效性。

Conclusion: AnyStep 通过预算对齐蒸馏与风险自适应调度，使世界-动作模型能够在可控计算预算下灵活生成动作，在大幅降低去噪步数的同时保持甚至提升任务成功率，实现了精度与效率的自适应权衡。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) couple predictive visual modeling with action generation, typically relying on iterative denoising with a fixed denoising steps. However, manipulation tasks contain actions chunks with varying sensitivity to generation errors: critical actions require precision, while less sensitive actions allow faster generation with fewer denoising steps. Here we introduce AnyStep World Action Model, a general framework for tunable-budget prediction and scene-dependent computation allocation. Our budget-aligned teacher-trajectory distillation trains interval-conditioned flow maps using explicit frozen-teacher transitions and shared low-rank adapters, supporting action generation from one-step prediction to multi-step refinement. Building on this capability, a lightweight risk-benefit scheduler predicts teacher-curvature-based difficulty and budget-specific student-teacher fidelity from a single one-step preview, selecting the smallest budget predicted to satisfy risk-adaptive fidelity requirements. We evaluate our framework on three widely used WAMs Motus, FastWAM, and LingBotVA using RoboTwin 2.0. Our method reduces average denoising steps by 60.2%, 49.8%, and 85.28%, respectively, while maintaining baseline task success rates. In particular, our AnyStep training substantially improves model performance under a one-step denoising budget, increasing task success rates by 7.07%, 12.08%, and 8.94% on Motus, FastWAM, and LingBotVA, respectively. Experiments on six real-world manipulation tasks further validate its effectiveness.

</details>


### [93] [Principal Steering Subspaces for Online Adaptation of Frozen Generative Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33765)
*Jialeng Ni,Nathan Zhao,Kunpeng Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出主引导子空间（Principal Steering Subspaces, PSS），一种前向查询接口，通过有限差分解码器响应构建固定低维控制基，仅用SAC控制响应最强方向、正交补从高斯先验重采样，从而在不更新预训练生成式机器人策略的前提下实现在线适应，提升收敛速度与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式机器人策略具有表达力强的行为先验，但通过在线交互更新大规模扩散或流匹配模型代价高昂。潜空间强化学习通过控制初始采样噪声来避免更新预训练生成器，然而高维噪声对解码动作的影响具有强各向异性，直接在高维潜空间上控制低效且不稳定。

Method: PSS 通过对解码器的有限差分响应构建一个固定的低维控制基。利用 Soft Actor-Critic 控制响应最强的若干主方向，而正交补部分在每次查询时独立地从高斯先验重采样。方法在三个 RoboMimic 任务、扩散与流匹配策略上进行验证，并做了 Controlled Diffusion-Square 消融，比较主响应方向与同等维度的随机子空间、最低响应子空间。此外，将 PSS 与冻结的闭源 30 亿参数视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略集成到人形学习系统中，采用同步转移采集、重置时优化和延迟感知异步部署。

Result: 在五个匹配的任务-生成器组合中，训练曲线显示 PSS 通常比全潜空间控制收敛更快、后期训练更稳定，且整体最终性能更强；响应谱表现出显著的集中性。消融实验表明，主响应方向优于同等维度的随机子空间与最低响应子空间。在探索性的螺丝刀放置评估中，冻结 VLA 策略成功率为 2/10，采用 SAC+PSS 适应后提升至 6/10。

Conclusion: 解码器响应几何可作为冻结生成式机器人策略在线适应的实用基础，PSS 在无需更新预训练生成器的条件下实现了更高效、更稳定的控制与性能提升。

Abstract: Generative robot policies provide expressive behavior priors, but updating a large diffusion or flow-matching model through online interaction is costly. Latent-space reinforcement learning avoids updating the pretrained generator by controlling its initial sampling noise, yet high-dimensional noise can have strongly anisotropic effects on decoded actions. We introduce Principal Steering Subspaces (PSS), a forward-query interface that constructs a fixed low-dimensional control basis from finite-difference decoder responses. Soft Actor-Critic controls the leading response directions, while the orthogonal complement is independently resampled from the Gaussian prior at each query. On three RoboMimic tasks with diffusion and flow-matching policies, response spectra reveal substantial concentration. Across five matched task-generator pairs, the training curves indicate that PSS generally converges faster and exhibits more stable late-training behavior than full-latent control, while achieving stronger final performance overall. Controlled Diffusion-Square ablations further show that leading-response directions outperform random and least-responsive subspaces of equal dimension. We further integrate PSS with a frozen, closed-source 3B-parameter vision-language-action (VLA) policy in a humanoid learning system with synchronous transition collection, reset-time optimization, and latency-aware asynchronous deployment. In an exploratory screwdriver-placement evaluation, success is observed in 2/10 trials for the frozen VLA policy and 6/10 after SAC+PSS adaptation. These results support decoder-response geometry as a practical basis for online adaptation of frozen generative robot policies.

</details>


### [94] [CodeActionBench: Evaluating Agentic Code-as-Policy for Embodied Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33807)
*Yiheng Lyu,Xueying Jiang,Wenhao Li,Shijian Lu,Gongjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 CodeActionBench 基准，包含 25 个操作任务，通过 agentic Code-as-Policy 评估通用多模态模型将视觉理解与推理转化为可执行操作代码的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究通用多模态模型能在多大程度上将视觉理解与推理转化为具身操作行为，并需要一个可控、可比的测试平台来衡量这一能力及其失败模式。

Method: 构建含 25 个操作任务的基准，采用 agentic Code-as-Policy 范式：不进行任务微调、不使用演示、无外部专用感知或抓取模块、无特权场景状态、无预定义策略；由共享机器人 API 提供 RGB 观测、标定几何操作、机器人反馈与受限运动，任务相关决策交由被评估智能体完成；使用固定任务实例、资源预算与隐藏的物理结果验证器保证受控比较。

Result: 在 9 种配置、675 次尝试中，成功率介于 2.7% 至 73.3%。最佳配置 GPT-6 Astra 搭配 Codex CLI 在三次尝试中至少一次解决了 25 个任务中的 22 个，表现最佳但仍留有较大提升空间。

Conclusion: 轨迹分析揭示空间对齐、物体保持与完成判定方面的困难，包括动作已完成却仍判定为任务失败的情况。CodeActionBench 为衡量通用模型将自身能力转化为操作行为、并分析典型失败场景提供了受控测试平台。

Abstract: How well can general-purpose multimodal models turn visual understanding and reasoning into embodied manipulation via executable code? We introduce CodeActionBench, a benchmark of 25 manipulation tasks that evaluates this capability through agentic Code-as-Policy. Without task-specific fine-tuning, demonstrations, external specialist perception or grasp modules, privileged scene state, or predefined task policies, agents should select visual evidence, form task-relevant 3D estimates, construct manipulation targets, and iteratively execute and revise their policies. A shared robot API provides RGB observations, calibrated geometric operations, robot feedback, and bounded motion, leaving task-dependent decisions to the evaluated agent. Fixed task instances, resource budgets, and a hidden physical-outcome verifier support controlled comparisons across models and harness configurations. Extensive evaluations across nine configurations and 675 attempts achieve success rates ranging from 2.7% to 73.3%. The strongest configuration, GPT-6 Astra with Codex CLI, solves 22 of 25 tasks at least once in three attempts, demonstrating the best performance while still leaving substantial room for improvement. Trajectory analyses reveal difficulties in spatial alignment, object retention, and completion judgment, including task failures despite successfully completed motions. CodeActionBench provides a controlled testbed for measuring how general-purpose models translate their capabilities into manipulation behavior and for examining typical failure scenarios in that process.

</details>


### [95] [Achieve What You Imagined: Learning to Align Actions with Visual Plans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33832)
*Yuheng Qiao,Ziran Wei,Xiaohan Wang,Daqiang Guo,Yichen Luo,Zhibo Pang,Peng Zhou,Sichao Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将世界动作模型（WAM）生成的视觉预测视为目标条件的视觉提案而非可执行计划，利用冻结的动作条件世界模型构造一致性反馈，并用Flow Policy Optimization优化WAM的动作头，在四个真实UR5操作任务上将平均成功率从43.4%提升至75.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WAM虽能联合预测未来视觉观测与机器人动作，但其视觉预测与所生成动作实际导致的后果之间存在不一致：模型常能先给出视觉上合理的任务完成结果，却难以产出能可靠实现该结果的动作序列。

Method: 把WAM生成的视觉预测当作目标条件的视觉提案；用一个冻结的动作条件世界模型预测动作条件下的后果；基于两个未来预测之间的一致性以及与终端目标的对齐程度构造反馈；再利用Flow Policy Optimization（FPO）优化WAM的动作头。该框架无需在线机器人交互，也无需额外训练任务特定的奖励模型。

Result: 在四个真实世界UR5操作任务上，平均成功率由43.4%提升至75.1%，对比π_{0.5}的61.4%。

Conclusion: 在所评估的操作任务中，跨模型的预测差异可以提供有效反馈，用于改进机器人策略。

Abstract: World-action models can jointly predict future visual observations and robot actions. However, discrepancies may exist between their visual predictions and the consequences implied by generated actions. We observe that WAMs can often generate visually plausible task-completion outcomes before producing action sequences that reliably achieve them. Consequently, we treat the WAM-generated visual prediction as a goal-conditioned visual proposal rather than a directly executable plan. We use a frozen action-conditioned world model to predict action-conditioned consequences and construct feedback based on consistency between the two future predictions and alignment with the terminal goal. Leveraging this feedback, we employ Flow Policy Optimization (FPO) to optimize the action head of the WAM. This framework avoids online robot interaction and additional training of task-specific reward models. Across four real-world UR5 manipulation tasks, our method increases the mean success rate from 43.4% to 75.1%, compared with 61.4% for $π_{0.5}$. These results show that cross-model prediction discrepancy can provide useful feedback for improving robot policies under the evaluated manipulation tasks. Website: https://imagine-to-achieve.github.io/

</details>


### [96] [DeltaSeek: Toward Active Perception in Evolving Construction Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33836)
*Sanjay Acharjee,Md Nazmus Sakib*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DeltaSeek主动感知框架，形式化机器人"感知实体"可容许的观测集合，并在IFC衍生基准上对比底盘式与腕部RGB-D相机在动态建筑环境中的观测能力与代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑环境持续演化产生大幅几何变化，使静态建图与配准性能下降，因而需要主动感知，让机器人主动选择感知配置以解析环境当前状态。作为该目标的先决条件，需先厘清机器人感知实体的形态如何约束其可获取的观测。

Method: 形式化定义感知实体的可容许观测集合；使用搭载UR5e机械臂的Husky A300机器人，在IFC衍生的基准场景中，配置底盘式与腕部式两种RGB-D相机；以几何可见性对观测打分，以可行驶距离衡量代价；在含八处受控变化（覆盖四种可观测性条件）的房间级场景中，对240个可容许底盘位姿与5种臂姿态进行穷举评估。

Result: 穷举评估显示，两处变化在底盘视角下不存在任何可见位姿，而腕部相机可同时分辨这两处变化；对于两种实体均能观测到的变化，腕部相机的中位底盘行驶距离为6.0 m，底盘相机为15.2 m。

Conclusion: 结果将"感知能力局限"与"获取代价"区分开来，可明确某观测究竟是根本不可能获得，还是仅需更多行驶代价，为主动感知中"何处、如何、何时观测"的推理奠定前提。

Abstract: Construction environments evolve continuously, causing large geometric changes that degrade static mapping and registration performance. This necessitates active perception, where robots deliberately select sensing configurations to resolve the environment's current state. We present DeltaSeek, an initial framework toward active perception in evolving built environments. While our broader objective is a system that reasons about where, how, and when to observe, this paper addresses a critical prerequisite: how a robot's sensing embodiment constrains the observations it can acquire. We formalize an embodiment's permissible observation set and evaluate with a Husky A300 equipped with a UR5e on an IFC-derived benchmark under chassis-mounted and wrist-mounted RGB-D configurations, scoring observations by geometric visibility and effort by drivable distance. In a room-scale scene with eight controlled changes spanning four observability conditions, exhaustive evaluation over 240 permissible base poses and five arm postures shows that two changes admit no chassis viewpoint whatsoever, while the wrist camera resolves both. For changes observed by both embodiments, the median base travel is $6.0$~m for the wrist camera and $15.2$~m for the chassis camera. These results distinguish sensing limitations from acquisition costs, clarifying whether an observation is impossible or simply requires more travel.

</details>


### [97] [Robot-GST: geometry-aware spatial-temporal robot policy representation and evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33872)
*Sichao Liu,Zekun Wang,Lixuan Tang,Yiming Li,Xiaohan Wang,Hanzhi Zhang,Daqiang Guo,Peng Zhou,Lihui Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Robot-GST 提出一种几何感知的时空行为表示与评估框架，用 3D 高斯泼溅与 SAM3D 从 RGB-D 观测构建真实到仿真的高斯-SAM 环境，在动作执行前进行仿真验证，从而提升长时程操作的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉-语言模型的机器人操作策略快速发展，但部署可靠性不足：多数策略缺乏显式预测任务结果、评估动作能否达成目标终态的机制，导致长时程操作中执行误差不断累积。

Method: 利用 3D Gaussian Splatting 与 SAM3D 从 RGB-D 观测构建高保真机器人环境，实现"先仿真评估再执行"；结合视觉观测与语言指令，用大型视觉-语言模型进行时空推理与长时程任务规划；引入高斯感知的终态估计（几何采样）与基于状态的轨迹规划，在执行前于高斯-SAM 环境中仿真并评估候选动作序列，过滤不可行行为。

Result: 在刚体、软体与可变形物体的代表性操作任务（方块放置、玩具打包、鸭子重排）上验证，表明几何感知的时空推理与状态感知执行能跨不同物体类别提升操作可靠性。

Conclusion: 将几何感知重建与高质量渲染和仿真相结合，为评估机器人操作行为提供了一种可扩展的方法。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation policies are advancing rapidly with increasing reliance on vision-language models for end-to-end decision making. However, reliable deployment remains challenging because many policies lack explicit mechanisms for predicting task outcomes and evaluating whether generated actions will achieve desired final states, causing execution errors to accumulate during long-horizon manipulation. We present Robot-GST, a geometry-aware spatio-temporal behaviour representation and evaluation framework that constructs a Gaussian-SAM robotic environment for real-to-sim policy verification and improves the reliability of real-world manipulation deployment. Our approach constructs a high-fidelity robotic environment from RGB-D observations using 3D Gaussian Splatting and SAM3D, enabling ``simulation and evaluation before acting''. It integrates visual observations and language instructions with spatio-temporal reasoning for long-horizon task planning using large vision-language models. To bridge high-level planning and real-world execution, we introduce Gaussian-aware final-state estimation through geometric sampling and state-based trajectory planning. Before execution, candidate action sequences are simulated and evaluated in the Gaussian-SAM environment to filter infeasible behaviours. We validate our approach on representative manipulation tasks involving rigid, soft, and deformable objects, including cube placing, toy packing, and duck rearrangement, demonstrating that geometry-aware spatio-temporal reasoning and state-aware execution improve manipulation reliability across different object categories. Our results suggest that combining geometry-aware reconstruction with high-quality rendering and simulation provides a scalable approach for evaluating robotic manipulation behaviours. Website: https://robot-gst.github.io

</details>


### [98] [DexTaG: Tactile-as-Guidance in Reinforcement Learning for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33882)
*Han Yang,Yian Wang,Yunlong Song,Zhenjia Xu,Chuang Gan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexTaG：一种触觉引导的强化学习框架，用于将手套采集的人手演示数据重定向到机器人灵巧手上，实现接触丰富的手内操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手套式动作捕捉是采集灵巧手演示数据的可扩展途径，但人手与机器人手之间存在运动学差异，录得的人手动作无法直接在机器人上执行，尤其在手内重定向这类接触丰富的工具使用任务中。先前工作通过强化学习或轨迹优化在仿真中弥合差距，但难以保留人的接触模式，常导致不自然的操作和不稳定的功能性抓取；且为每条参考轨迹单独训练策略或求解优化，效率低下。

Method: 提出 DexTaG：一个触觉引导的强化学习框架。训练时，手套采集的触觉信号引导策略搜索朝向测得的人手接触模式，降低对精确参考几何进行接触监督的依赖；为提升效率，对同一对象的所有训练轨迹联合训练一个可泛化的重定向器，并将其蒸馏为以目标物体轨迹为条件的无触觉学生控制器，用于真实世界部署。

Result: 在马克笔和锤子操作任务上，DexTaG 学到了自然、接触丰富的行为，而基于距离接触启发式的基线无法学到；方法可泛化到同一物体与任务的留出轨迹，并在 OakInk2 上优于单轨迹基线。

Conclusion: 触觉引导结合联合训练的通用重定向器与蒸馏的无触觉学生控制器，可有效弥合人机运动学差距，实现自然、接触丰富的灵巧操作并支持真实部署。

Abstract: Glove-based motion capture is emerging as a scalable approach to collecting dexterous-hand demonstration data. However, due to the kinematic gap between the human and robot hand, the recorded human motions cannot be executed directly on the robot, especially for contact-rich tool-use tasks involving in-hand reorientation. Prior work bridges this gap in simulation through reinforcement learning (RL) or trajectory optimization, but the human contact pattern is hard to preserve under such formulations, often producing unnatural manipulation and unstable functional grasps. These methods also train a separate policy or solve a separate optimization for each reference trajectory, which is inefficient. To solve these problems, we propose DexTaG, a tactile-guided RL framework for dexterous manipulation. During training, tactile signals captured by the glove guide policy search toward the measured human contact pattern, reducing reliance on precise reference geometry for contact supervision. To improve efficiency, we train a single generalizable retargeter jointly on all training trajectories of the same object. The retargeter is further distilled into a tactile-free student controller conditioned on the target object trajectory for real-world deployment. On marker-pen and hammer manipulation tasks, DexTaG learns natural, contact-rich behaviors that baselines with distance-based contact heuristics fail to learn, generalizes to held-out trajectories of the same object and task, and outperforms single-trajectory baselines on OakInk2.

</details>


### [99] [Residual Learning-Based Control of Vehicle Platoons with $\ell_2$ Stability Guarantees via Recurrent Equilibrium Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33892)
*Brian Delgado,Anh-Tu Nguyen,Hamid Taghavifar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种残差学习控制框架，用于存在参数不确定性和外部扰动的异构车队，通过REN残差补偿提升标称控制器性能并保证稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构车队存在参数不确定性和外部扰动，仅依赖基于LMI的标称控制器与扰动观测器补偿难以应对建模误差，需引入数据驱动残差项进一步提升间距与速度控制精度，同时保持可证明的稳定性。

Method: 以LMI设计的标称控制器结合扰动观测器补偿作为基线；利用存储轨迹与标称模型预测误差离线训练循环平衡网络（REN）作为残差项；对REN施加预设的ℓ2增益约束，从而借助小增益条件证明局部闭环稳定性与扰动弦稳定性。

Result: 实验表明，相比标称控制器，所提框架降低了车队间距误差与速度误差。

Conclusion: 将受ℓ2增益约束的REN残差学习与LMI标称控制及扰动观测器结合，可在保证稳定性的前提下有效改善异构车队在不确定性与扰动下的跟踪性能。

Abstract: This paper proposes a residual learning-based control framework for heterogeneous vehicle platoons subject to parametric uncertainty and external disturbances. A nominal controller designed via Linear Matrix Inequalities (LMIs), along with disturbance-observer compensation, is enhanced by a Recurrent Equilibrium Network (REN) trained offline using stored trajectories and nominal-model prediction errors. The REN is constrained to satisfy a prescribed $\ell_2$-gain bound, enabling sufficient small-gain conditions for local closed-loop stability and disturbance string stability. Experiments demonstrate reduced spacing and velocity errors relative to the nominal controller.

</details>


### [100] [ArticulateArena: A Metric for Articulated Kinematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33931)
*Yumeng He,Yongfei She,Huanyu Chen,Chun Yuan,Peihao Li,Joseph Masterjohn,Yin Yang,Ying Jiang,Chenfanfu Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出ArticulateArena，一种用于铰接物体关节（articulation）评估的表示不变度量，并发布包含19,977个铰接物体的数据集ArticulateArena-20K。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法能重建可仿真的铰接物体（预测几何与关节运动），但关节评估困难：关节描述的是运动而非形状，缺乏公认的运动间距离。现有协议分别对关节类型、轴方向、原点、运动限位打分，而这些参数联合描述同一物理运动，且同一运动可写成不同参数值，导致等价编码可能被评为"最大错误"，且部分分量误差在预测准确时反而病态或未定义。

Method: 提出ArticulateArena，作为Chamfer距离在关节上的表示不变对应物，比较单自由度关节所诱导的运动而非其编码参数。用关节的Lie代数端点twist的无序对表示每个关节，证明所得商距离是度量。该度量统一了固定、旋转、棱柱和螺旋关节，并通过紧化将连续关节纳入同一评分，按质量分布加权后可解释为运动部件以米为单位的RMS运动。进一步提出运动感知的树编辑距离，将度量提升到完整运动学树，以相同运动单位评估结构错误（多余或缺失关节），并在固定内积下保持为树的度量（至重标记）。同时发布ArticulateArena-20K数据集（19,977个带验证运动学的铰接物体），并在新度量下重新评估已发表的重建方法。

Result: 商距离被证明为度量；树编辑距离在固定内积下保持为树的度量（至重标记）；发布了含19,977个已验证运动学铰接物体的ArticulateArena-20K数据集，并对已发表重建方法进行了重新评估。

Conclusion: ArticulateArena提供了表示不变、物理可解释的关节与运动学树评估框架，解决了现有协议中参数编码依赖导致的评估问题，并通过新数据集支持对重建方法的统一评估。

Abstract: Modern methods reconstruct or generate simulation-ready articulated objects, predicting not only their geometry but also how their parts are connected and allowed to move. Evaluating the geometry is straightforward, but evaluating the predicted articulation is not, because articulation specifies a motion rather than a shape, and there is no agreed distance between two motions. More specifically, existing protocols score joint type, axis direction, origin, and motion limits separately, although these parameters jointly describe a single physical motion, and the same motion can be written as different parameter values. As a result, a joint can score maximally wrong against an equivalent encoding of itself, and several component errors are ill-conditioned or undefined exactly where predictions become accurate. We propose ArticulateArena, a representation-invariant counterpart of Chamfer distance for articulation that compares the motions one-DOF joints induce rather than the parameters that encode them. It represents each joint by the unordered pair of its Lie-algebra endpoint twists, and we prove that the resulting quotient distance is a metric. It unifies fixed, revolute, prismatic, and helical joints, brings continuous joints into the same score through a compactification, and reads as the RMS motion of the moving part in meters when weighted by its mass distribution. A motion-aware tree edit distance lifts the metric to full kinematic trees, pricing structural errors such as spurious or missing joints in the same motion units as joint errors, and for a fixed inner product it remains a metric on trees up to relabeling. Alongside the metric we release ArticulateArena-20K, a new suite of 19,977 articulated objects with verified kinematics, and we re-evaluate published reconstruction methods on it under the new metric. Project page: https://heyumeng.com/ArticulateArena-web/

</details>


### [101] [EpiTransfer: Sparse, Training-Free Long-Range Depth Estimation from Temporal Monocular Aerial Frames](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33939)
*Diksha Aggarwal,Rutvik Dagadkhair,Sanjana Srivastava,Bradley Denby,Kevin Kochersberger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需训练、基于几何推导的深度估计方法，利用对极传输与仅两张单目图像及相机位姿，通过合成虚拟立体对实现更稳健的深度估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习型深度估计方法在分布内精度高，但在新视角与不同高度下泛化能力差，难以满足自主导航、避障与场景重建对可靠3D空间理解的需求。

Method: 方法为几何推导且无需训练：仅使用两张单目图像与相机位姿估计，借助对极传输，利用相机运动合成具有自由选择基线的虚拟立体对，将时间对应转化为立体三角测量任务，并缓解直接双视图三角测量固有的几何退化问题。

Result: 在户外无人机飞行（最大约90 m）与室内OptiTrack环境中以LiDAR真值验证：室内AbsRel为0.092，δ&lt;1.25为0.940，与直接三角测量（AbsRel 0.073）相当，但在更多困难场景中保持有效深度；显著优于未针对该域训练或微调的ZoeDepth（AbsRel 0.225）与Depth Anything V2（AbsRel 0.570），且无需训练数据。

Conclusion: 该方法以无需训练、几何推导的方式，在新视角与高度变化下实现可靠深度估计，并在挑战性场景中比学习型基线更具泛化性与有效性。

Abstract: Reliable 3D spatial understanding is essential for autonomous navigation, obstacle avoidance, and scene reconstruction. While state-of-the-art learned depth estimation techniques achieve high accuracy in-distribution, they often generalize poorly to novel viewpoints and altitudes. This paper presents a geometrically derived, training-free depth estimation method using epipolar transfer with only two monocular images and camera pose estimates. By leveraging camera motion to synthesize a virtual stereo pair with a freely chosen baseline, our approach transforms temporal correspondence into a stereo triangulation task while mitigating geometric degeneracies inherent to direct two-view triangulation. Validated across outdoor drone flights (to a maximum range of approximately 90\,m) and indoor OptiTrack environments against LiDAR ground truth, the method achieves an indoor AbsRel of 0.092 and $δ< 1.25$ of 0.940, comparable to direct triangulation (AbsRel 0.073) while retaining valid depth over a larger fraction of challenging scenes, and substantially outperforms off-the-shelf learning-based baselines such as ZoeDepth (AbsRel 0.225) and Depth Anything V2 (AbsRel 0.570), which are not trained or fine-tuned for this domain, with no training data required.

</details>


### [102] [ReSync: Re-Aligning the Two Clocks of Asynchronous World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33944)
*Xi Lin,Feihong Zhang,Yulong Shi,Yanghong Mei,Zuxing Lu,Xiaofan Zhu,Zihao Liang,Zhirui Gao,Zhaowen Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文将"动作与视频双流异步去噪"形式化为双时钟视角，提出可直接从采样计划中读取的"承诺-证据差"（commitment-evidence gap），并据此给出无需改参、无需候选比较的推理重排方法 ReSync：在动作状态保持不动的情况下仅推进世界流至支持窗口内，再恢复原生去噪，从而提升世界-动作模型的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前世界-动作模型普遍采用双流异步去噪：动作流用少步解码以保证控制实时性，视频流用更多步以保持预测未来清晰。这种设计虽有意为之，却使两条流处于不同"时钟"上——动作可能已经可执行，而应为其提供依据的未来仍未解算完成。作者希望刻画并利用这一异步推理中的结构性问题，而不是依靠搜索或增加候选来弥补。

Method: 1）将上述现象形式化为双时钟异步推理视角，定义"承诺-证据差"：该量直接从模型自身的采样计划中读取，而非通过搜索测量。2）分析该差值的预测性：差值扩大时，候选效用更难辨识、额外候选采样的收益递减，而推进世界流的收益递增，二者存在交叉点。3）由此得出"有用区间两端封闭"的结论，且两端均可在任何 rollout 之前从采样计划中读出。4）提出 ReSync：在该区间内放置计算量——保持动作状态不变，仅在支持窗口内推进世界流，随后恢复原生去噪；不改变任何参数，也不比较候选。

Result: 在冻结的配对 RoboCasa 评测面板上，ReSync 将成功率提升 4.48 个百分点；等计算量但仅等待、不推进世界流的对照方法无任何提升；同一规则可迁移至第二个基准与第二个骨干网络，且无需重新调参。

Conclusion: 异步去噪中的额外世界计算并非越多越好，其有效区间两端封闭，且可由采样计划事先读出。ReSync 表明，把计算精确置于该区间内即可稳定提升性能，同时保持零参数改动、零候选比较，并具备跨基准与跨骨干的免调参迁移性。

Abstract: Jointly generating future video and actions has become a standard recipe for world-action models, and the strongest systems denoise the two streams on separate schedules: actions are decoded in few steps so control stays fast, while the video stream runs longer to keep the predicted future sharp. The design is deliberate, but it leaves the two streams on different clocks, and an action can become executable while the future that should justify it is still largely unresolved. We formalize this as a two-clock view of asynchronous inference and introduce the commitment-evidence gap, a quantity read directly from a model's own sampling schedule rather than measured by search. The gap is predictive: as it widens, candidate utility becomes harder to identify and extra candidate sampling buys less, while advancing the world stream buys more, and the two cross. Spending more world computation is therefore not simply better. The useful interval is closed at both ends, and both ends can be read off the schedule before any rollout. ReSync places the computation inside it: hold the action state, advance only the world within the supported window, then resume native denoising. No parameters change and no candidates are compared. On a frozen paired RoboCasa panel this improves success by 4.48 points, while an equal-compute control that waits without advancing the world does not move, and the same rule transfers to a second benchmark and a second backbone without retuning.

</details>


### [103] [Integrity Detection and Characterization of Malicious Injections in RAVEN II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33951)
*Xingli Zhang,Diba Afroze,Fei Hu,Xiali Hei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文刻画了RAVEN II手术机器人上恶意注入的检测边界：在命令与观测路径的三个注入点上，以步进、窗口、会话三种时间离散度注入，并在窗口尺度和会话尺度上量化了在可接受误报率下可分辨的最小末端执行器偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术机器人日益复杂（更多传感器、控制器、通信接口与模型驱动控制组件），攻击面持续扩大。完整性问题被利用会导致非预期行为和患者安全风险，因此需要明确系统性监控手段对恶意注入的检测能力边界，而非仅报告任意阈值下的检测率。

Method: 使用同时包含RAVEN II遥测数据与外部高分辨率编码器真值的公开数据集。在命令路径和观测路径上确定三个注入点，设计三种时间离散度递增的注入模式；在两个时间尺度（窗口尺度与会话尺度）执行检测，并以手术操作可接受的告警率为约束，度量每个注入点与模式下的最小可分辨末端执行器偏差。

Result: 可检测性强烈依赖注入偏差在时间上的分布方式。突发阶跃型注入可在远低于1 mm临床容差的偏差下被检出；而将相同总体偏差分散到整个窗口或整个会话时，则可能被单窗口统计量漏检。

Conclusion: 手术机器人完整性的检测边界取决于注入点与偏差的时间离散度，单窗口检测对缓慢分散的偏差存在结构性盲区，需要跨尺度（窗口—会话）联合监控；代码与数据可复现（http://github.com/RAVENIIROS/RAVENIIIntegrity）。

Abstract: The increasing adoption of robotic systems in surgery, together with the expanding range of procedures they can support and the growing level of autonomy they provide, has substantially increased the complexity of surgical robots. As these systems integrate more sensors, controllers, communication interfaces, and model-driven control components, their attack surface continues to expand. A compromise of the integrity of a surgical robot can therefore cause unintended robot behavior and potentially threaten patient safety.
  In this paper, we characterize the detection boundary of malicious injections on RAVEN II using a public dataset that pairs the platform's telemetry with external high-resolution encoder ground truth. We identify three injection points spanning the command and observation paths and evaluate three injection patterns with increasing temporal dispersion. To capture different detection behaviors, we perform detection at two timescales: the window scale and the session scale. Rather than reporting detection rates at an arbitrarily chosen threshold, we quantify, for each injection point and injection pattern, the smallest end-effector deviation that can be resolved while maintaining an alarm rate acceptable for surgical operation. Our results show that detectability is strongly influenced by how the injected deviation is distributed over time. An abrupt step can be detected at deviations well below the 1 mm clinical tolerance, whereas the same overall deviation spread across a window or a session can remain hidden from single-window statistics. The open source code can be found at http://github.com/RAVENIIROS/RAVENIIIntegrity.

</details>


### [104] [FINGR: Learning Dexterous Hand Control for Real-World Rubik's Cube Solving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33973)
*Yutong Liang,Quanquan Peng,Matthew Kim,Xiaolong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FINGR策略，用指相对几何表示与多尺度未来交互预测监督，在真实灵巧手上实现魔方连续层转操作，300次尝试成功率99.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单只灵巧手操作魔方需要持续的接触丰富控制：部分手指支撑、部分手指推转层、随后脱接触并复位。该任务对指间协调与接触状态感知要求高，传统策略难以稳定学习这种多阶段、强接触依赖的动作序列。

Method: FINGR由共享点云编码器、未来token监督模块与流策略组成。编码器以指尖为参考系表达魔方点云并聚合，不依赖cubie编号索引；可学习的未来token共享观测编码器，在多个时间尺度上接受接触力变化、层转进度与指关节位移的监督；所得表示用于条件化流策略，直接生成手指动作。

Result: 真实灵巧手上300次层转尝试成功率达99.0%，基线流策略为79.7%。结合抓取与桌面辅助重抓取后，系统能解开全部10个打乱的2×2×2魔方，平均完整系统耗时约137秒。

Conclusion: 指相对几何表示与多尺度未来交互预测监督能显著提升接触丰富灵巧操作的鲁棒性与成功率，并可与抓取、重抓取模块集成完成完整魔方求解流程。

Abstract: Manipulating a Rubik's Cube with a single dexterous hand is a challenging test of sustained, contact-rich control: the hand must execute successive layer turns while keeping the cube secure. Each turn requires some fingers to support the cube while others push a moving layer, release contact, and reset for the next move. To learn this coordination, we introduce FINGR (Future-supervised Interaction Network with Geometric Representations), a policy that combines finger-relative geometry with future interaction prediction. A shared point encoder expresses the cube relative to each fingertip and aggregates its points without depending on cubie indexing. Learned future tokens share the observation encoder and receive supervision for contact-force changes, layer-turn progress, and finger joint displacement at multiple time scales. The resulting representation conditions a flow policy that directly generates finger actions. On a real dexterous hand, our policy achieves 99.0% success over 300 turn attempts, compared with 79.7% for the base flow policy. Integrated with grasping and table-assisted regrasping, the policy solves all ten scrambled $2\times2\times2$ cubes in a mean complete-system time of approximately 137 seconds. The project website is available at https://www.lyt0112.com/projects/FINGR

</details>


### [105] [Test-Time Spatial Reasoning for Robot Manipulation Using Generative Real-to-Sim](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33982)
*Ivan Kapelyukh,Yafei Hu,Ran Gong,Brandon May,Tushar Kusnur,Laura Herlant,Karl Schmeckpeper,Edward Johns,Xiaohan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Simify是一个免训练、测试时框架，通过大规模并行物理仿真实现显式空间推理，可从单张RGB-D图像重建仿真可用资产，并用进化搜索优化物体摆放，在真实机器人上完成复杂重排任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间推理是通用机器人智能的基础，使机器人能够完成涉及多物体交互的长时程任务。现有基础模型在空间推理方面存在不足，且需要完整准确的几何信息才能成功实现仿真到现实的迁移。

Method: Simify从单张场景RGB-D图像出发，利用3D生成模型和视觉语言模型重建可仿真的资产；给定由奖励函数指定的任务（如搭建最高的塔），在仿真中启动数千个并行rollout，并通过进化搜索优化物体排列。

Result: 在真实机器人硬件上进行定量实验，证明该框架能够端到端执行此前未见物体的复杂物体重排任务，通常在数秒内收敛，并在空间推理方面优于先前的基础模型工作。

Conclusion: 在推理阶段有效利用大规模并行仿真可提升空间推理性能，同时完整且准确的几何信息对成功的仿真到现实迁移至关重要。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning is fundamental to general robot intelligence, as it enables robots to complete long-horizon tasks involving multi-object interaction. We introduce Simify, a training-free, test-time framework that performs explicit spatial reasoning via massively parallel physics simulation. From a single RGB-D image of a scene, Simify reconstructs simulation-ready assets leveraging 3D generative models and vision-language models. Then given a task specified by a reward function (e.g., build the tallest tower), Simify launches thousands of parallel rollouts in simulation and performs an evolutionary search to optimize object arrangements, typically converging within seconds. We conduct quantitative experiments on real-robot hardware to demonstrate the ability of our framework to execute complex object rearrangement tasks end-to-end with previously unseen objects. Results show that our framework outperforms prior work on foundation models for spatial reasoning by effectively exploiting large-scale parallel simulation during inference, and also highlight the importance of complete and accurate geometry for successful sim-to-real transfer.

</details>


### [106] [TacGooseBumps (TacGB): Retrofitting Normal-Only Tactile Sensors with Shear Encoding for Learning Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34006)
*Wenjie Li,Binyu Yang,Yuxin Chen,Ambrose Wang,Masayoshi Tomizuka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 TacGooseBumps（TacGB）：一种被动穹顶薄膜，将切向载荷机械编码为既有触觉传感器压力图的图案变化，无需标定剪切力，即可让模仿学习策略区分剪切相关接触状态，显著提升插入、易碎物放置与绘图任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富任务中，不同物理状态外观相似但所需动作不同；相机难以分辨连接器是否对齐或完全就位，而仅有法向感知的触觉传感器会遗漏垂直于抓取方向的切向交互。核心问题是：学习策略是否需要经过标定的剪切测量，还是只需一个可重复、能区分剪切相关接触状态的观测。

Method: 设计 TacGB：被动穹顶薄膜，切向加载使每个穹顶倾斜并在其足迹内重新分布压力，从而在现有传感器的压力图中形成图案变化。端到端策略直接消费该压力图，无需额外电子器件、力重建或触觉单元级的穹顶对齐。在四个模仿学习任务与两条数据采集流程中评估。

Result: TacGB 提升目标达成率、效率与接触质量：插入成功率最高提升 36 个百分点，成功插入更快完成；易碎物体放置更轻柔，绘图更连续、更笔直。信号、分阶段、失效模式与轨迹分析表明，增益来自视觉与仅法向压力难以分辨的任务相关切向交互接触阶段。

Conclusion: 剪切无需被度量式测量即可使机器人学习受益；可通过机械方式编码，且不改变底层触觉传感器或策略的压力图输入格式。

Abstract: Contact-rich policies often fail because distinct physical states look alike yet require different actions. Cameras may not reveal whether a connector is aligned or fully seated, while many normal-only tactile sensors can miss the tangential interactions perpendicular to the grasping direction that distinguish these states.
