### [177] [CoBrush: A Hierarchical Planning Framework for Human-Robot Co-Painting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35476)
*Dantong Qin,Yike Guo,Qinlin Liu,Alessandro Bozzon,Pan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CoBrush分层框架，将多轮人机协同绘画建模为语义、空间与执行三阶段协调过程，在真实丙烯画布上实现内容逐步丰富的协同创作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人机协同绘画系统（参考驱动型绘画器或反应式助手）多针对单次渲染或草图补全优化，难以在人类意图随交互演变的情况下维持连贯的多轮协作，也难以随时间构建复杂、内容丰富的场景。

Method: CoBrush采用分层框架，将多轮协同绘画形式化为语义、空间、执行三者协调的过程：将高层意图推断与空间落地、笔画级控制解耦，从而支持在真实丙烯画布上进行渐进式场景构建。

Result: 通过真实人机绘画会话、压力测试与用户研究进行评估。与单轮基线相比，该方法在语义对齐、空间推进稳定性以及机器人动作的感知合理性方面均表现更优。

Conclusion: 结构化的多阶段推理能够提升交互式绘画的连贯性与鲁棒性，并支持内容丰富的实体艺术作品的渐进式发展。

Abstract: Embodied co-painting requires a robot to repeatedly update a shared physical canvas while human intent evolves over interaction. Existing reference-driven painters or reactive assistants are typically optimized for single-shot rendering or sketch completion, limiting their ability to sustain coherent multi-round collaboration or to construct complex, content-rich scenes over time. We present CoBrush, a hierarchical framework that formulates multi-round co-painting as a coordinated semantic, spatial, and execution process. By separating high-level intent inference from spatial grounding and stroke-level control, the system supports progressive scene development on real acrylic canvases. We evaluate the framework through real human-robot painting sessions, stress tests, and user studies. Compared to single-turn baselines, our approach achieves stronger semantic alignment, more stable spatial progression, and higher perceived plausibility of robot actions. These results demonstrate that structured multi-stage reasoning improves the coherence and robustness of interactive painting and supports the progressive development of content-rich physical artworks.

</details>


### [178] [Robot Tool Design from Scratch via Behavior-Aware Hierarchical Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35479)
*Yinghan Chen,Xiyao Tian,Yizan Dai,Yuyang Li,Yixin Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出HOT分层优化框架，从零开始联合设计工具的结构、形状与动作：上层用BASS在离散结构空间中搜索，下层进行物理优化评估任务行为并返回里程碑进展作为行为证据，最终输出联合优化的形状与动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人工具设计方法通常在预先给定或生成的结构下优化工具的连续形状与动作，结构本身游离于物理优化环路之外，限制了从任务物理效果出发的端到端设计能力。作者希望实现"从零开始"的任务驱动工具设计，即工具结构、形状和动作均由期望的物理结果推导而来。

Method: 提出HOT（分层优化）方法：上层通过BASS在离散工具结构空间中进行搜索，下层执行物理优化以评估候选结构的任务行为，并将里程碑进展作为行为证据反馈给上层搜索，从而最终给出联合优化的形状与动作。该方法覆盖包含多达5600万个结构的搜索空间。

Result: 在四个具有不同物理功能的工具使用任务上，HOT仅评估搜索空间中的一小部分即可发现功能性结构；随后对其几何形状进行细化，在所有任务上均降低任务损失并保持成功，且变形具有功能可解释性。3D打印后的工具在真实机器人上以仿真中找到的动作完成了全部任务。

Conclusion: 从所需物理效果而非已知工具目录出发设计工具，是迈向人类和动物那种开放式工具制造的一步，表明结构、形状与动作可以联合优化并迁移到真实机器人。

Abstract: The ability to design a tool for a task marks a level of intelligence beyond merely understanding, selecting, or using one. Existing methods for robotic tool design typically optimize a tool's continuous shape and action within a structure that is prescribed or generated beforehand, so the structure itself stays outside the physical optimization loop. We study task-driven tool design from scratch, where tool structure, shape, and action are all derived from the desired physical outcome. Here we show that the three elements can be designed jointly by HOT, a hierarchical optimization whose upper level searches over discrete tool structures with BASS, while lower-level physical optimization evaluates their task behavior and returns milestone progress as behavioral evidence for the search, ultimately providing jointly optimized shape and action. On four tool-use tasks with distinct physical functions, HOT discovers functional structures after evaluating only a small fraction of search spaces containing up to 56 million structures, and the subsequent refinement of their geometry lowers the task loss on all tasks while preserving success, through deformations that are functionally interpretable. Once 3D printed, the tools accomplish all tasks on a real robot with the actions found in simulation. Designing tools from required physical effects, rather than a catalog of known tools, is a step toward the open-ended tool making seen in humans and animals.

</details>


### [179] [ForVis: An In-Field Dataset and Benchmark for VIO Using Under-Canopy UAV Flights in Forests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35482)
*Arman Kiani,Masoud Ataei,Elvis Gyaase,Jeffrey Eiyike,Aaron Weiskittel,Prabuddha Chakraborty,Vikas Dhiman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ForVis——一个面向森林环境下无人机VI-SLAM评估的实地数据集与基准，包含12次飞行、563.8秒、1096.8米轨迹，并用7个开源VI-SLAM系统完成504次测试，发现传感器选择对轨迹误差的影响大于算法间差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机VI-SLAM在真实森林环境中难以评估，因为运动、光照变化、重复植被纹理和振动都会影响估计精度；现有评估缺乏针对该场景的实地数据与统一基准。

Method: 构建ForVis数据集：在每种环境的开阔草地、冠层上方和冠层下方三种条件下采集，共12次飞行；同步使用Intel RealSense D435i与OAK-D Pro Wide相机，并记录惯性与飞控数据；在此基础上对7个开源VI-SLAM系统开展504次运行基准测试。

Result: 数据集总计563.8秒飞行时间、1096.8米轨迹。基准结果显示，传感器选择对轨迹误差的影响大于算法之间的差异：7种方法在OAK-D Pro上的中位误差均低于在D435i上的中位误差。

Conclusion: ForVis可作为评估VI-SLAM在挑战性森林飞行中速度、精度与鲁棒性的数据集与基准平台。

Abstract: Visual-inertial Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (VI-SLAM) for UAVs remains difficult to evaluate in real forest environments, where motion, illumination changes, repetitive vegetation, and vibration can all affect estimation. We present ForVis, an in-field dataset and benchmark for evaluating VI-SLAM during UAV flight in forest environments. The dataset contains twelve flights across open meadow, above-canopy, and under-canopy conditions in each environment. In total, it provides 563.8s of flight over 1096.8m of trajectory, recorded simultaneously with an Intel RealSense D435i and an OAK-D Pro Wide together with inertial and flight-controller data. We benchmark seven open-source VI-SLAM systems over 504 runs. The results show that sensor choice has a larger effect on trajectory error than the spread between algorithms: all seven methods achieve lower median error on the OAK-D Pro than on the D435i. ForVis is intended to support evaluation of speed, accuracy and robustness for VI-SLAM in challenging forest flight.

</details>


### [180] [Terrain-Aware Autonomous Planetary Exploration for Exteroceptive-Proprioceptive Mapping with Quadruped Scouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35493)
*Alberto Sanchez-Delgado,João Carlos Virgolino Soares,Victor Barasuol,Claudio Semini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个面向类月面环境的四足机器人地形感知探索框架，融合外部感知与本体感知建图，实现自主探索并降低运动能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主行星探索要求机器人能在未知、崎岖地形上导航，并评估风险、可通行性与能量消耗。四足侦察机器人适合此类任务，因其能穿越不规则地表并在行走过程中获取与移动性相关的信息。

Method: 机载RGB-D相机构建以机器人为中心的高程图，估计几何可通行性并推导规划所需的导航代价；同时利用本体感知测量提供交互感知的地形线索，补充基于几何的评估。局部地图增量配准为全局多层表示，探索模块据此在未探索的兴趣区域选择目标，自主导航系统结合地图与代价层引导避碰运动。

Result: 在NVIDIA Isaac Sim上的仿真结果显示：实现了自主探索与地图扩展，并揭示了地形几何与机器人-地形交互之间的空间关联；利用该信息进行后续导航时，平均运输代价（CoT）低于初始探索阶段。

Conclusion: 融合外部感知与本体感知的多层地图表示可有效支持四足机器人在类月面环境中的自主探索，且交互感知信息有助于提升导航能效。

Abstract: Autonomous planetary exploration requires robots to navigate unknown, uneven terrain while assessing risk, traversability, and energetic cost. Quadruped scouts are well suited for this task because they can traverse irregular surfaces and gather mobility-relevant information during locomotion. This paper presents a terrain-aware exploration framework that combines exteroceptive and proprioceptive mapping for a quadruped robot in lunar-like environments. An onboard RGB-D camera builds robot-centered elevation maps, estimates geometric traversability, and derives navigation costs for autonomous planning. In parallel, proprioceptive measurements provide interaction-aware terrain cues that complement geometry-based assessment. Local maps are incrementally registered into a global multi-layer representation, which is used by an exploration module to select targets in unexplored regions of interest. The targets are reached by an autonomous navigation system that guides collision-aware motion using the available map and cost layers. Simulation results on NVIDIA Isaac Sim show autonomous exploration, map expansion, and spatial association between terrain geometry and robot-terrain interaction. Subsequent navigation using this information exhibits lower average Cost of Transport (CoT) than initial exploration.

</details>


### [181] [Inspection-SPARS: Task-Oriented Sparse Roadmaps for Inspection Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35516)
*Adir Morgan,Oren Salzman,Kiril Solovey*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Inspection-SPARS，首个面向巡检任务的路线图稀疏化方法，将 SPARS 框架从纯几何准则推广到任务导向准则，在覆盖与路径质量保证下将顶点和边压缩 4-8 倍，使 GIP 求解器得到最多短 25% 的巡检路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于采样的巡检规划将连续问题转化为离散路线图上的图巡检规划（GIP）问题。稠密路线图能捕捉更多巡检视角与运动捷径、解质量更高，但组合搜索空间过大，现有 GIP 求解器难以在实用时间内找到好解。路线图稀疏化可缓解该负担，但现有方法要么与巡检任务无关，要么只保证 POI 覆盖而不考虑最终巡检路径的质量。

Method: 提出 Inspection-SPARS。将流行的任务无关稀疏化框架 SPARS 从纯几何准则推广为任务导向准则，引入"巡检感知"的顶点接纳机制，把 POI 覆盖与连通性、路径质量并列为第一类稀疏化准则，从而在相对稠密路线图保持覆盖与路径质量保证的前提下，将稠密路线图重构为紧凑表示。

Result: 在真实三维环境中的实验表明，Inspection-SPARS 在保持覆盖的同时将顶点数和边数减少 4-8 倍，使 GIP 求解器计算出的巡检路径比使用稠密路线图或最先进巡检路线图时最多短 25%。

Conclusion: Inspection-SPARS 表明，稀疏化可以在不牺牲解质量保证的情况下实现任务感知，为巡检规划中稠密路线图带来的组合爆炸问题提供了有效且带保证的解决途径。

Abstract: Inspection planning seeks a minimum-length collision-free robot tour that observes a given set of points of interest (POIs). Sampling-based methods reduce this continuous problem to a graph inspection planning (GIP) problem over a discrete roadmap, which is then solved using combinatorial solvers. Dense roadmaps capture diverse inspection viewpoints and motion shortcuts, and thus admit higher-quality solutions, but they induce large combinatorial search spaces on which state-of-the-art GIP solvers struggle to find good solutions within practical time budgets. Roadmap sparsification---restructuring a dense roadmap into a compact representation that preserves connectivity and path lengths---can alleviate this burden. However, existing sparsification approaches are either agnostic to the underlying inspection task, or strive to ensure coverage of the POIs without accounting for the quality of the resulting inspection plan. We present Inspection-SPARS, which is, to our knowledge, the first inspection-roadmap sparsifier with POI coverage and path-quality guarantees relative to the dense roadmap. To this end, we generalize the SPARS framework, a popular task-agnostic sparsifier, from purely geometric criteria to task-oriented ones, introducing an inspection-aware vertex admission mechanism that treats POI coverage as a first-class sparsification criterion alongside connectivity and path quality. Experiments in realistic 3D environments show that Inspection-SPARS reduces vertex and edge counts by 4-8x while preserving coverage, allowing the GIP solver to compute tours up to 25% shorter than with the dense roadmap or state-of-the-art inspection roadmap. More broadly, Inspection-SPARS shows that sparsification can be made task-aware without sacrificing guarantees on solution quality.

</details>


### [182] [EdgeVLN: Runtime-Aware Deployment Ready Quantized Vision Language Navigation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35570)
*Rithvik Jonna,Man Namgung,Aakash Gurram,Tinoosh Mohsenin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 EdgeVLN，一种面向边缘设备的运行时感知量化 VLN 模型，通过量化 StreamVLN 骨干、轻量因果 Transformer 停止头 LATTE 及 llama.cpp 驱动，在 Jetson Orin NX 上实现可部署的实时导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLN 模型性能虽好，但依赖计算资源丰富的平台，难以部署在内存和功耗受限的机器人边缘设备上。仅靠压缩无法判断模型是否同时满足边缘平台的内存、延迟与能耗预算，并保持导航行为。

Method: EdgeVLN 将量化 StreamVLN 与 LATTE（一种轻量因果 Transformer 停止头，通过预测 Stop Action 验证器排名改善实时停止）结合，并通过 llama.cpp VLN 驱动执行，在板载重建流式上下文并剪枝内存 token。方法系统刻画了 8 至 2 bit 权重量化与多种推理运行时，LATTE 复用骨干隐藏状态，无需额外视觉编码器或额外骨干前向。

Result: 在全部 1,839 个 R2R VLN-CE val-unseen 回合上评估 6 种骨干精度和 7 种停止头。LATTE 在部署的 4-bit 模型上取得最高 SR 58.02%，超过 BF16 基线，仅增加 0.013 s/步延迟。4-bit 格式 SR 近乎相同，但每步能耗因执行路径相差 36.8 倍。仅 IQ4 NL 配合该驱动适配开发板，占用 11.35 GB 常驻内存，速度比存储流式 BF16 快 20.8 倍、能耗低 13.3 倍。INT2 崩溃。

Conclusion: 运行时选择、内存 token 剪枝与量化对高效边缘部署至关重要；EdgeVLN 证明了在资源受限机器人平台上部署高性能 VLN 的可行性。

Abstract: Vision-language navigation (VLN) models perform well but target compute-rich platforms, limiting deployment on memory- and power-constrained robotic edge devices. Compression alone does not establish whether a VLN model fits the memory, latency, and energy budgets of an edge platform while preserving navigation behavior. We introduce EdgeVLN, a runtime-aware, deployment-ready quantized VLN model that closes this gap. EdgeVLN combines a quantized StreamVLN model with Latent Trajectory Termination Extractor (LATTE), a lightweight causal transformer that improves real-time stopping by predicting a Stop Action verifier rank. Both execute through our llama.cpp VLN driver, which reconstructs streaming context and prunes memory tokens on-board. We characterize a pretrained StreamVLN backbone across weight quantization from 8 to 2 bits and multiple inference runtimes to identify a feasible operating point. LATTE reuses backbone hidden states within the budget freed by quantization, requiring neither a second vision encoder nor an additional backbone forward pass. We evaluate six backbone precisions and seven candidate stop heads on BF16 and IQ4 NL across all 1,839 R2R VLN-CE val-unseen episodes. We measure success rate (SR) in simulation and latency, energy, and resident memory on an NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX 16 GB. LATTE achieves our highest SR, 58.02 percent on the deployed 4-bit model, exceeding the BF16 baseline with only 0.013 s additional latency per navigation step. Four-bit formats achieve nearly identical SR, but step energy varies 36.8 times by execution path. Only IQ4 NL under our VLN driver fits the board, using 11.35 GB resident memory while running 20.8 times faster and using 13.3 times less energy than storage-streamed BF16. INT2 collapses. Runtime selection, memory-token pruning, and quantization are essential for efficient edge deployment.

</details>


### [183] [F4R: Failure-Driven Recognition, Reconstruction, Refinement, and Redeployment for Continual Robot Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35575)
*Zhuoyuan Yu,Jiacheng Wang,Tianle Liu,Yihua Ren,Peng Yu,Chen Bai,Ziheng Zhang,Yufei Jia,Jindou Jia,Yuhang Zhang,Xinrui Zhang,Shang Yujing,Yuxiang Chen,Chuhao Zhou,Tiancai Wang,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出F4R框架，将真实世界失败转化为仿真重建与强化学习闭环，无需额外真实纠正演示即可提升VLA模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型受限于专家演示覆盖不足及物理交互理解欠缺，常通过采集新失败的真实演示来补救，但该过程成本高、低效、存在安全隐患且难以规模化。

Method: F4R是一个失败驱动的real-to-sim-to-real闭环学习框架：首先由智能体从rollout中自动识别并诊断失败；将每个失败重建为保留任务相关空间与物理条件的交互式、以物体为中心的桌面环境；随后通过失败条件化的仿真-真实协同训练（sim-real co-training）及在重建环境中的定向强化学习精化策略；改进后的策略重新部署，新观察到的失败持续反馈进入下一轮重建与学习循环。

Result: 在四项真实世界操作任务上，F4R达到93.75%的分布内（In-Distribution）成功率和90.0%的分布外（OOD）成功率，在OOD条件下相较预算匹配的Targeted BC基线高出18.75个百分点，且未采集额外真实纠正演示。

Conclusion: F4R通过将真实失败转化为仿真重建与定向学习，实现了无需额外真实纠正数据的高效策略改进，验证了失败驱动real-to-sim-to-real闭环在提升VLA模型泛化与鲁棒性方面的有效性。

Abstract: The real-world performance of current vision-language-action models is fundamentally constrained by the limited coverage of expert demonstrations and their insufficient understanding of physical interactions. A common remedy is to collect additional real-world demonstrations of newly encountered failures. However, this process is costly, inefficient, potentially unsafe, and difficult to scale. To address this challenge, we propose Failure for Rising (F4R), a failure-driven real-to-sim-to-real closed-loop learning framework that converts real-world failures into targeted policy improvement. F4R first uses an agent to automatically identify and diagnose failures from rollouts. It reconstructs each failure as an interactive, object-centric table-top environment that preserves the task-relevant spatial and physical conditions. The policy is then refined through failure-conditioned sim-real co-training followed by targeted reinforcement learning in the reconstructed environments. The improved policy is subsequently redeployed, while newly observed failures are continuously fed back into the next reconstruction and learning cycle. Real-world evaluations on four manipulation tasks show that F4R achieves 93.75% In-Distribution and 90.0% Out-of-Distribution (OOD) success, outperforming the budget-matched Targeted BC baseline by 18.75 percentage points under OOD conditions without collecting additional real-world corrective demonstrations.

</details>


### [184] [CollisionSplatting: Collision-Aware Motion Planning in 3DGS Scenes with Image-Conditioned Objectives and Adjustable Conservatism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35619)
*R. Khorrambakht,Joaquim Ortiz-Haro,Stephan Weiss,Ludovic Righetti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CollisionSplatting，一种直接作用于3D高斯泼溅场景、GPU加速、概率启发式且可调保守度的距离度量，可统一几何碰撞代价与图像空间视觉目标，实现联合几何-视觉的运动规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将稠密视觉信息融入运动规划存在困难：几何规划器依赖抽象场景表示而丢弃视觉丰富度；学习式视觉模型通常缺乏几何可解释性与计算效率。因此需要一种兼具视觉丰富度、几何可解释性与实时效率的规划方案。

Method: 提出CollisionSplatting：简单、模块化、GPU加速、概率启发式的距离度量，直接作用于标准3DGS场景，保守度可调。将其与学习到的图像条件奖励函数结合，统一碰撞感知代价与图像空间目标，并集成到GPU加速的MPPI与RRT规划器中。

Result: 在碰撞分类任务上与代表性基线持平或更优，同时实现显著更高的碰撞检测吞吐量，并大幅降低VRAM占用；在真实世界的视觉引导导航与操作任务中验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 3DGS可作为连接丰富感知与实时运动规划的实用桥梁，CollisionSplatting有效实现了联合几何与视觉的运动规划。

Abstract: Incorporating dense visual information into motion planning remains challenging, as geometric planners rely on abstracted scene representations that discard visual richness, while learned visual models often lack geometric interpretability and computational efficiency. This paper introduces CollisionSplatting, a simple, modular, GPU-accelerated, probability-inspired distance metric with tunable conservatism that operates directly on standard 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) scenes. When combined with learned image-conditioned reward functions, this metric enables joint geometric and visual planning by unifying collision-aware costs with image-space objectives. We integrate the metric into GPU-accelerated Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) and Rapidly-Exploring Random Tree (RRT) planners, and show on-par or better collision-classification performance compared to representative baselines while achieving substantially higher collision-checking throughput and significantly lower VRAM usage. Finally, we demonstrate the effectiveness of our metric in real-world vision-guided navigation and manipulation tasks, highlighting 3DGS as a practical bridge between rich perception and real-time motion planning.

</details>


### [185] [Denoising Multi-Robot Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35651)
*Yuhao Zhang,Keisuke Okumura,Ajay Shankar,Amanda Prorok*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文基于动力学感知的扩散去噪框架D4orm，构建了适用于多种作业需求的多机器人轨迹规划器族（解耦、在线滚动时域、分布式），在仿真与真实平台上验证了其高效性与可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人轨迹规划具有非凸、多模态、高维特性，传统数值优化方法计算困难；现有采样类优化方法（如MPPI）与学习型扩散模型方法在求解质量、速度与可靠性上仍有不足，且难以适配不同资源与部署场景。

Method: 以D4orm为核心构件：该框架采用基于采样的优化，通过GPU等现代并行架构进行大规模并行采样，并以扩散-去噪结构对候选控制轨迹的"形变"进行迭代优化，从而生成满足动力学可行性与无冲突的轨迹。在此基础上设计三类规划器：提升可扩展性的解耦式规划器、带反馈控制的在线滚动时域规划器，以及面向资源受限场景的分布式规划器。

Result: 在2D与3D环境中的差速驱动与全向机器人评测表明，基于D4orm的方法相比MPPI等采样类优化方法及一种基于学习扩散模型的方法，能更快、更可靠地获得高质量解。进一步实现：十架真实四旋翼在有障碍环境中的零样本部署、100个仿真机器人的大规模解冲突，以及六台地面机器人的完全机载分布式"终身"运行。

Conclusion: 结果确立了扩散去噪作为多机器人协调中可扩展且可靠的规划框架，兼具通用性、实时性与实际部署能力。

Abstract: Multi-robot trajectory planning is a fundamental problem in multi-robot coordination but remains computationally challenging due to its nonconvex, multimodal, and high-dimensional nature. This work builds upon D4orm, a dynamics-aware diffusion-denoising framework, and develops a family of planning architectures for diverse operational requirements. Unlike conventional numerical optimization methods, D4orm employs sampling-based optimization to generate solution trajectories through massively parallel sampling, leveraging modern computing architectures such as GPUs. Its diffusion-denoising structure iteratively optimizes \textit{deformations} to candidate control trajectories, providing an efficient and versatile paradigm for generating kinodynamically feasible and conflict-free trajectories. Using D4orm as the building block for advanced planners, we present a decoupled planner for improved scalability, an online receding-horizon planner with feedback control, and a distributed planner for resource-constrained settings. Evaluations with differential-drive and holonomic robots in 2D and 3D environments demonstrate that D4orm-based approaches find high-quality solutions faster and more reliably than other sampling-based optimization methods, such as MPPI, as well as a learned diffusion-model-based method. We further demonstrate zero-shot deployment on ten real quadrotors with obstacles, large-scale deconfliction with 100 simulated robots, and fully onboard distributed `lifelong' operation with six ground robots. Overall, these results establish diffusion denoising as a scalable and reliable framework for multi-robot coordination. Code and video: https://github.com/proroklab/d4orm

</details>


### [186] [MM-ABC: Towards Generalist Mobile Manipulation via Seeing, Coordinating and Imagining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35652)
*Qiwei Liang,Guangyu Chen,Shaolong Zhu,Zikuan Xiao,Jinxuan Lu,Yifan Xie,Renjing Xu,Wenbo Ding,Tianxing Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出移动操作基础模型MM-ABC，以"感知—协同—想象"为核心，通过稀疏多层级VLM特征、训练期未来分支监督与掩码联合注意力实现臂-基座协同，在多项基准与真实任务上取得领先成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动操作使可达工作空间本身可控，但带来两大挑战：连续自运动下的空间感知，以及异构机械臂与移动基座的协调控制。既有方法或用显式3D表示/预测式世界模型强化几何感知，或将移动与操作解耦为独立动作流。作者认为有效移动操作不仅需要解耦，还需要支持跨流高效协作的表示。

Method: MM-ABC围绕Seeing、Coordinating、Imagining Arm-Base Collaboration构建：(1) 融合稀疏多层级VLM特征做空间感知；(2) 引入仅训练期使用的未来分支，以世界想象与几何意图作为额外监督，强化感知与操作意图预测并改善整体学习信号；(3) MM-APT通过掩码联合注意力与clean-action x-prediction协调独立的操作流与移动流。预训练使用5000+小时异构机器人数据，覆盖40万+回合、12个数据集、17种本体。

Result: 消融显示：将clean-action预测换成velocity prediction，RoboCasa365 composite-seen成功率由32.8%降至29.2%；移除未来监督或多层级条件化导致更大下降。MM-ABC在EBench达44.71%，RoboCasa365达61.2%，LIBERO达99.1%，无扰动训练的LIBERO-Plus达82.8%，五项真实任务平均成功率83%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，跨移动与操作流的协同表示对移动操作至关重要；训练期未来想象监督与多层级VLM条件化贡献显著，clean-action x-prediction优于速度预测。MM-ABC作为统一基础模型，在仿真基准与真实移动操作任务上均展现强泛化与高成功率。

Abstract: Mobile manipulation extends robot interaction beyond a fixed kinematic workspace by making the reachable region itself controllable. This flexibility introduces two central challenges: spatially grounded perception under continuous ego-motion and coordinated control of heterogeneous arm and base actions. Existing approaches strengthen geometry through explicit 3D representations or predictive world models, and often decouple mobility and manipulation into separate action streams. We argue that effective mobile manipulation requires not only decoupling, but also representations that support efficient cross-stream collaboration. We present MM-ABC, a foundation model built around Seeing, Coordinating, and Imagining Arm-Base Collaboration. MM-ABC combines sparse multi-level VLM features for spatial perception; a training-only future branch that uses world imagination and geometric intent as extra supervision, strengthening perception and manipulation-intent prediction and improving the overall learning signal; and MM-APT, which coordinates separate manipulation and mobility streams through masked joint attention and clean-action x-prediction. In controlled ablations, replacing clean-action prediction with velocity prediction lowers success on RoboCasa365 composite-seen tasks from 32.8% to 29.2%, and removing future supervision or multilevel conditioning causes larger drops. We pretrain MM-ABC on 5,000+ hours of heterogeneous robot data spanning 400K+ episodes, 12 datasets, and 17 embodiments. Experiments cover EBench, RoboCasa365, ManiSkill-HAB, LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, and real-world mobile manipulation. MM-ABC achieves 44.71% success on EBench, 61.2% on RoboCasa365, 99.1% on LIBERO, 82.8% on LIBERO-Plus without perturbation training, and 83% mean success on five real-world tasks.

</details>


### [187] [Agent Priors-guided Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35690)
*Puming Jiang,Tianrun Hu,Haozhe Du,Yibo Li,Zhiwei Xue,Xinhu Li,Harold Soh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 APPL，将训练时策略的结构先验作为组合与技能之间的接口，从而同时提升技能的分布外泛化与组合泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从少量示范中学习的机器人需要两种泛化：组合泛化（重组技能完成新任务）与技能泛化（同一技能策略适应新情形）。二者相互依赖，但信息在组合与所调用技能之间丢失：技能适用范围由其策略训练时的结构决定，而组合只能通过名称、指令或符号算子等描述访问技能，这些描述遗漏了该结构。

Method: 提出 Agent Priors-guided Policy Learning（APPL）。构建智能体将完整示范切分为可复用技能，为每个技能提出若干结构先验（如抓取仅依赖夹爪相对物体的位姿），并为每个先验训练与验证一个策略；运行时智能体依据这些先验接口选择相应策略并组合以完成新任务目标。

Result: 在 MetaWorld 与长时域 ManiSkill 任务上，APPL 提升了分布外技能泛化，并实现了此前未见的技能组合；消融接口信息后性能显著下降。

Conclusion: 训练时的结构性假设可作为技能学习与技能组合之间的桥梁。核心思想是用每个策略的结构先验作为组合与技能之间接口的一部分。

Abstract: Robots that learn from a few demonstrations often require two forms of generalization. Compositional generalization recombines skills to solve new tasks, and skill generalization lets the learned policy behind each skill work in new situations. The two depend on each other, yet information is lost between composition and the skills it calls. Where a skill works is determined by the structure its policy is trained with, while composition sees the skill only through a separate description, such as a name, an instruction, or a symbolic operator, that omits this structure. Our key idea is to use each policy's structural prior as part of the interface between composition and the skill. A structural prior states what a behavior depends on, for example that a grasp depends only on the gripper's pose relative to the object. Built into training, it shapes where the policy generalizes; stated in language, it tells composition where the policy applies. We instantiate this idea in Agent Priors-guided Policy Learning (APPL). A construction agent segments complete demonstrations into reusable skills, proposes several structural priors for each skill, and trains and verifies one policy per prior. A runtime agent then selects among these prior-specific policies and composes them toward new task goals using their interfaces. Across MetaWorld and long-horizon ManiSkill tasks, APPL improves out-of-distribution skill generalization and enables previously unseen skill compositions; ablating the interface information substantially reduces performance. These results support the use of training-time structural assumptions as a bridge between skill learning and skill composition.

</details>


### [188] [LQR-ArUco Fusion: Robust Hierarchical Control for Navigation and Asymmetric Manipulation in Two-Wheeled Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35700)
*Anupam Chatterjee,Arpita Kumari*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出分层控制框架，解决两轮倒立摆（TWIP）机器人非对称抓取时的动态失稳与导航漂移问题：离板视觉用ArUco做全局航点导航，板载低延迟环抑制扰动，实测可过减速带、动态跷跷板并稳定载物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两轮倒立摆机器人机动性强，但持续平衡调节使板载里程计不可靠，难以精确导航；侧装机械臂举升负载会产生未驱动的横滚力矩，在非平坦地形上问题被进一步放大。因此需要同时解决导航漂移与扰动抑制这对耦合问题。

Method: 提出分层双环控制架构以分工处理：离板视觉系统通过追踪顶部ArUco标记提供高延迟的全局航点导航，绕过里程计漂移；同时板载低延迟控制环利用惯性测量与编码器数据主动抑制物理扰动（负载引起的横滚力矩等）。

Result: 在物理实验中，该双环方法使自建机器人实现精确导航，能够抑制减速带带来的瞬态冲击、适应动态跷跷板斜坡，并在狭窄轮距上安全携带负载而不翻倒。

Conclusion: 分层双环架构可有效解耦高延迟全局定位与低延迟扰动抑制，使两轮倒立摆机器人具备非对称负载操作下的鲁棒导航与平衡能力，验证了分工式控制设计的有效性。

Abstract: We propose a hierarchical control framework to address severe dynamic instabilities and navigational drift that arise when a two-wheeled inverted pendulum (TWIP) robot attempts asymmetric object manipulation. While two-wheeled platforms are highly manoeuvrable, their constant balancing adjustments make onboard odometry highly unreliable for precise navigation. Furthermore, the addition of a side-mounted robotic arm introduces unactuated lateral roll moments when a payload is lifted, a challenge heavily compounded on uneven terrain. To solve these coupled problems, our architecture divides the workload. An offboard vision system tracks overhead ArUco markers to provide high-latency global waypoint navigation, bypassing odometry drift. Simultaneously, a low-latency onboard control loop rejects active physical disturbances using inertial and encoder data. In our physical experiments, this dual-loop approach enabled the custom-built robot to navigate accurately, reject transient impacts from speed bumps, adapt to a dynamic seesaw ramp, and carry a payload securely without falling over its narrow wheelbase.

</details>


### [189] [Humanoid Loco-Manipulation With Discrete VLA Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35709)
*Wenxin Shao,Siqi Chai,Kun Li,Kerou Zhang,Xinzhou Jiang,Wei Xu,Qiang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Holo-M是首个面向人形机器人全身移动操作(loco-manipulation)的离散VLA模型，通过将动作token扩展进语言模型词表，并提出统一动作分词器与分组离散扩散解码，在SIMPLE基准上取得通用与专用评测的最高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于离散动作token的VLA模型主要用于机械臂操作，而人形机器人全身动作空间（腿、躯干、臂、手）维度更高且异质，带来分词、训练与实时推理的新挑战；同时使用独立连续动作专家的模型存在"知识隔离"问题。

Method: 提出Holo-M：(1) 统一动作分词器，将人形动作空间分解为四个身体部位专用分词器——末端执行器、身体、手、运动学，支持跨异构本体与数据源（遥操作、第一人称人类视频、仿真）训练；(2) 将动作token加入语言模型词表，避免知识隔离；(3) 采用分组离散扩散解码替代动作token的自回归解码以满足实时控制需求。

Result: 在SIMPLE人形移动操作基准上进行了大量实验，Holo-M在通用(generalist)与专用(specialist)评测中均取得最高成功率，且显著领先第二名。

Conclusion: Holo-M验证了将动作token内在融入语言模型以构建全身人形VLA的可行性，兼顾跨本体训练与实时推理；作者将开源全部代码与模型权重。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models using discrete action tokens have proven effective for controling robotic arms on manipulation tasks. For a humanoid, however, the whole-body action space -- legs, torso, arms, and hands -- is far higher-dimensional and heterogeneous, raising tokenization, training, and real-time inference challenges that the previous VLA models do not address. We present Holo-M, to our knowledge the first discrete VLA model for humanoid loco-manipulation that intrinsically exploits the language model by extending its vocabulary with action tokens. In this model, we devise a unified action tokenizer that decomposes the humanoid action space into four body-part-specific tokenizers -- end-effector, body, hand, and kinematics -- enabling training across drastically different embodiments and data sources, including humanoid teleoperation, ego-centric human video, and simulation. By extending the language model's vocabulary with these action tokens, we avoid the knowledge-insulation problem inherent to the models that use separate continuous action experts. To meet real-time control requirements, we decode each body part's action tokens through grouped discrete diffusion decoding, rather than using autoregression on the action tokens. We have conducted extensive experiments on the SIMPLE humanoid loco-manipulation benchmark, in which Holo-M achieves the highest success rates in both the generalist and specialist evaluations, leading the second best by significant margins. We will release all the code and model weights.

</details>


### [190] [RoboCompiler: Graph-Native Compilation of Closed-Chain Robots for Consistent Modeling, Control, and Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35717)
*Mehdi Heydari Shahna,Joongheon Kim,Jouni Mattila*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了RoboCompiler——一个以图为核心的编译框架，将规范机构图编译为共享机械接口，统一了运动学回路、驱动与接触机器人的构型、运动、力与动力学建模，并在多个机器人平台上验证了机械一致性与性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具有运动学回路、耦合驱动器和变化接触的机器人需要一致的构型、运动、力与动力学模型。然而这些接口在控制与仿真中常被分别重建，导致闭链与驱动一致性难以维持。因此需要一种能统一构建这些接口的方法。

Method: RoboCompiler基于规范机构图（刚体、关节、坐标系、惯量、驱动器端口），构建闭链路径与解析残差雅可比；通过秩检验的连续延拓与修正组装可行构型；用切空间提升将独立速度映射为完整机器人与任务运动，配对驱动器端口映射保持虚功；用约束曲率修正将约简扩展到加速度与投影刚体动力学（含浮动基与支撑模式）；采用环局部求值、生成式雅可比与依赖感知复用实现闭链输入变化时的局部更新。

Result: 在Komatsu挖掘机、Unitree Go2、Franka Panda、Kangaroo与六UPS Stewart平台上评估物理回路与任务约束。高精度约束动力学与独立Pinocchio校验确认了机械一致性；MuJoCo与Isaac Sim/PhysX执行展示了原生接触下的任务性能与模型复用。对Kangaroo，编译将残差与雅可比求值时间减少96.7%，闭环仿真墙钟时间减少66.8%，且动力学与控制保持不变。

Conclusion: RoboCompiler通过图原生编译为闭链、耦合驱动与接触机器人提供了统一的机械接口，兼顾机械一致性与模型复用，并显著提升计算效率，适用于控制与仿真一致的场景。

Abstract: Robots with kinematic loops, coupled actuators, and changing contacts require consistent models of configuration, motion, force, and dynamics. Yet these interfaces are often reconstructed separately for control and simulation, making closure and actuation consistency difficult to maintain. This paper presents RoboCompiler, a graph-native framework that compiles a canonical mechanism graph into a shared mechanical interface. From bodies, joints, frames, inertias, and actuator ports, it constructs closure paths and analytic residual Jacobians, then assembles feasible configurations through rank-checked continuation and correction. A tangent lift maps independent velocities to full robot and task motion, while paired actuator-port maps preserve virtual work. A constraint-curvature correction extends the reduction to accelerations and projected rigid-body dynamics, including floating-base and support modes. Cycle-local evaluation, generated Jacobians, and dependency-aware reuse enable localized updates when closure inputs change. We evaluate physical loops and task-induced constraints on a Komatsu excavator, Unitree Go2, Franka Panda, Kangaroo, and a six-UPS Stewart platform. High-precision constrained-dynamics and independent Pinocchio checks confirm mechanical consistency; MuJoCo and Isaac Sim/PhysX executions demonstrate task performance and model reuse under native contact. For Kangaroo, compilation reduces residual-and-Jacobian evaluation time by 96.7% and closed-loop rollout wall time by 66.8%, with dynamics and control held fixed.

</details>


### [191] [DexRoam: Learning Mobile Bimanual Dexterous Manipulation from Egocentric Whole-Body Human Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35761)
*Rui Zhou,Yibo Yuan,Junkai Zhao,Fangyuan Zhao,Xiaoguang Zhao,Shanghang Zhang,Sirui Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexRoam是一个从人类全身演示中学习移动双臂灵巧操作的系统，通过免追踪器采集与三阶段对齐，将人类全身运动完整迁移至机器人，显著提升VLA策略成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动双臂灵巧操作需在单一轨迹内持续协调移动、全身运动与手指级灵巧性，造成严重的机器人演示瓶颈。第一视角人类演示是可扩展的替代方案，但已有方法为便于迁移而简化人类运动，丢弃了此类任务所依赖的细粒度耦合结构。

Method: 提出DexRoam系统，在人类到机器人的迁移全过程中保持全身运动的连续性与耦合性。构建免追踪器采集系统，仅用消费级VR头显与头戴双目相机，无需外部相机或动捕设备，实现可扩展的全身人类操作演示采集。随后进行具身、动作语义与时间三个显式对齐阶段，将采集动作映射至机器人动作空间，保留细粒度全身运动，使人类与机器人演示可被标准VLA策略联合学习。

Result: 在真实世界实验中，不同VLA骨干网络下人类演示均持续提升策略学习效果：GR00T N1.7平均成功率由29%提升至56%，pi0.5由32%提升至57%；仅用一半机器人演示即可达到纯机器人训练的水平。消融实验确认每个对齐阶段均为必要。

Conclusion: 结果凸显了人类演示在保留细粒度运动结构的前提下，对可扩展全身移动操作的潜力。

Abstract: Mobile bimanual dexterous manipulation requires continuous coordination of locomotion, whole-body motion, and finger-level dexterity within a single trajectory, creating a severe robot demonstration bottleneck. Egocentric human demonstrations offer a scalable alternative, but prior approaches ease the transfer by simplifying human motion, discarding exactly the fine-grained, coupled structure such tasks depend on. We present DexRoam, a complete system for learning mobile bimanual dexterous manipulation from human demonstrations, in which whole-body motion remains continuous and coupled throughout the human-to-robot transfer process. To enable scalable collection of whole-body human manipulation demonstrations, we develop a tracker-free capture system using only a consumer VR headset and a head-mounted stereo camera, without external cameras or motion trackers. We then perform three explicit alignment stages---embodiment, action-semantic, and temporal---to map captured motion into the robot action space, preserving fine-grained whole-body motion and allowing human and robot demonstrations to be jointly learned by standard VLA policies. Real-world experiments with different VLA backbones show that human demonstrations consistently improve policy learning across training paradigms, raising average success from 29% to 56% on GR00T N1.7 and from 32% to 57% on pi0.5, while matching robot-only training with half the robot demonstrations. Ablations confirm that each alignment stage is necessary. These results highlight the potential of human demonstrations for scalable whole-body mobile manipulation with preserved fine-grained motion structure.

</details>
